# 1. Bulbasaur
## Look
A small, quadrupedal Pokémon with blue-green skin and darker patches. It has red eyes with white pupils. Most notably, a large, green plant bulb is growing on its back from birth. This bulb has a symbiotic relationship with the Pokémon. It has a short, blunt snout and a wide mouth, with two small, pointed teeth visible in the upper jaw when its mouth is open. Its legs are stout, ending in three sharp claws.
## Lore
Bulbasaur is known as the Seed Pokémon and is a dual-type Grass/Poison Pokémon. The seed on its back is planted at birth and grows larger as the Pokémon ages. It can survive for days solely on the sunlight stored in the bulb. This makes it a very resilient Pokémon, often found napping in bright sunlight. The bulb contains nutrients, and by absorbing sunlight, it allows Bulbasaur to go for days without eating. It is a well-behaved Pokémon, known for its loyalty and calm nature, making it an excellent choice for new Trainers.

# 2. Ivysaur
## Look
A larger, more developed version of Bulbasaur. Its skin is a similar blue-green color. The bulb on its back has sprouted into a larger, pink bud with green leaves, which is now too heavy for it to stand on its hind legs for long. The bud gives off a sweet-smelling aroma when it is ready to bloom, which is a sign of its impending evolution. Its ears are more defined, and its fangs are slightly larger.
## Lore
Ivysaur, the Seed Pokémon, is the evolved form of Bulbasaur. The weight of the bud on its back strengthens its legs and posture. To support the bud's weight, Ivysaur must spend more time absorbing sunlight, which also nourishes the flower. The pleasant aroma it emits can become stronger when it's absorbing nutrients, attracting other Pokémon. It is considered a very caring Pokémon, often seen looking after younger Bulbasaur.

# 3. Venusaur
## Look
The final evolution of the Bulbasaur line, Venusaur is a large, bulky, toad-like Pokémon. The bud on its back has fully bloomed into a massive, vibrant pink flower with large green fronds. The flower is supported by a thick, trunk-like stalk. Its skin is a slightly lighter shade of blue-green and has a warty, rough texture. It has small, pointed ears and reddish eyes. Its sheer size and the giant flower make it an imposing figure.
## Lore
Venusaur, the Seed Pokémon, is known for the large flower on its back. The flower captures the sun's rays and converts them into energy, which can be used to power its attacks. The flower's color becomes more vivid after a rainy day. The aroma given off by the flower can calm the emotions of people and Pokémon. In battle, Venusaur uses this flower to lure opponents and then attack with powerful solar beams. It often leads other Pokémon of its evolutionary line in migrations to find sunlight.

# 4. Charmander
## Look
A small, bipedal, reptilian Pokémon with a primarily orange body and a cream-colored underside. It has blue eyes and two small fangs visible in its upper jaw. Its most distinctive feature is the flame that is constantly burning on the tip of its tail. The flame is a measure of Charmander's life force.
## Lore
Charmander is a Fire-type Pokémon known as the Lizard Pokémon. The flame on its tail indicates its health and emotions; it burns brightly when the Pokémon is strong, wavers when it is weak, and flares up when it is happy or excited. It is said that if this flame were to ever go out, Charmander would die. They prefer warm places and can be heard roaring when they are enjoying themselves. They are known to be loyal and obedient partners.

# 5. Charmeleon
## Look
The evolved form of Charmander, Charmeleon is a darker, fiercer-looking reptilian Pokémon. Its skin is a shade of red, and it has a more pronounced horn-like protrusion on the back of its head. Its claws are larger and sharper. The flame on its tail burns more intensely than Charmander's, reflecting its increased power.
## Lore
Charmeleon, the Flame Pokémon, has a notoriously hot-headed and vicious nature. It is a powerful Pokémon that seeks out strong opponents to battle. In its excitement for a fight, it can breathe out bluish-white flames. It is known to be ruthless in battle, using its sharp claws to slash at its foes. If it encounters a strong foe, it becomes aggressive, and the flame at the tip of its tail flares with a bluish-white color.

# 6. Charizard
## Look
The final evolution, Charizard is a large, draconic, bipedal Pokémon. It has a lean build with a long neck and two large, leathery wings with a teal-blue underside. Its body is orange with a cream-colored belly. It has two horn-like structures on its head and a powerful tail with a large flame at the tip. Its arms are short, but its claws are sharp.
## Lore
Charizard, the Flame Pokémon, is a dual-type Fire/Flying Pokémon. It flies through the sky in search of powerful opponents. Its fiery breath is incredibly hot, capable of melting boulders and glaciers. Despite its power and draconic appearance, it is not a Dragon-type. It will never turn its flame on an opponent weaker than itself. The flame on its tail burns hotter when it has experienced harsh battles. When enraged, the flame at the tip of its tail is said to flare up in a bluish-white color.

# 7. Squirtle
## Look
A small, bipedal turtle-like Pokémon with a light blue body, a cream-colored underside, and a long, curled tail. Its most prominent feature is its hard, brown shell, which has a white ridge. It has large, reddish-brown eyes and a slightly hooked upper jaw. Its shell provides excellent protection.
## Lore
Squirtle is known as the Tiny Turtle Pokémon. Upon birth, its back swells and hardens into a protective shell. It is a capable swimmer and can spray powerful streams of water from its mouth for both hunting and defense. When threatened, it retracts its limbs and neck into its shell, making it nearly impervious to attack. The surface of its shell is grooved to reduce water resistance, allowing it to swim at high speeds.

# 8. Wartortle
## Look
Wartortle is a larger version of Squirtle, with a deeper indigo skin tone. It has developed prominent, feathery ears and a larger, fluffier tail, both of which are covered in thick fur. These ears and tail are believed to aid in balance while swimming and are also a symbol of longevity. Its eyes are sharper and more focused.
## Lore
Known as the Turtle Pokémon, Wartortle is a symbol of longevity and is said to live for 10,000 years. It is a more aggressive swimmer than Squirtle, actively hunting prey in the water. The scratches on its shell are a sign of its toughness and experience in battle. It can store air in its fur-covered tail, allowing it to stay underwater for extended periods during deep dives.

# 9. Blastoise
## Look
A large, bipedal, tortoise-like Pokémon. Blastoise is characterized by the two powerful water cannons that protrude from the top of its sturdy, dark blue shell. Its body is a lighter blue with a cream-colored belly. It has a stocky build with thick limbs and a stern facial expression with triangular ears.
## Lore
Blastoise, the Shellfish Pokémon, is a living battleship. The water cannons on its shell can shoot jets of water with enough force to punch through steel. The accuracy of these cannons is remarkable, capable of striking small targets from over 160 feet away. To withstand the powerful recoil of its cannons, Blastoise plants itself firmly on the ground with its heavy body. It is a natural leader, often found leading groups of Squirtle and Wartortle.

# 10. Caterpie
## Look
A small, green, caterpillar-like Pokémon. Its most notable features are the large, black, eye-like markings on its head, which are used to scare off predators. Its true eyes are small and black. It has a bright red, forked osmeterium (antenna) on its head that retracts when not in use. Its body is segmented, and it moves on several small, suction-cup-like feet.
## Lore
Caterpie, the Worm Pokémon, has an insatiable appetite. It can devour leaves larger than itself in a very short amount of time, a necessity for its rapid growth and evolution. When threatened, it releases a foul odor from its red antenna to repel enemies. Its suction-cup feet allow it to climb any surface with ease, helping it find the best leaves to eat.

# 11. Metapod
## Look
Metapod is a crescent-shaped, chrysalis Pokémon. Its body is entirely encased in a hard, green shell. The only features visible are its eyes, which peer out from within the shell. It remains almost completely motionless, conserving energy for its impending evolution.
## Lore
Known as the Cocoon Pokémon, Metapod's stage is a test of patience and endurance. While its outer shell is as hard as steel, the body inside is soft and vulnerable as it undergoes a massive transformation. It cannot do much other than harden its shell to protect itself from predators. It is said that a strong impact can cause its soft innards to pop out, so it tries to avoid battle at all costs.

# 12. Butterfree
## Look
An insectoid Pokémon resembling a large butterfly. It has a dark purple body, large, compound red eyes, and two small blue hands and feet. Its most prominent feature is its large, white wings, which are covered in a fine, water-repellent powder. It has two black antennae.
## Lore
Butterfree, the Butterfly Pokémon, is highly adept at finding nectar and honey. It can locate flowers up to six miles away from its nest. The powder on its wings is highly toxic and is its primary defense mechanism; by flapping its wings at high speed, it can release this dust into the air to poison or paralyze foes. It is a gentle Pokémon that spends its days fluttering from flower to flower.

# 13. Weedle
## Look
A small, larval Pokémon with a brown, segmented body. It has a round, pink nose and small, black, beady eyes. On its head is a sharp, two-inch-long venomous stinger, and on its tail is another, smaller stinger. It has several small, pink, suction-cup-like feet.
## Lore
Weedle, the Hairy Bug Pokémon, has a very acute sense of smell. It uses its large, red nose to find the leaves it likes to eat. It is a voracious eater, consuming its own weight in leaves every day. The stinger on its head packs a potent poison and is its main defense. It hides in forests and grasslands, often under leaves to avoid being spotted by predators like Pidgey.

# 14. Kakuna
## Look
Kakuna is a yellow, cocoon-like Pokémon, transitioning from a larval to an adult stage. It is shaped like a pyramid with a domed top. It has black, slitted eyes that peer out from within the shell. While it appears motionless, it is very active on the inside.
## Lore
Known as the Cocoon Pokémon, Kakuna remains almost completely still, leaning against trees as it prepares to evolve. It can only harden its shell to protect itself. While it may seem docile, it can extend its poison stinger if it feels threatened. It generates a great deal of heat from the transformation occurring inside its shell, making its body hot to the touch.

# 15. Beedrill
## Look
A large, bipedal, wasp-like Pokémon. Its body is yellow and black with two pairs of translucent, veined wings. It has large, red, compound eyes and a tapered snout. Its most dangerous features are the three venomous stingers: two large, drill-like stingers on its forelegs and another on its abdomen.
## Lore
Beedrill, the Poison Bee Pokémon, is extremely territorial and aggressive. They often attack in swarms. They use their three poison stingers to jab their foes repeatedly. The two large stingers on their arms are used to strike, while the one on their tail delivers the final, most potent dose of poison. They are incredibly fast flyers and will chase down any intruder that disturbs their nest.

# 16. Pidgey
## Look
A small, plump, avian Pokémon. It has brown plumage, a cream-colored face and underside, and small, black markings around its eyes. It has a short, stubby beak and pinkish-grey feet. It is a common and gentle-looking bird.
## Lore
Pidgey, the Tiny Bird Pokémon, has an extremely sharp sense of direction. It can always find its way back to its nest, no matter how far away it may be. It is a docile Pokémon and prefers to avoid conflict. If threatened, it will flap its wings at ground level to kick up sand and dust to blind its foe, creating an opportunity to escape.

# 17. Pidgeotto
## Look
Pidgeotto is a larger and more sleek-looking bird than its pre-evolution. It has a long, flowing red-and-yellow crest on its head. Its plumage is brown and cream, but its tail feathers are a striking red. It has sharp talons and a focused, predatory gaze.
## Lore
Known as the Bird Pokémon, Pidgeotto is highly territorial. It claims a large area as its own and flies around it, patrolling for intruders. It has incredible eyesight, allowing it to spot prey, such as an Exeggcute, from high altitudes. It uses its well-developed talons to clutch its prey and carry it back to its nest.

# 18. Pidgeot
## Look
The final evolution, Pidgeot is a large and majestic bird of prey. Its plumage is sleek, and its head crest is even longer and more magnificent than Pidgeotto's, flowing down its back. It has powerful wings, sharp talons, and an intimidating gaze. Its musculature is well-developed, hinting at its immense speed.
## Lore
Pidgeot, the Bird Pokémon, is capable of flying at Mach 2 speed. Its powerful wings can whip up gusts of wind strong enough to bend large trees. It is a beautiful Pokémon that is difficult to train due to its pride. When hunting, it skims the surface of the water to snatch unsuspecting prey like Magikarp. Its glossy feathers are a sign of its health.

# 19. Rattata
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, rodent Pokémon. It has purple fur, a cream-colored belly, large red eyes, and prominent ears. Its most notable features are its large front incisors, which grow continuously. It has a long, thin tail that is often curled. An Alolan form exists with dark fur and a mustache-like marking.
## Lore
Rattata, the Mouse Pokémon, is known for its incredible caution and resilience. Its fangs grow constantly, so it must gnaw on hard objects to keep them filed down. It can live in almost any environment and will eat practically anything. It is a prolific breeder and can be found in large numbers in many regions. If you see one, it's highly likely there are forty more hiding nearby.

# 20. Raticate
## Look
A large, bipedal rodent Pokémon, evolved from Rattata. Its fur is brown, and its belly is cream-colored. It is much larger and more robust than Rattata, with even larger, more powerful incisors capable of chewing through solid concrete. It has three rows of whiskers that help it maintain balance. The Alolan form is dark-furred and much bulkier.
## Lore
Raticate, the Mouse Pokémon, is a tough and aggressive creature. Its powerful fangs can topple buildings. It uses its whiskers to maintain balance and will slow down if they are cut. Its hind feet are webbed, making it a capable swimmer when it needs to cross rivers to find food. It is known to be the leader of Rattata nests.

# 21. Spearow
## Look
A small, avian Pokémon with rough, brown plumage on its head and back, and a cream-colored underside. It has a short, hooked, pinkish beak, and its wings have a reddish tint. Its expression is typically angry and aggressive, with narrow, white eyes.
## Lore
Spearow, the Tiny Bird Pokémon, is known for its territorial and aggressive nature. Unlike the docile Pidgey, Spearow will attack any intruders in its territory without hesitation, often calling for backup. It has a very loud cry that can be heard from half a mile away. Its short wings mean it has to flap constantly to stay airborne, but it is a very fast and nimble flyer.

# 22. Fearow
## Look
A large, avian Pokémon with a long, elegant neck and massive wings. Its plumage is mostly brown, with a cream-colored belly. It has a long, sharp, drill-like beak and a vibrant red crest on its head. Its wingspan is impressive, allowing it to soar for long periods.
## Lore
Fearow, the Beak Pokémon, is the evolved form of Spearow. It has the stamina to fly all day without needing to land. Its long neck and sharp beak are perfect for plucking prey from the ground or water. It has been seen carrying prey as large as an Ekans back to its nest. It is a very old Pokémon, with drawings of it found in ancient murals.

# 23. Ekans
## Look
A serpentine Pokémon with a primarily purple body. It has a yellow ring pattern around its neck and yellow, slitted eyes. Its most distinctive feature is its tail, which has a rattle-like structure at the end that it shakes to produce a warning sound. It has no visible nose or ears.
## Lore
Ekans, the Snake Pokémon, is a stealthy predator. It can unhinge its jaw to swallow prey much larger than itself, such as the eggs of Pidgey and Spearow. The rattle on its tail grows longer with age. It uses this rattle to intimidate foes before striking with a venomous bite. It moves silently through the grass, hunting for its next meal.

# 24. Arbok
## Look
A large, cobra-like Pokémon. Its body is a dark purple, and it has a large, intimidating pattern on its hood, which it flares to scare enemies. This pattern resembles a face and can vary between individuals and regions. It has red and yellow eyes and a menacing expression.
## Lore
Arbok, the Cobra Pokémon, is a terrifying predator. The pattern on its hood is not just for show; it is used to paralyze foes with fear. It is incredibly strong, capable of crushing its victims by coiling its body around them. Once it has its prey in its grip, it is nearly impossible to escape. It is a very vengeful Pokémon and will hunt down anyone who harms it or its young.

# 25. Pikachu
## Look
A small, chubby, rodent-like Pokémon. It has yellow fur with two brown stripes on its back, and a lightning bolt-shaped tail. Its most recognizable features are its long, black-tipped ears and the two red, circular pouches on its cheeks, which store electricity.
## Lore
Pikachu, the Mouse Pokémon, is the most well-known of all Pokémon. It stores electricity in the pouches on its cheeks. When it feels threatened, it can discharge this electricity in powerful shocks. It raises its tail to monitor its surroundings and can sometimes be struck by lightning in this pose, which it uses to recharge its electrical energy. Groups of Pikachu can cause massive electrical storms.

# 26. Raichu
## Look
The evolved form of Pikachu, Raichu is a larger, bipedal rodent. It has orange fur, a white belly, and large, brown feet. Its ears are long and curled, with a yellow interior. Its long, thin tail ends in a massive, lightning-bolt-shaped tip. The Alolan form has a lighter color, blue eyes, and rides on its tail like a surfboard.
## Lore
Raichu, the Mouse Pokémon, can store even more electricity than Pikachu. Its electrical shocks can reach up to 100,000 volts, powerful enough to knock out a Dragonite. Its long tail acts as a ground, protecting it from its own powerful electrical discharges. It can become aggressive if it stores too much electricity.

# 27. Sandshrew
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon with a body covered in a tough, brick-patterned, sand-colored hide. It has a short snout, blue eyes, and sharp claws on its hands and feet. It can curl into a tight ball for protection. The Alolan form is ice-blue and its hide is made of steel-like ice.
## Lore
Sandshrew, the Mouse Pokémon, lives in arid, desert regions. Its body is adapted to absorb water, allowing it to survive with very little moisture. When threatened, it rolls up into a spiny ball that is resistant to almost any attack. It can also use this form to roll away from danger at high speed. It burrows underground to create its nest and to escape the desert heat.

# 28. Sandslash
## Look
A bipedal, pangolin-like Pokémon. Its back is covered with large, sharp, brown spikes. It has a long snout, small eyes, and massive, scythe-like claws on its forepaws. Its body is a lighter, cream color. The Alolan form is covered in sharp, ice-like quills of steel.
## Lore
Sandslash, the Mouse Pokémon, is known for the formidable spikes on its back. If it rolls up into a ball, these spikes provide both offense and defense. It can run at high speeds, and if it trips, it will curl up and continue rolling, slashing at anything in its path. Its massive claws are not just for digging; they are also used to slash at foes. It sheds its spikes periodically, and they grow back quickly.

# 29. Nidoran (Female)
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, rodent-like Pokémon. It has light blue fur with darker blue spots. It has large, black eyes, prominent ears, and visible whiskers. It possesses a small, venomous barb on its forehead, though it is smaller than the male's.
## Lore
Nidoran♀, the Poison Pin Pokémon, is a gentle creature despite the venom it carries. The barb on its head is small and used primarily for defense. While not aggressive, it will release a powerful toxin if it feels threatened. The female is more sensitive to smells than the male.

# 30. Nidorina
## Look
The evolved form of the female Nidoran, Nidorina is a larger, bipedal Pokémon. Its fur is a deeper shade of blue. Its horn is more developed, and its ears are larger. It has a more robust and sturdy build than its pre-evolution.
## Lore
Nidorina, the Poison Pin Pokémon, is a very caring and motherly creature. It is known to be gentle and dislikes fighting. When it is with its friends or family, it retracts its venomous barbs to avoid accidentally harming them. If separated from its group, it becomes nervous and wary.

# 31. Nidoqueen
## Look
A large, bipedal Pokémon with a tough, scaly, blue-gray hide. It is a powerful-looking creature with a stout build, a prominent horn on its forehead, and small, spiky protrusions on its back. It has a thick, powerful tail and strong limbs.
## Lore
Nidoqueen, the Drill Pokémon, is a dual-type Poison/Ground Pokémon. Its body is covered in hard scales that provide excellent protection. It is fiercely protective of its young and its nest, and will go on a rampage if they are threatened. It can use its massive body to block the entrance to its burrow and fend off any attackers. It is known to be at its strongest when defending its children.

# 32. Nidoran (Male)
## Look
A small, quadrupedal Pokémon with pinkish-purple fur. It has large, prominent ears with a teal interior, red eyes, and a large, venomous horn on its forehead. This horn is much larger than the female's and is its primary weapon.
## Lore
Nidoran♂, the Poison Pin Pokémon, is an alert and aggressive Pokémon. It constantly moves its large ears to scan for danger. The horn on its forehead secretes a powerful venom. It is known to be more aggressive than its female counterpart and will not hesitate to attack with a venomous jab if it feels threatened.

# 33. Nidorino
## Look
Evolved from the male Nidoran, Nidorino is a larger and more aggressive-looking Pokémon. Its purple hide is tougher, and its horn is now as hard as a diamond. It has a ridge of toxic spines running down its back. Its ears are large and pointed.
## Lore
Nidorino, the Poison Pin Pokémon, is a fierce and independent creature. It is easily angered and will stab at foes with its venom-laced horn, which can inject a powerful poison. It is known to be a violent Pokémon, and its temperament makes it difficult to train. It uses its horn to shatter rock and search for moon stones.

# 34. Nidoking
## Look
The final evolution, Nidoking is a massive, bipedal Pokémon. Its hide is a thick, purple, armor-like plate. It has a huge, powerful horn, large ears, and a ridge of spines down its back. Its most prominent feature is its thick, powerful tail, which it can swing with enough force to snap a telephone pole.
## Lore
Nidoking, the Drill Pokémon, is a dual-type Poison/Ground Pokémon of immense power. A single swing of its tail can topple a metal transmission tower. Once it goes on a rampage, there is no stopping it. It uses its horn to pierce through its foes and its tail to sweep them away. Its tough hide protects it from most attacks.

# 35. Clefairy
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon with a star-shaped silhouette. It has pink skin, a curled tuft of hair on its head, and a small pair of wings on its back. It has a plump body, short limbs, and a curled, bouncy tail. Its eyes are large and brown.
## Lore
Clefairy, the Fairy Pokémon, is a shy and reclusive creature. They are said to be most active during the full moon, when they come out to dance and play. The light of the moon is said to be stored in the wings on their back, allowing them to float. They are extremely rare and are believed to live in quiet, remote mountain areas.

# 36. Clefable
## Look
The evolved form of Clefairy, Clefable is a larger, pink, bipedal Pokémon. It retains the star-shaped silhouette and has larger wings. Its ears are long and pointed with brown tips. It has a gentle and friendly appearance.
## Lore
Clefable, the Fairy Pokémon, has extremely sensitive hearing. It can hear a pin drop from over half a mile away. Because of this, it lives in quiet, remote mountain areas, far from any noise or civilization. It uses its wings to walk on water, making it appear as if it is skipping across the surface of a lake. It is a very timid creature and is rarely seen by humans.

# 37. Vulpix
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, fox-like Pokémon. It has reddish-brown fur, brown eyes, and a large, fluffy tail. Initially, it is born with a single white tail, which splits into six beautiful, curled tails as it matures. It has a tuft of fur on its head. The Alolan form is white and has a more flowing, cloud-like tail and fur.
## Lore
Vulpix, the Fox Pokémon, is a Fire-type. Inside its body burns a flame that never goes out. During the day, when the temperature rises, it expels flames from its mouth to prevent its body from overheating. It is known to feign injury to escape from powerful predators. It is a very intelligent Pokémon.

# 38. Ninetales
## Look
A graceful, nine-tailed, fox-like Pokémon. Its fur is a luxurious, golden-white, and its nine tails are long and flowing, each tipped with orange. It has piercing red eyes. It moves with an elegant and almost supernatural grace. The Alolan form is a light blue and white, with flowing fur that resembles clouds.
## Lore
Ninetales, the Fox Pokémon, is a mystical and highly intelligent creature. It is said that each of its nine tails possesses a different mystical power. It can live for 1,000 years, and it is a very vengeful Pokémon. It is said that pulling one of its tails will result in a 1,000-year curse. It can understand human speech.

# 39. Jigglypuff
## Look
A small, round, pink Pokémon with a tuft of curled fur on its head. It has large, blue-green eyes, small, pointed, cat-like ears, and stubby arms and feet. Its body is soft and balloon-like.
## Lore
Jigglypuff, the Balloon Pokémon, is famous for its lullaby. When it sings, it uses a sound wavelength that matches the brain waves of someone who is sleeping, making it impossible for its audience to stay awake. It can inflate its body like a balloon and float. It will continue to sing until its foe falls asleep, and it can endanger itself by not stopping to breathe.

# 40. Wigglytuff
## Look
The evolved form of Jigglypuff, Wigglytuff is a larger, pink, balloon-like Pokémon with a teardrop-shaped body. It has long, rabbit-like ears with black interiors and a white tuft of fur on its head. Its fur is said to be incredibly soft and luxurious.
## Lore
Wigglytuff, the Balloon Pokémon, has a body that is soft, flexible, and rubbery. It can inflate itself to enormous sizes by inhaling. The larger it becomes, the more it can bounce. Its fine fur is a luxury item, and sleeping on it is said to be a heavenly experience. It is a very friendly Pokémon, but if it gets angry, it will inflate itself to intimidate its opponent.

# 41. Zubat
## Look
A small, blue, bat-like Pokémon. It has no visible eyes and a large mouth with four pointed fangs. It has two long, thin legs and a pair of purple, membranous wings. It navigates using echolocation.
## Lore
Zubat, the Bat Pokémon, lives in dark caves and avoids sunlight, as prolonged exposure can cause it to become unhealthy. It has no eyes, so it uses ultrasonic waves emitted from its mouth to check its surroundings and hunt for prey. These sound waves bounce off objects, creating a mental map of its environment. They often gather in large, noisy colonies.

# 42. Golbat
## Look
A larger, more menacing bat Pokémon. Its body is blue, and its wings are a deep purple. Its most prominent feature is its massive, gaping mouth, which is almost always open, revealing its four sharp fangs. It has small, pointed ears and a pair of legs with one toe each.
## Lore
Golbat, the Bat Pokémon, is a vampiric creature. It has a voracious appetite for blood and can drink over 10 ounces of it in one sitting. It uses its sharp fangs to pierce the hide of its prey and drain its blood. It can sometimes become so engorged with blood that it becomes too heavy to fly properly.

# 43. Oddish
## Look
A small, round Pokémon that resembles a root vegetable. Its body is a dark blue, and it has two small feet and beady red eyes. On top of its head sprouts a tuft of five large, green leaves.
## Lore
Oddish, the Weed Pokémon, is a nocturnal creature. During the day, it buries its body in the soil, leaving only its leaves exposed to absorb sunlight and nutrients. At night, it uses its root-like feet to walk around, sometimes traveling over 1,000 feet to sow its seeds. It is often mistaken for a simple weed.

# 44. Gloom
## Look
The evolved form of Oddish, Gloom is a bipedal Pokémon with a dark blue body. It appears to be constantly drooling a sticky, foul-smelling nectar from its mouth. On its head is a large, rafflesia-like flower with four red, spotted petals and a yellow pistil. Its eyes are half-closed, giving it a drowsy appearance.
## Lore
Gloom, the Weed Pokémon, is known for the terrible stench that emanates from the flower on its head. This odor is so foul that it can cause fainting at a distance. However, it is said that about one in a thousand people find this aroma to be pleasant. The drool from its mouth is actually a sweet nectar that it uses to lure prey, despite its foul smell.

# 45. Vileplume
## Look
A bipedal Pokémon with a blue body and red eyes. Its most defining feature is the massive, rafflesia-like flower on its head. This flower has five large, red, spotted petals and a dense, orange center. The flower is so large and heavy that Vileplume has difficulty holding its head up.
## Lore
Vileplume, the Flower Pokémon, has the largest petals in the world. The flower on its head is filled with a highly toxic and allergenic pollen. The more toxic the pollen, the more vibrant the color of the petals. It releases this pollen in dense clouds, which can trigger severe allergic reactions and even paralysis. It uses this to trap its prey.

# 46. Paras
## Look
A small, insectoid Pokémon with an orange body and six legs. It has large, white eyes with black pupils. Its most notable feature is the two small, parasitic mushrooms, known as tochukaso, growing on its back.
## Lore
Paras, the Mushroom Pokémon, has a parasitic relationship with the mushrooms on its back. The mushrooms absorb nutrients from the Paras host. In return, the mushrooms offer some defense. These mushrooms are known to have powerful life-extending properties and are sought after by collectors. The bug itself prefers to live in damp, dark places.

# 47. Parasect
## Look
A large, cicada-like insect Pokémon. The parasitic mushrooms on its back have grown into a single, massive mushroom that has completely taken over the host's body. The host bug's eyes are now blank and white, indicating that the mushroom is in full control.
## Lore
Parasect, the Mushroom Pokémon, is a "zombie" Pokémon, fully controlled by the large mushroom on its back. The mushroom makes all the decisions, compelling the host to seek out damp places where the mushroom can thrive. It drains nutrients from trees and releases poisonous spores. The spores are used in traditional medicine.

# 48. Venonat
## Look
A small, round Pokémon covered in a coat of fuzzy, purple fur. Its most prominent features are its large, compound eyes, which are red and act like radar. It has a small pair of fangs, two white antennae, and small hands and feet.
## Lore
Venonat, the Insect Pokémon, has eyes that function as a radar. They can see in the dark and are capable of shooting powerful beams. Its entire body is covered in a thin, stiff fur that provides protection. It is said that if its eyes are glowing, it is a sign that it is about to fire a beam. It lives in the shadows of tall trees.

# 49. Venomoth
## Look
A large, moth-like Pokémon. It has a light purple body and large, dusty wings. The wings are covered in scales that vary in color depending on the type of poison they carry. It has large, blue, compound eyes and two feathery antennae.
## Lore
Venomoth, the Poison Moth Pokémon, is a nocturnal creature, only active at night. It is attracted to light. The scales on its wings are its primary weapon. They are coded by color to indicate their effect; dark scales cause paralysis, while lighter scales cause poison. A single flap of its wings can release enough of this toxic dust to fell a large tree.

# 50. Diglett
## Look
A small, brown Pokémon that appears to be just a head and a nose emerging from the ground. It has a simple, cylindrical body, large, black eyes, and a prominent, pink, oval-shaped nose. The rest of its body is always hidden underground, the appearance of which is a subject of much speculation.
## Lore
Diglett, the Mole Pokémon, lives about a yard underground, where it feeds on plant roots. Its skin is very thin, and it dislikes sunlight, so it only comes to the surface when it is dark. It is a vital part of agriculture in some regions, as its tunneling aerates the soil, making it perfect for farming.

# 51. Dugtrio
## Look
Dugtrio appears to be three individual Diglett heads emerging from the same spot. The three heads are identical, each with beady black eyes and a pink nose. They seem to be connected underground, though how is unknown. They think and act as one. The Alolan form has golden, hair-like whiskers on each head.
## Lore
Dugtrio, the Mole Pokémon, are three Diglett that have bonded together. They are powerful burrowers, capable of digging at speeds of over 60 miles per hour, triggering earthquakes. They work as a team, with the three heads bobbing up and down to loosen the soil, allowing them to dig through even the hardest ground.

# 52. Meowth
## Look
A small, feline Pokémon with cream-colored fur. It has a distinctively large, oval-shaped coin, known as a Koban, embedded in its forehead. It has large eyes with slitted pupils, prominent whiskers, and black-tipped ears. It can retract its claws. The Galarian form is a shaggy, steel-type, and the Alolan form is a sleek, purple-gray.
## Lore
Meowth, the Scratch Cat Pokémon, is fascinated by shiny, round objects, particularly coins. It is a nocturnal Pokémon, spending its nights wandering city streets in search of dropped change. Its charm and cunning nature are well-known. It is one of the few Pokémon capable of learning the move Pay Day, which generates money in battle.

# 53. Persian
## Look
A large, sleek, feline Pokémon with a graceful and elegant appearance. It has cream-colored fur, a small face, and a red, jewel-like organ on its forehead. Its muscles are flexible and allow it to walk without making a sound. Its long whiskers are sensitive and help it detect air movements. The Alolan form is gray and has a distinctively rounder face.
## Lore
Persian, the Classy Cat Pokémon, is admired for its elegant fur, but it is notoriously difficult to raise as a pet due to its proud and fickle nature. It has a very savage streak and will scratch and bite without warning if it is not pleased. The jewel on its forehead is said to gleam when it is on the prowl.

# 54. Psyduck
## Look
A yellow, duck-like Pokémon that stands on two legs. It is perpetually plagued by a headache, which it clutches with its three-fingered hands. It has a vacant, confused look in its eyes, a wide, flat beak, and a few stray black hairs on top of its head.
## Lore
Psyduck, the Duck Pokémon, is constantly suffering from a headache. This headache is the source of its mysterious psychic powers. When its headache becomes severe, it can unleash powerful psychokinetic blasts, but it has no memory of doing so afterward. It is unable to control its power, which only activates when its brain cells are in a heightened state.

# 55. Golduck
## Look
A tall, bipedal, blue, duck-like Pokémon. It is a strong swimmer, with webbed hands and feet. It has a red gem embedded in its forehead, which is said to glow when it uses its full psychic power. It has a sleek, aerodynamic body and a long, tapering tail.
## Lore
Golduck, the Duck Pokémon, is the fastest swimmer of all Pokémon. Its sleek body and webbed limbs allow it to out-swim even the most accomplished human athletes. When it swims at full speed, the gem on its forehead glows. It is often seen swimming gracefully in lakes and rivers. Despite its name, it is a pure Water-type, not a Psychic-type.

# 56. Mankey
## Look
A small, spherical, primate-like Pokémon covered in shaggy, off-white fur. It has a pig-like snout, triangular ears, and brown hands and feet. It has a long, prehensile tail. Its eyes are typically narrowed in an expression of anger.
## Lore
Mankey, the Pig Monkey Pokémon, is known for its incredibly short temper. It can become enraged in an instant, for almost no reason. When it is angry, it begins to tremble and its breathing becomes heavy. It is impossible to tell what will set it off, making it a very unpredictable Pokémon. They live in groups in treetops.

# 57. Primeape
## Look
The evolved form of Mankey, Primeape is a perpetually enraged primate Pokémon. Its fur is shaggy, and it has developed metal shackles on its wrists and ankles. Its hands and feet are now distinct, and its eyes are always wide with fury. Veins are often seen bulging from its forehead.
## Lore
Primeape, the Pig Monkey Pokémon, is always in a state of extreme anger. Its rage increases its blood circulation, which in turn makes its muscles even stronger, but at the cost of its intelligence. It will chase its target relentlessly and will not give up until it has been caught. It becomes even more furious if it makes eye contact.

# 58. Growlithe
## Look
A quadrupedal, canine Pokémon with bright orange fur marked by black stripes. It has a shaggy, cream-colored mane on its chest and a similar tuft of fur on its head and tail. It has a friendly and loyal appearance with brown eyes and a black nose. The Hisuian form has shaggier fur and a rock-like horn.
## Lore
Growlithe, the Puppy Pokémon, is known for its brave and loyal nature. It is fiercely protective of its Trainer and its territory and will bark and bite to repel any intruders. It has a superb sense of smell and will never forget a scent. It will fearlessly stand up to opponents much larger and stronger than itself.

# 59. Arcanine
## Look
A large, majestic, canine Pokémon with orange fur and jagged black stripes. It has a long, flowing, cream-colored mane that covers its neck and back. It is known for its incredible speed and graceful movements, which make it look as if it is gliding when it runs. Its expression is noble and proud.
## Lore
Arcanine, the Legendary Pokémon, is renowned for its beauty and speed. It is said to be capable of running 6,200 miles in a single day and night. The flame that burns inside its body is the source of its power. It has long been admired for its majestic appearance and has been depicted in ancient legends.

# 60. Poliwag
## Look
A small, spherical, tadpole-like Pokémon. Its body is blue, and its most notable feature is the black-and-white swirl pattern on its round belly, which is actually its internal organs seen through its thin skin. It has large eyes, a wide mouth, and a long, thin tail. It has no arms, but has just recently grown two small legs.
## Lore
Poliwag, the Tadpole Pokémon, has very thin skin that allows its coiled intestines to be seen. The direction of the swirl changes after it evolves. It is a better swimmer than it is a walker, and it prefers to stay in the water. The swirl on its belly is said to undulate gently.

# 61. Poliwhirl
## Look
The evolved form of Poliwag, Poliwhirl is a larger, bipedal tadpole. It has a blue, spherical body and the same swirl pattern on its belly. It now has two muscular arms with glove-like hands. Its legs are more developed, but it still prefers to be in the water. Its skin is always moist and slick.
## Lore
Poliwhirl, the Tadpole Pokémon, is capable of living on both land and in water. When it is on land, its body sweats a slimy fluid to keep its skin from drying out. The swirl on its belly can induce drowsiness in its opponents if they stare at it for too long. Despite having legs, it is still more comfortable in the water.

# 62. Poliwrath
## Look
A large, bipedal, frog-like Pokémon with a muscular build. Its body is blue, and it retains the swirl on its belly. Its eyes have a much tougher and more aggressive look. It has powerful arms and legs, and its body is rippling with muscle.
## Lore
Poliwrath, the Tadpole Pokémon, is a dual-type Water/Fighting Pokémon. Its body is almost pure muscle, with very little fat. This allows it to swim tirelessly without getting fatigued. It is a master of many swimming styles, including the butterfly and crawl, and can easily outpace the best human swimmers. It is a formidable fighter both in and out of the water.

# 63. Abra
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon with a humanoid appearance. It has a yellow, segmented, armor-like hide. It has a fox-like face with closed eyes, as it is almost always sleeping. It has a large head, a small body, and a thick tail with a brown band.
## Lore
Abra, the Psi Pokémon, sleeps for 18 hours a day. It uses its psychic abilities even while it is asleep. It can sense impending danger, and it will teleport itself to safety before the danger even occurs, without ever waking up. If it does not teleport away in its sleep, it is a sign that it trusts its surroundings completely.

# 64. Kadabra
## Look
The evolved form of Abra, Kadabra is a taller, bipedal Pokémon. It holds a silver spoon in its right hand, which is used to amplify its psychic powers. It has a large, star-shaped symbol on its forehead and a long, bushy tail. Its eyes are typically open, and it has a more focused expression.
## Lore
Kadabra, the Psi Pokémon, emits special alpha waves from its body. These waves can cause headaches and make precision machinery malfunction if anyone gets too close. It is said that a boy with psychic powers awoke one morning to find he had transformed into a Kadabra. The spoon it holds amplifies its telekinetic abilities.

# 65. Alakazam
## Look
A humanoid Pokémon with a large mustache and a brown, segmented body. It holds a silver spoon in each hand. Its head is large, housing its incredibly powerful brain. It has a sleek, aerodynamic build despite its lack of wings.
## Lore
Alakazam, the Psi Pokémon, has a brain that never stops growing, causing its head to be disproportionately large. Its brain cells multiply continuously, and it is said to have an IQ of 5,000. It can outperform a supercomputer and remembers everything it has ever experienced. It uses its psychic powers to move its body, rather than its muscles.

# 66. Machop
## Look
A small, humanoid Pokémon with grayish-blue skin. It has a well-muscled body, with three crest-like ridges on its head. It has red eyes and a reptilian face. Despite its small size, it possesses immense superhuman strength.
## Lore
Machop, the Superpower Pokémon, loves to train and build its muscles. It is small but has enough power to hurl one hundred adult humans. It trains in every style of martial art to become stronger. It is a very dedicated Pokémon that never gives up, no matter how tough the training.

# 67. Machoke
## Look
A larger, more muscular humanoid Pokémon. Its skin is a grayish-blue, and it wears a black belt that is said to hold back its incredible power. Its body is incredibly defined, with bulging muscles. It has the same head crests as Machop.
## Lore
Machoke, the Superpower Pokémon, is so powerful that it must wear a power-save belt to control its strength. Without it, its power would be uncontrollable. It is known to help humans with heavy labor. Its muscles are as hard as steel, and it is capable of lifting a sumo wrestler with just one finger.

# 68. Machamp
## Look
A massive, humanoid Pokémon with four powerful arms. Its skin is a grayish-blue, and its lower body resembles briefs. It has a formidable, muscular build and an aggressive facial expression. It has two arms on each side of its body, allowing it to attack from multiple angles at once.
## Lore
Machamp, the Superpower Pokémon, is a master of all martial arts. Its four arms can throw a flurry of 1,000 punches in just two seconds. It can launch its opponent over the horizon with a single punch. It is a powerful but honorable Pokémon that is often found training in remote mountains.

# 69. Bellsprout
## Look
A plant-like Pokémon with a thin, flexible, brown stem for a body. It has two root-like feet and two large, green leaves for arms. Its head is a yellow, bell-shaped flower bud with two small, beady eyes.
## Lore
Bellsprout, the Flower Pokémon, is a carnivorous plant. It may be thin and flexible, but it is incredibly fast. It uses its root-like feet to absorb moisture and nutrients from the soil. It captures and eats small insects by striking with incredible speed. It prefers hot and humid places.

# 70. Weepinbell
## Look
The evolved form of Bellsprout, Weepinbell has a larger, yellow, bell-shaped body. It has a large, hook-shaped appendage on its rear. It has two large leaves and a wide mouth. It is often seen hanging from tree branches by its hook.
## Lore
Weepinbell, the Flycatcher Pokémon, is a dangerous predator. At night, it hangs from a tree branch by its hook and sleeps. If it is disturbed while sleeping, it will swallow anything that moves. It spits a powder that can poison its foes and then uses an acid to dissolve them.

# 71. Victreebel
## Look
A large, carnivorous plant Pokémon. Its head is a massive, bell-shaped pitcher with two sharp fangs. It has a large, green leaf on top of its head that acts as a lid. A long, vine-like tendril hangs from its body. It has two large leaves for arms.
## Lore
Victreebel, the Flycatcher Pokémon, is said to live in large colonies deep in jungles. It lures prey with a sweet, honey-like aroma. Once the prey is inside its mouth, it closes the lid and secretes an acid that can dissolve anything, even bone. The vine on its body is used to help it balance its heavy head.

# 72. Tentacool
## Look
A small, jellyfish-like Pokémon. It has a light blue, translucent body with two large, red, crystal-like spheres on its head. It has two long, stinging tentacles that it uses for hunting and defense.
## Lore
Tentacool, the Jellyfish Pokémon, is composed of 99% water. It drifts aimlessly in shallow seas. The two red orbs on its head are often mistaken for eyes, but they are actually sensory organs that can refract sunlight into powerful beams. Its tentacles are filled with a potent neurotoxin.

# 73. Tentacruel
## Look
A large, jellyfish-like Pokémon with a menacing appearance. It has a large, blue body with a red, gem-like organ on its head. It possesses 80 long, extendable tentacles, each ending in a venomous stinger. The two large red orbs on its head can glow brightly.
## Lore
Tentacruel, the Jellyfish Pokémon, is a powerful predator of the sea. Its 80 tentacles can be extended to create a massive, poisonous net to ensnare prey. The tentacles can absorb water and grow to incredible lengths. The red orbs on its head glow brightly right before it unleashes a powerful ultrasonic blast.

# 74. Geodude
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a floating, gray boulder with two muscular arms. It has a rugged, rocky exterior, a frowning mouth, and trapezoidal eyes. It is almost always seen levitating slightly off the ground.
## Lore
Geodude, the Rock Pokémon, is often found on mountain paths and fields. They are frequently mistaken for ordinary rocks, and people often step on them by accident. They are very proud of their hard, rocky bodies and will compete with each other by smashing together to see who is tougher. They use their arms to pull themselves up steep mountain trails.

# 75. Graveler
## Look
A larger, more complex rock Pokémon. It has a boulder-like body, but now possesses four arms and two stout legs. Its body is covered in a layer of smaller rocks, giving it a lumpy texture. It has a craggy, determined expression.
## Lore
Graveler, the Rock Pokémon, lives on steep mountain sides. It moves by rolling down cliffs, and it does not care about what it crushes in its path. It can gain incredible speed and is capable of plowing through any obstacle. It feeds on rocks, preferring those that are covered in moss.

# 76. Golem
## Look
A massive, spherical Pokémon made of solid rock. It has a reptilian head and limbs that can retract into its boulder-like shell. Its body is incredibly tough and craggy. It has a formidable and imposing presence.
## Lore
Golem, the Megaton Pokémon, lives in volcanic craters. Its body is so hard that it can withstand dynamite blasts without any damage. It sheds its skin once a year, and its old shell immediately explodes. It moves by curling up into a ball and rolling, and it is known to cause landslides as it careens down mountains.

# 77. Ponyta
## Look
A small, equine Pokémon with a cream-colored coat. Its most striking feature is its mane and tail, which are made of bright, flickering flames. It has large, brown eyes and a small horn on its forehead. Its hooves are incredibly hard. The Galarian form is a psychic-type with a pastel-colored, fluffy mane.
## Lore
Ponyta, the Fire Horse Pokémon, is born with a weak flame that grows stronger as it becomes more capable of running. Its fiery mane and tail only appear about an hour after its birth. Its hooves are ten times harder than diamond, and it can trample anything flat. It is a very fast runner and can be seen galloping across plains.

# 78. Rapidash
## Look
A majestic, unicorn-like equine Pokémon. Its coat is a creamy white, and its mane and tail are a spectacular, flowing blaze of fire. It has a single, long horn on its forehead. It is known for its incredible speed and graceful movements.
## Lore
Rapidash, the Fire Horse Pokémon, is an incredibly fast creature, capable of reaching speeds of up to 150 miles per hour. When it gallops at full speed, its fiery mane seems to blaze even more brightly, creating a stunning visual that resembles a comet. It is a very competitive Pokémon and will chase after anything that moves fast.

# 79. Slowpoke
## Look
A pink, quadrupedal Pokémon with a dopey, vacant expression. It has a long, tapering tail, a cream-colored muzzle, and small ears. It is almost always seen with a blank, bewildered look on its face. The Galarian form has yellow patches on its head and tail.
## Lore
Slowpoke, the Dopey Pokémon, is known for its incredible sluggishness, both in thought and action. It can take up to five seconds for it to register pain. It is a skilled fisher, using its tail as a lure to catch prey. The tail secretes a sweet, alluring sap. It is often seen lazing by the water's edge, completely oblivious to its surroundings.

# 80. Slowbro
## Look
A bipedal Pokémon that is the result of a Shellder biting onto a Slowpoke's tail. The Slowpoke retains its dopey expression, while the Shellder has transformed into a gray, spiral-shaped shell that is permanently attached. The Galarian form has the Shellder attached to its arm, turning it into a cannon.
## Lore
Slowbro, the Hermit Crab Pokémon, is a symbiotic creature. The Shellder on its tail provides balance, allowing Slowpoke to stand on two legs. In return, the Shellder gets to feed on the scraps of Slowbro's meals. The bite from the Shellder is said to have unlocked latent psychic powers within Slowpoke, though it is still just as dopey.

# 81. Magnemite
## Look
A robotic Pokémon composed of a gray, spherical metal body with a single, large eye. It has two horseshoe magnets on its sides that act as arms, and a single large screw on top of its head. It floats through the air using electromagnetism.
## Lore
Magnemite, the Magnet Pokémon, is an inorganic creature that is powered by electricity. It is often found near power plants and other sources of electricity. The units on its sides generate a powerful anti-gravity field, allowing it to float. It can be a nuisance, as it often attaches itself to power lines and drains electricity.

# 82. Magneton
## Look
Magneton is a Pokémon composed of three individual Magnemite linked together by a strong magnetic force. The three spheres are arranged in a triangular formation. Each Magnemite retains its individual eye and magnets.
## Lore
Magneton, the Magnet Pokémon, is the result of three Magnemite being drawn together. The combined magnetic force of the three is incredibly powerful and can be disruptive to electronic equipment. They often appear when sunspots flare up. The three Magnemite that make up a Magneton are linked telepathically.

# 83. Farfetch'd
## Look
A brown, duck-like Pokémon that always carries a leek or spring onion stalk. It has a yellow beak, a small crest on its head, and a V-shaped black marking on its forehead. It has a proud and noble expression. The Galarian form is larger and carries a massive leek.
## Lore
Farfetch'd, the Wild Duck Pokémon, is a rare creature that is always seen with a plant stalk. This stalk is its most prized possession and is used as a weapon, a nesting material, and, in dire situations, a food source. It will defend its stalk with its life. It is said that they cannot live without their stalk.

# 84. Doduo
## Look
A bipedal, avian Pokémon with two heads. It has a round, brown body covered in shaggy feathers, and two long, slender necks, each topped with a head that has a sharp beak and black eyes. It has two long, powerful legs. It has no wings.
## Lore
Doduo, the Twin Bird Pokémon, is a flightless bird that is an incredibly fast runner. Its two heads have separate brains and seem to operate independently, though they are generally in agreement. One head is always awake while the other sleeps, providing a constant lookout for danger. It can run at speeds of up to 60 miles per hour.

# 85. Dodrio
## Look
The evolved form of Doduo, Dodrio has three heads. Its body is larger, and it has a fan-like tail of brown feathers. Each of its three heads has a different personality: one represents joy, one represents sorrow, and one represents anger.
## Lore
Dodrio, the Triple Bird Pokémon, is a powerful and complex creature. Its three heads each have their own brain and personality. This allows it to gather information and formulate complex strategies. It is a formidable runner, even faster than Doduo. When it runs at full speed, its three heads are held out in a straight line to maintain balance.

# 86. Seel
## Look
A pinniped Pokémon with a sleek, white, fur-covered body. It has a small horn protruding from its forehead, a red tongue that is often sticking out, and two small tusks in its upper jaw. It has a long, powerful tail and two front flippers.
## Lore
Seel, the Sea Lion Pokémon, lives in icy seas. Its thick layer of blubber and its dense fur protect it from the cold. The horn on its head is used to break through ice floes. It is a graceful swimmer but is clumsy on land. It hunts for prey in the frigid waters beneath sheets of ice.

# 87. Dewgong
## Look
The evolved form of Seel, Dewgong is a larger, more elegant pinniped Pokémon. Its body is pure white and very sleek, with a long, flowing tail that resembles a mermaid's. It has a graceful and serene appearance.
## Lore
Dewgong, the Sea Lion Pokémon, is a master of icy waters. It is incredibly resistant to the cold and can swim at a steady 8 knots even in freezing temperatures. Its sleek, aerodynamic body allows it to move through the water with very little resistance. It stores thermal energy in its body to keep warm.

# 88. Grimer
## Look
An amorphous Pokémon made of purple sludge. It has two large, white eyes with small, black pupils, and a wide mouth. Its body is constantly shifting and dripping, leaving a trail of filth wherever it goes.
## Lore
Grimer, the Sludge Pokémon, is born from sludge that has been exposed to X-rays from the moon. It thrives in polluted, filthy areas and feeds on industrial waste and sewage. Wherever a Grimer passes, poisonous plants will grow. The sludge that makes up its body is filled with a variety of germs and toxins.

# 89. Muk
## Look
The evolved form of Grimer, Muk is a much larger and more toxic pile of sludge. Its body is a similar purple color, but it is much larger and more imposing. Its eyes are half-lidded, and its mouth is constantly agape.
## Lore
Muk, the Sludge Pokémon, has a truly horrific stench. Its body contains a mixture of potent toxins that can be fatal. A single drop of its body fluid can poison a large pool of water. It is so toxic that even its footprints are poisonous. In recent years, its population has dwindled due to improvements in sanitation.

# 90. Shellder
## Look
A bivalve Pokémon. It is encased in a purple, clam-like shell with two large, white eyes that peer out from the opening. It has a large, red tongue that it often has lolling out of its shell. The shell is incredibly hard.
## Lore
Shellder, the Bivalve Pokémon, has a shell that is harder than diamond. It is almost impervious to attack when its shell is closed. However, it is vulnerable when it opens its shell to hunt or observe its surroundings. It can swim backward by rapidly snapping its shell open and shut.

# 91. Cloyster
## Look
The evolved form of Shellder, Cloyster is a bivalve Pokémon encased in a massive, spiky, gray shell. The shell is incredibly tough. Inside the shell is a black, pearl-like core with a single horn and two menacing eyes.
## Lore
Cloyster, the Bivalve Pokémon, is a formidable defensive Pokémon. Its shell is so hard that not even a bomb can scratch it. It only opens its shell to attack. When it attacks, it launches the sharp spikes from its shell at its foe. The core inside the shell is a mystery, as no one has ever been able to see it clearly.

# 92. Gastly
## Look
A Pokémon composed of a black, spherical core surrounded by a wispy, purple gas. It has large, white eyes with small, black pupils, and a wide mouth with two small, vampire-like fangs. It is almost entirely made of gas.
## Lore
Gastly, the Gas Pokémon, is a mischievous and dangerous creature. Its body is made of a thin, gas-like substance that can envelop a foe of any size. This gas is poisonous and can cause fainting. It can be dispersed by a strong gust of wind, but it will quickly reform. It often lurks in old, abandoned buildings.

# 93. Haunter
## Look
The evolved form of Gastly, Haunter is a purple, gaseous Pokémon with a more defined form. It has a ghostly body with two detached, three-fingered hands. It has a large, menacing grin and triangular, white eyes.
## Lore
Haunter, the Gas Pokémon, is a terrifying predator. It can pass through solid walls, making it impossible to escape from. It stalks its prey in the dark, and its touch can drain the victim's life force, causing them to tremble uncontrollably. If it licks its victim, it can cause paralysis. It is said to be from another dimension.

# 94. Gengar
## Look
The final evolution, Gengar is a bipedal, dark purple Pokémon with a roundish body. It has a wide, sinister grin, red eyes, and pointed ears. It has spikes running down its back. It has arms and legs, but it can also become intangible.
## Lore
Gengar, the Shadow Pokémon, is a creature of pure mischief and malice. It enjoys playing pranks on people, such as pretending to be their shadow and then suddenly moving on its own. It is said that Gengar can steal the heat from its surroundings, and a sudden chill is a sign that one is nearby. It hides in the shadows, waiting for the perfect moment to frighten its victim.

# 95. Onix
## Look
A massive Pokémon composed of a series of gigantic, gray boulders linked together to form a serpentine body. It has a fin-like crest on its head and simple, black eyes. It has a magnet in its brain that acts as a compass.
## Lore
Onix, the Rock Snake Pokémon, can burrow through the ground at speeds of up to 50 miles per hour. It twists and squirms its way through the earth, creating a network of tunnels. As it grows, its rocky body becomes harder and more like a diamond. It makes a tremendous rumbling noise as it tunnels, and it can be heard from miles away.

# 96. Drowzee
## Look
A bipedal Pokémon with a humanoid appearance, resembling a tapir. It has a yellow upper body and a brown lower body. It has a short, trunk-like nose, droopy eyes, and triangular ears. It holds its hands in a way that suggests it is about to perform hypnosis.
## Lore
Drowzee, the Hypnosis Pokémon, is a creature that feeds on dreams. It puts its enemies to sleep and then eats their dreams through their nostrils. It is said that it can distinguish between good dreams and bad dreams, and it prefers to eat the fun, tasty dreams of children. It can get sick if it eats nightmares.

# 97. Hypno
## Look
The evolved form of Drowzee, Hypno is a taller, humanoid Pokémon. It has a more pronounced nose, pointed ears, and a ruff of white fur around its neck. It always carries a pendulum, which it uses to hypnotize its foes.
## Lore
Hypno, the Hypnosis Pokémon, is a master of hypnotism. The pendulum it carries is used to lull its foes into a deep sleep. It is said that there was once an incident where it led away a child it had hypnotized. It polishes its pendulum regularly to ensure its hypnotic suggestions are effective.

# 98. Krabby
## Look
A small, crustacean Pokémon that resembles a crab. It has a red upper body and a cream-colored lower body. It has two large pincers, with the left one being significantly larger than the right. It has two small eyes on top of its head.
## Lore
Krabby, the River Crab Pokémon, lives on beaches and in sandy areas. If it feels threatened, it will retreat into its burrow. It uses its large pincers for defense and for maintaining balance when it walks sideways. If it loses a pincer, a new one will grow back in its place.

# 99. Kingler
## Look
The evolved form of Krabby, Kingler is a much larger and more formidable crab Pokémon. Its left pincer has grown to an enormous size, becoming its primary weapon. Its body is a mix of red and orange, and it has a crown-like set of spikes on its head.
## Lore
Kingler, the Pincer Pokémon, has a massive pincer that boasts a crushing strength of 10,000 horsepower. However, this pincer is so large and heavy that Kingler has difficulty aiming it properly. It can be seen waving its massive pincer in the air to communicate with others.

# 100. Voltorb
## Look
A spherical Pokémon that bears a striking resemblance to a Poké Ball. It has a red upper half, a white lower half, and a black line around its equator. It has two angry-looking eyes on its red half. The Hisuian form resembles an old-fashioned, wooden Poké Ball.
## Lore
Voltorb, the Ball Pokémon, is a mysterious creature often found in power plants. It is highly volatile and is known to explode with little to no provocation, especially when it is angry or gets bumped. It is often mistaken for a Poké Ball by unsuspecting Trainers, leading to a shocking surprise.

... I will now continue generating the rest of the entries as a continuation of this list.

# 101. Electrode
## Look
The evolved form of Voltorb, Electrode is a larger, spherical Pokémon that resembles an upside-down Poké Ball. Its upper half is white, and its lower half is red. Its most notable feature is a wide, manic grin, contrasting with its still-angry eyes.
## Lore
Electrode, the Ball Pokémon, is infamous for its tendency to explode. It is filled with a massive amount of electrical energy, and it will detonate at the slightest stimulus. It is known to feed on electricity directly from power plants. The more energy it consumes, the faster it moves. It is often referred to as the "Bomb Ball."

# 102. Exeggcute
## Look
Exeggcute appears to be a group of six individual, egg-like creatures. Each "egg" has a slightly different facial expression, and some may have cracks in their shells. They are a pale pink color. Despite their appearance, they are more closely related to plant seeds than to eggs.
## Lore
Exeggcute, the Egg Pokémon, is a collective of six individuals that are linked by a strong telepathic bond. They will always travel together. If one of the heads gets separated, the others will quickly gather to find it. They are known to attract Pidgeotto with their appearance, forcing them to use their psychic powers to defend themselves.

# 103. Exeggutor
## Look
A very tall, palm tree-like Pokémon. Its trunk-like body is brown, and its head is a canopy of three to six round, coconut-like heads, each with a different facial expression. It has two large, green leaves for arms. The Alolan form is absurdly tall with a long, slender neck and a fourth head on its tail.
## Lore
Exeggutor, the Coconut Pokémon, is a dual-type Grass/Psychic Pokémon. Each of its heads thinks independently, but they are generally friendly and never argue. It is said that if one of its heads falls off, it will regroup with others to form a new Exeggcute. The strong sunlight of the Alola region is responsible for its massive growth in that form.

# 104. Cubone
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon with a brown hide. Its most tragic and defining feature is the skull of its deceased mother that it wears as a helmet, obscuring its true face. It always carries a bone, which it uses as a weapon. Its eyes, visible through the sockets of the skull, are tear-filled.
## Lore
Cubone, the Lonely Pokémon, is perpetually in mourning for the mother it will never see again. The skull it wears is its mother's, and it is said that no one has ever seen Cubone's real face. Its cries echo inside the skull, coming out as a sad and mournful melody. It often looks at the full moon and weeps, as it reminds it of its mother.

# 105. Marowak
## Look
The evolved form of Cubone, Marowak is a taller, fiercer Pokémon. The skull it wears has now fused to its head and can no longer be removed. It is a bipedal creature with a tough, brown hide and a spiky crest on its back. It is an expert at wielding its bone club. The Alolan form is a Ghost/Fire type with a dark body and ghostly green flames on its bone.
## Lore
Marowak, the Bone Keeper Pokémon, has overcome its grief through evolution and has become a powerful and hardened warrior. It uses its bone club with incredible skill, capable of using it as both a weapon and a boomerang. It is a very protective Pokémon, especially of young Cubone. They live in mountains and collect bones to build their homes.

# 106. Hitmonlee
## Look
A humanoid Pokémon with a brown, ovoid body and long, extendable limbs. It has no visible head or neck, with its face situated on its torso. Its most prominent features are its incredibly flexible legs, which can stretch to twice their normal length. It has three-toed feet.
## Lore
Hitmonlee, the Kicking Pokémon, is a master of kicking attacks. Its legs can stretch and contract like a spring, allowing it to strike foes from a distance. Its sense of balance is superb. After a battle, it will carefully rub down its legs to relax the muscles and prepare for the next fight.

# 107. Hitmonchan
## Look
A humanoid Pokémon with a tan body, wearing what appears to be a purple tunic and boxing gloves. It has a sturdy, athletic build. Its arms are incredibly fast, and it has a determined expression. It wears skirt-like plating on its hips.
## Lore
Hitmonchan, the Punching Pokémon, is said to possess the spirit of a world-champion boxer. Its punches are faster than a speeding bullet train. It can throw a variety of punches, including fire, ice, and thunder punches. It needs a short break after every three minutes of fighting.

# 108. Lickitung
## Look
A large, bipedal, pink Pokémon with a remarkably long and dextrous tongue. It has a round body, a thick tail, and a cream-colored bib-like pattern on its chest. Its tongue is often lolling out of its mouth and is twice the length of its body.
## Lore
Lickitung, the Licking Pokémon, uses its incredibly long tongue for everything from cleaning itself to investigating objects and attacking foes. A lick from its tongue can cause paralysis due to a numbing saliva. It has an excellent memory for tastes and textures and will always lick something new it encounters.

# 109. Koffing
## Look
A spherical, purple Pokémon filled with toxic gases. It has a skull-and-crossbones-like marking below its face. Its body is covered in small, geyser-like protrusions that vent gas. It has a mischievous, toothy grin and wide, vacant eyes.
## Lore
Koffing, the Poison Gas Pokémon, is a floating ball of toxins. It stores a variety of poisonous gases inside its body. It can mix these gases to create different effects. It is often found in urban areas, near garbage dumps. It has a tendency to explode if it gets too agitated.

# 110. Weezing
## Look
Weezing is composed of two Koffing heads that have fused together. The larger, primary head is connected to a smaller, secondary head. Both have the same vacant expression and skull-and-crossbones marking. It is constantly leaking poisonous gas. The Galarian form is green with tall, smokestack-like "hats".
## Lore
Weezing, the Poison Gas Pokémon, is the result of two Koffing finding each other and merging. It grows by absorbing the fumes from rotting garbage. It is said that in extremely rare cases, a triplet can be formed. The two heads take turns inflating and deflating to mix their toxic gases for maximum potency.

# 111. Rhyhorn
## Look
A quadrupedal Pokémon that resembles a rhinoceros. Its body is covered in thick, gray, rocky plates that act as armor. It has a single, powerful horn on its snout and a smaller one above it. Its legs are short and stubby, and it has a small, triangular head.
## Lore
Rhyhorn, the Spikes Pokémon, has a brain that is very small, making it a creature of simple, straightforward charges. Once it starts running, it cannot stop or turn easily. It is not very intelligent and will forget why it started charging. Its bones are 1,000 times harder than human bones, and it can topple a skyscraper with its charge.

# 112. Rhydon
## Look
The evolved form of Rhyhorn, Rhydon is a bipedal, rhinoceros-like Pokémon. Its hide is a similar gray, but it now stands on two powerful legs. It has a prominent, drill-like horn on its snout and a series of rocky plates on its back. It is a powerful and intimidating creature.
## Lore
Rhydon, the Drill Pokémon, has a horn that can shatter diamonds. It is protected by a hide that is tough enough to withstand volcanic eruptions. It is said to be able to live in molten lava. Its brain has developed since evolving, making it more intelligent than Rhyhorn, but it is still quite forgetful.

# 113. Chansey
## Look
A large, pink, ovoid Pokémon with a gentle and caring appearance. It has a single egg that it holds in a pouch on its belly. It has a tuft of hair on each side of its head and walks on two small feet.
## Lore
Chansey, the Egg Pokémon, is a very kind and compassionate creature. It is known to share its highly nutritious egg with injured people and Pokémon. These eggs are so delicious and full of nutrients that they are considered a delicacy. Chansey is a very rare Pokémon and is said to bring happiness to the Trainer who catches it.

# 114. Tangela
## Look
A mysterious Pokémon whose true body is completely obscured by a thick, tangled mass of blue vines. The only visible features are its two large, white eyes, which peer out from the darkness of the vines. It has two red, boot-like feet.
## Lore
Tangela, the Vine Pokémon, is a creature shrouded in mystery. Its body is hidden by a cloak of blue vines that are constantly growing. If any of the vines are pulled off, they can be regrown immediately. The vines are always in motion, which unnerves its opponents. No one knows what its true face or body looks like.

# 115. Kangaskhan
## Look
A large, bipedal, kangaroo-like Pokémon. It has a tough, brown hide and armor-like plates on its head and back. Its most defining feature is the baby that it raises in the pouch on its belly. The mother has a fierce, protective look in her eyes.
## Lore
Kangaskhan, the Parent Pokémon, is a motherly and fiercely protective creature. It will never abandon its young, no matter what danger it faces. The baby rarely leaves the safety of its mother's pouch until it is three years old. If its young is threatened, the mother will go into a violent rage to protect it.

# 116. Horsea
## Look
A small, blue, seahorse-like Pokémon. It has a curled tail that it uses to anchor itself to coral and rocks. It has a long snout, small wings on its head, and red eyes. It can shoot ink from its mouth.
## Lore
Horsea, the Dragon Pokémon, is a small and gentle creature. It is a very capable swimmer, using its dorsal fin to move through the water. If it senses danger, it will shoot a thick, black ink from its mouth and use the confusion to escape. It can be seen playing with others of its kind, using their tails to link together.

# 117. Seadra
## Look
The evolved form of Horsea, Seadra is a larger and more aggressive seahorse Pokémon. Its body is covered in sharp, venomous spines. It has a larger, more powerful dorsal fin and a fiercer expression. Its snout is long and pointed.
## Lore
Seadra, the Dragon Pokémon, is a formidable predator. The spines on its body are laced with a potent venom. It is a very fast swimmer, capable of creating whirlpools by swimming in circles. It is very territorial and will attack anyone who comes near its nest.

# 118. Goldeen
## Look
A graceful, fish-like Pokémon with a white body and orange markings. It has a single, sharp horn on its forehead, flowing, gown-like fins, and blue eyes. It is known for its beauty and elegance.
## Lore
Goldeen, the Goldfish Pokémon, is a powerful swimmer, capable of swimming up waterfalls and strong river currents at a speed of five knots. It is known as the "Water Dancer" for its elegant swimming style. The horn on its head is a powerful weapon, and it will use it to ram any foe that disturbs its home.

# 119. Seaking
## Look
The evolved form of Goldeen, Seaking is a larger and more powerful fish Pokémon. It has a vibrant orange and white body, and its horn is now much larger and more drill-like. Its fins are elaborate and flowing. The males have larger horns than the females.
## Lore
Seaking, the Goldfish Pokémon, is a very protective parent. In the autumn, the males can be seen performing elaborate courtship dances in rivers to attract a mate. They use their powerful horns to drill through boulders in rivers to create a safe place for their eggs. They will defend their nests with their lives.

# 120. Staryu
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a golden-brown sea star. It has five points and a red, gem-like core in the center of its body. This core glows and pulses with light. It is an asexual creature.
## Lore
Staryu, the Star Shape Pokémon, is a mysterious creature often found on seabeds. The red core in its center is its primary organ. At night, it will float to the surface and flash its core, blinking in time with the stars in the sky. It has incredible regenerative abilities; it can regrow any of its points as long as the central core remains intact.

# 121. Starmie
## Look
The evolved form of Staryu, Starmie is composed of two sea stars stacked on top of each other. The front star has a large, faceted, red gem, while the back star is a deep purple. It can rotate its back star 360 degrees.
## Lore
Starmie, the Mysterious Pokémon, is a bizarre and otherworldly creature. The core in its center is said to glow with the seven colors of the rainbow. It is also said to emit strange radio signals into the night sky, leading some to believe it is a form of extraterrestrial life. Its geometric body and strange abilities make it a subject of much research.

# 122. Mr. Mime
## Look
A humanoid Pokémon with a clown-like appearance. It has a pink, spherical body with a white center, and blue, pointed "hair." It has thin, white limbs and large, five-fingered hands. It has a perpetual smile on its face. The Galarian form is an Ice/Psychic type with a more jester-like appearance.
## Lore
Mr. Mime, the Barrier Pokémon, is a master of pantomime. It can create invisible, solid walls out of thin air by vibrating its fingertips to harden the air molecules. It is very proud of its miming skills and will slap anyone who interrupts its performance. It can convince people that something invisible is actually there.

# 123. Scyther
## Look
A bipedal, insectoid Pokémon with a reptilian head. It has a green, exoskeletal body, a pair of wings, and two massive, scythe-like blades for forearms. It has a fierce, predatory look and moves with blinding speed.
## Lore
Scyther, the Mantis Pokémon, is a ninja-like predator. It is so fast that it can create after-images of itself as it moves. Its scythes are incredibly sharp and can slice through a large tree with a single swipe. It is a solitary Pokémon that hones its skills in deep mountains. It rarely flies, preferring to use its speed on the ground.

# 124. Jynx
## Look
A humanoid Pokémon with a female appearance. It has a purple face, large, pink lips, and long, blonde hair. It wears a red, dress-like garment and has white arms. It moves in a swaying, dance-like manner.
## Lore
Jynx, the Human Shape Pokémon, communicates through a series of dance-like movements that can be hypnotic to watch. It is said that its strange, human-like cries sound like a unique language. It sways its hips rhythmically as it walks, which can cause people to dance along with it uncontrollably.

# 125. Electabuzz
## Look
A bipedal, humanoid Pokémon with yellow fur and black stripes. It has two long antennae on its head and a powerful, muscular build. It has a single, large fang in its upper jaw. It is constantly crackling with electrical energy.
## Lore
Electabuzz, the Electric Pokémon, is a creature that feeds on electricity. It is often found near power plants, where it can cause massive blackouts. In a storm, they will climb to high places in the hopes of being struck by lightning. It can discharge electricity from its entire body, and a blue light emanates from it when it is storing up a charge.

# 126. Magmar
## Look
A bipedal, avian-like Pokémon with a body that appears to be made of molten rock. It has a duck-like beak, a flame on its tail, and a fiery pattern on its chest. It has a crest of flames on its head and is always surrounded by a shimmering heat haze.
## Lore
Magmar, the Spitfire Pokémon, is a creature born in the heart of a volcano. Its body temperature is over 2,200 degrees Fahrenheit. It dislikes cold places and will heal its injuries by dipping itself in lava. When it breathes, it expels scorching flames, and it can turn its surroundings into a sea of fire.

# 127. Pinsir
## Look
