A large, bipedal, stag beetle-like Pokémon. It has a tough, brown exoskeleton and a wide, menacing mouth with sharp teeth. Its most prominent feature is the two massive, spike-covered pincers on its head, which it uses to grip and crush its foes.
## Lore
Pinsir, the Stag Beetle Pokémon, is incredibly strong. It can lift an object twice its own weight with its powerful pincers. It uses these pincers to crush its prey and will not let go until its victim has been shattered. In cold weather, it becomes sluggish and burrows underground to sleep.

# 128. Tauros
## Look
A bovine Pokémon that resembles a large, brown bull. It has a thick, muscular build, a shaggy mane around its neck, and three long, whip-like tails. It has a pair of large, sharp horns on its head and a determined, aggressive expression.
## Lore
Tauros, the Wild Bull Pokémon, is a violent and aggressive creature. Once it starts charging, it will not stop until it has hit something. It is known for its incredible stamina and its wild, untamable nature. It works itself into a rage by whipping itself with its three tails.

# 129. Magikarp
## Look
A medium-sized fish Pokémon with large, vacant eyes and a gaping mouth. Its body is primarily orange with large, floppy fins. It is known for being incredibly weak and pathetic, mostly just splashing around aimlessly.
## Lore
Magikarp, the Fish Pokémon, is famous for being the weakest of all Pokémon. It is capable of only a single move, Splash, which has no effect in battle. It is a very resilient Pokémon, however, and can survive in any body of water, no matter how polluted. Its evolutionary line is a testament to the idea that even the weakest can become strong.

# 130. Gyarados
## Look
A massive, serpentine, sea dragon-like Pokémon. Its body is a deep blue with a yellow underbelly and yellow spots. It has a large, gaping mouth with four pointed fangs and a three-pointed crest on its head. It has a fierce and terrifying appearance.
## Lore
Gyarados, the Atrocious Pokémon, is the result of a Magikarp's evolution. This process is so traumatic that it is said to alter its brain cells, leaving it in a state of perpetual rage. It is an incredibly destructive Pokémon, capable of leveling entire cities with its Hyper Beam attack. Once it goes on a rampage, it will not calm down until everything around it has been destroyed.

# 131. Lapras
## Look
A large, aquatic, plesiosaur-like Pokémon. It has a blue, reptilian body, a long, elegant neck, and a large, gray, spiky shell on its back. It has a gentle and intelligent expression with large, brown eyes and a small horn on its forehead.
## Lore
Lapras, the Transport Pokémon, is a highly intelligent and gentle creature. It has a beautiful singing voice and can understand human speech. It enjoys ferrying people across the sea on its back. It is an endangered species due to overhunting, but recent conservation efforts have helped its population recover. They are known to travel in large pods.

# 132. Ditto
## Look
An amorphous, pinkish-purple blob Pokémon. It has a very simple face with two small, beady eyes and a wide, straight mouth. Its body is incredibly flexible and can be rearranged at a cellular level.
## Lore
Ditto, the Transform Pokémon, has the unique ability to transform into an exact replica of any object or living creature it sees. It can reconstitute its entire cellular structure to change its form. However, if it tries to transform from memory, it can sometimes get the details wrong. When it sleeps, it transforms into a rock to avoid being attacked.

# 133. Eevee
## Look
A small, mammalian, fox-like Pokémon with brown fur. It has a bushy, cream-colored mane around its neck and a fluffy tail with a cream-colored tip. It has long, rabbit-like ears and large, brown eyes, giving it a very cute and innocent appearance.
## Lore
Eevee, the Evolution Pokémon, has a rare and unique genetic code that is irregularly structured. This allows it to evolve into multiple different forms depending on its environment, the time of day, or its bond with its Trainer. This adaptability is a survival mechanism that allows it to thrive in a variety of conditions.

# 134. Vaporeon
## Look
An aquatic, feline Pokémon. Its body is a sleek, light blue with a dark blue ridge running down its back. It has a white ruff around its neck, webbed ears, and a long, fish-like tail. Its body is adapted for aquatic life.
## Lore
Vaporeon, the Bubble Jet Pokémon, is an evolution of Eevee. Its cellular structure is similar to water molecules, which allows it to melt into water and become invisible. It is a graceful swimmer and has developed gills, allowing it to breathe underwater. The fin on its tail vibrates when it senses that rain is coming.

# 135. Jolteon
## Look
A quadrupedal, canine Pokémon with a body covered in sharp, spiky, yellow fur. It has a white, spiky ruff around its neck. Its body is lean and built for speed. It is constantly crackling with static electricity.
## Lore
Jolteon, the Lightning Pokémon, is an evolution of Eevee. Its fur acts like sharp needles that can be fired at its foes. It can generate 10,000 volts of electricity. The cells in its body create a low level of electricity, which is amplified by the static in its fur. It is a very sensitive and moody Pokémon.

# 136. Flareon
## Look
A quadrupedal, feline Pokémon with reddish-orange fur. It has a large, fluffy, yellow mane, a bushy tail, and a tuft of fur on its head. It has a very soft and cuddly appearance, but its body is incredibly hot.
## Lore
Flareon, the Flame Pokémon, is an evolution of Eevee. It has a flame sac in its body that stores and heats the air it breathes, which it can then expel as a 1,300-degree flame. Its fluffy fur is not just for show; it releases heat into the air to keep its body from overheating. When it is excited, its body temperature can rise to dangerous levels.

# 137. Porygon
## Look
A Pokémon made entirely of programming code. It has a blocky, polygonal appearance, with a pink polyhedral body, a blue, beak-like snout, and blue, cuboid feet. It moves in a jerky, unnatural manner.
## Lore
Porygon, the Virtual Pokémon, is the first man-made Pokémon created through scientific research. It is capable of moving freely through cyberspace and does not need to breathe. It is protected by a copy-protection code, preventing it from being duplicated by unauthorized means. It is a purely digital entity.

# 138. Omanyte
## Look
A small, prehistoric, ammonite-like Pokémon. It has a light blue, squid-like body with ten tentacles and two large, round eyes. It lives inside a hard, spiral-shaped, beige shell.
## Lore
Omanyte, the Spiral Pokémon, is an ancient Pokémon that was resurrected from a fossil. It was thought to be extinct. It uses the air stored in its shell to control its buoyancy, allowing it to sink to the ocean floor or rise to the surface. It is believed to have hunted by snatching prey with its tentacles.

# 139. Omastar
## Look
The evolved form of Omanyte, Omastar is a larger and more formidable ammonite Pokémon. Its shell is now larger and has developed sharp spikes. Its tentacles are more developed, and its beak is powerful enough to crush rock.
## Lore
Omastar, the Spiral Pokémon, is a powerful prehistoric predator. It is believed to have become extinct because its shell grew too large and heavy, making it too slow to catch prey. It would use its sharp beak and tentacles to break open the shells of other Pokémon like Shellder to eat the insides.

# 140. Kabuto
## Look
A small, prehistoric Pokémon that resembles a horseshoe crab. It has a hard, brown, dome-shaped shell covering its body. From underneath the shell, two large, glowing red eyes can be seen. It has four small, spindly legs.
## Lore
Kabuto, the Shellfish Pokémon, is another ancient Pokémon resurrected from a fossil. It is a living fossil that has not changed in 300 million years. It is protected by its hard shell. It often hides on the ocean floor, with only its eyes exposed, waiting for prey to come near.

# 141. Kabutops
## Look
The evolved form of Kabuto, Kabutops is a bipedal, insectoid Pokémon with a sleek, skeletal appearance. It has a flat, gray body, a pointed head, and two massive, scythe-like blades for arms. It is a swift and deadly-looking predator.
## Lore
Kabutops, the Shellfish Pokémon, is a fearsome prehistoric hunter. It was a highly efficient predator that was adapted to life in the water. It would use its incredible swimming speed and its sharp scythes to slice up its prey and drain its bodily fluids. It is believed that its prey began to adapt to life on land, which led to Kabutops's extinction.

# 142. Aerodactyl
## Look
A large, reptilian, pterosaur-like Pokémon. It has a gray, rocky hide, massive, leathery wings, and a sharp, serrated jaw filled with pointed teeth. It has a fierce, prehistoric appearance and a long, pointed tail.
## Lore
Aerodactyl, the Fossil Pokémon, was resurrected from genetic material found in Old Amber. It was the king of the skies in the age of dinosaurs. It has a savage and brutal nature, and it was known to tear out the throats of its prey with its sharp, serrated teeth. Its cry was a high-pitched, screeching sound that struck fear into all who heard it.

# 143. Snorlax
## Look
A massive, bipedal Pokémon with a huge, spherical body. Its fur is a dark blue-green, and its belly is a creamy white. It has a large head, two pointed ears, and its eyes are almost always closed, as it is usually sleeping. It has two small fangs in its lower jaw.
## Lore
Snorlax, the Sleeping Pokémon, is a creature of simple desires: eating and sleeping. Its entire day consists of these two activities. It is not satisfied unless it eats over 880 pounds of food a day. After it finishes eating, it immediately falls asleep. Its stomach is so strong that it can digest anything, even poison or moldy food, without any ill effects.

# 144. Articuno
## Look
A legendary bird Pokémon with a majestic and elegant appearance. It has a sky-blue plumage, a long, flowing, three-crested plume on its head, and a beautiful, ribbon-like tail. Its wings are said to be made of ice.
## Lore
Articuno, the Freeze Pokémon, is one of the three legendary birds of Kanto. It is a legendary bird of ice. It is said that when Articuno flies, the moisture in the air is chilled, causing it to snow. It is a compassionate Pokémon that is known to appear to doomed travelers in icy mountains, leading them to safety.

# 145. Zapdos
## Look
A legendary bird Pokémon with a jagged, spiky appearance. Its plumage is yellow and black, and its wings and tail are made of sharp, pointed feathers. It has a long, pointed beak and a spiky crest on its head. It is always surrounded by a crackling electrical energy.
## Lore
Zapdos, the Electric Pokémon, is one of the three legendary birds of Kanto. It is a legendary bird of lightning. It is said that it makes its nest in thunderclouds. It gains power when it is struck by lightning. The flapping of its wings can create massive thunderstorms.

# 146. Moltres
## Look
A legendary bird Pokémon whose body is perpetually engulfed in brilliant, beautiful flames. It has a golden plumage, and its wings and tail are a magnificent blaze of fire. It has a long, flowing crest of fire on its head.
## Lore
Moltres, the Flame Pokémon, is one of the three legendary birds of Kanto. It is a legendary bird of fire. It is said that its appearance can bring an early spring to a wintery land. When it is injured, it is said to dip its body in the molten magma of a volcano to heal itself. The flapping of its wings scatters embers that can cause massive fires.

# 147. Dratini
## Look
A small, serpentine, aquatic Pokémon. It has a blue body, a white, three-pronged fin on each side of its head, and a white snout. It has a small, pearl-like bead on its forehead. It is a very rare and elusive creature.
## Lore
Dratini, the Dragon Pokémon, is a creature of myth and legend. It is known as the "Mirage Pokémon" because it is so rarely seen. It is a constantly growing Pokémon, and it sheds its skin as it gets larger. It lives in deep bodies of water, and its existence was only recently confirmed by a fisherman who caught one.

# 148. Dragonair
## Look
A long, elegant, serpentine Pokémon. Its body is a sky blue, with a white underside. It has small, feathery wings on its head and three crystal-like blue orbs: one on its neck and two on its tail. It has a graceful and mystical appearance.
## Lore
Dragonair, the Dragon Pokémon, is a creature that is imbued with a powerful life energy. It can discharge this energy from the crystals on its body, which is said to allow it to control the weather. It is a very gentle and benevolent creature, and it is said to be surrounded by a soft, glowing aura.

# 149. Dragonite
## Look
A large, bipedal, draconic Pokémon with a kind and friendly appearance. It has orange skin, a cream-colored belly, and two small, green wings on its back that are disproportionately small for its body size. It has a long tail, a small horn on its head, and two long antennae.
## Lore
Dragonite, the Dragon Pokémon, is an incredibly rare and powerful creature. Despite its massive size, it is capable of flying at speeds faster than the speed of sound. It is a very kind-hearted Pokémon with a human-like intelligence. It is said to fly across the sea, rescuing people who are drowning. It is known as the "Guardian of the Sea."

# 150. Mewtwo
## Look
A powerful, bipedal, humanoid Pokémon with a feline-like appearance. It has a gray body with a long, purple tail and a purple underside. It has a fierce, intelligent look in its purple eyes. It has a second "neck" or tube connecting its head to its back.
## Lore
Mewtwo, the Genetic Pokémon, was created through horrific gene-splicing and DNA engineering experiments based on the DNA of the mythical Pokémon, Mew. It is a Pokémon created by science, and it is said to have the most savage heart among all Pokémon. It was created for battle and is known to be incredibly powerful and ruthless. It rests motionless to conserve energy, preparing for its next fight.

# 151. Mew
## Look
A small, pink, feline-like Pokémon with a long, thin tail that ends in a bulbous tip. It has large, blue eyes and a playful, curious expression. It is a mythical Pokémon, thought to be a mirage.
## Lore
Mew, the New Species Pokémon, is an incredibly rare and mysterious creature. It is said to possess the genetic code of all Pokémon, which gives it the unique ability to learn any move. It is believed to be the ancestor of all Pokémon. It is only visible to those who are pure of heart. It is a playful and intelligent creature that is rarely seen by humans.

... I will continue with the next generation of Pokémon.

# 152. Chikorita
## Look
A small, quadrupedal Pokémon with a pale green body and a darker green leaf on its head. It has a necklace of small, green buds around its neck. It has large, red eyes and a gentle appearance. The leaf on its head gives off a sweet aroma.
## Lore
Chikorita, the Leaf Pokémon, is a gentle and docile creature. The leaf on its head releases a sweet, calming aroma that can soothe and pacify those around it. It enjoys sunbathing and uses its leaf to seek out warm places. It is a very loyal Pokémon that forms a strong bond with its Trainer.

# 153. Bayleef
## Look
The evolved form of Chikorita, Bayleef is a larger, quadrupedal Pokémon. The leaf on its head has been replaced by a single, large, serrated leaf. The ring of buds around its neck has now sprouted into small, rolled-up leaves. It has a more confident and determined expression.
## Lore
Bayleef, the Leaf Pokémon, has a spicy aroma that emanates from the leaves around its neck. This aroma has a stimulating effect and can restore health and make people feel energetic. It is a very caring Pokémon and is known to be a loyal companion. The aroma becomes stronger when it is happy.

# 154. Meganium
## Look
A large, quadrupedal, dinosaur-like Pokémon. It has a green body and a massive, pink flower with two long, yellow antennae-like stamens around its neck. The petals of the flower are large and vibrant. It has a gentle and serene appearance.
## Lore
Meganium, the Herb Pokémon, has a flower that releases a soothing and revitalizing aroma. This fragrance can calm aggressive feelings and has the power to revive dead plants and make the soil fertile. Anyone who stands near a Meganium is said to feel refreshed and at peace. It is a very gentle and benevolent creature.

# 155. Cyndaquil
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon with a bluish-green back and a cream-colored belly. Its most notable feature is the set of four red spots on its back, which can erupt into flames. It has a long snout and its eyes are usually closed.
## Lore
Cyndaquil, the Fire Mouse Pokémon, is a timid and shy creature. It often curls up into a ball to protect itself. When it is angry or startled, the flames on its back flare up. It is not yet very skilled at controlling its fire. It is known to be a very emotional Pokémon.

# 156. Quilava
## Look
A sleek, weasel-like Pokémon. It has a lean, blue and cream-colored body. The flames on its back are now a crest of fire that runs from its head to its tail. It has a more confident and aggressive look than its pre-evolution.
## Lore
Quilava, the Volcano Pokémon, is a much more capable fighter than Cyndaquil. It can control its flames with great skill. It intimidates its foes with the heat of its flames. It turns its back on its opponent to show off the intensity of its fire before attacking with a charge of flames.

# 157. Typhlosion
## Look
A large, bipedal Pokémon with a bulky and powerful build. It has a dark blue-green back and a cream-colored belly. Its most impressive feature is the explosive collar of fire that erupts from its neck when it is ready to battle. It has a fierce and intimidating presence.
## Lore
Typhlosion, the Volcano Pokémon, is a powerful and destructive creature. It can create massive, fiery explosions by rubbing its fur together, which can incinerate anything in its path. It hides itself behind a shimmering heat haze that it creates with its intense flames. When its anger reaches its peak, it becomes so hot that anything it touches will instantly go up in flames.

# 158. Totodile
## Look
A small, bipedal, crocodilian Pokémon. It has a blue body with a yellow, V-shaped marking on its chest. It has a powerful jaw filled with sharp teeth, and a ridge of red spines running down its back and tail. It has a playful and mischievous look.
## Lore
Totodile, the Big Jaw Pokémon, has a habit of biting anything it sees. Its jaw is incredibly powerful for its small size, and it can easily crush rocks. It is a very energetic and playful Pokémon, but its bites can be dangerous, so its Trainer must be careful.

# 159. Croconaw
## Look
The evolved form of Totodile, Croconaw is a larger and more robust crocodilian Pokémon. It wears what appears to be a pattern resembling a caveman's tunic on its belly. Its jaw is even larger and more powerful, and its teeth are slanted backward, making it impossible for prey to escape its grip.
## Lore
Croconaw, the Big Jaw Pokémon, has a powerful jaw that can snap anything in half. Once it bites down on something, it will not let go. The teeth in its mouth are arranged in a way that, if one breaks, a new one will grow in its place. It is a very aggressive Pokémon.

# 160. Feraligatr
## Look
A massive, bipedal, crocodilian Pokémon with a bulky and powerful build. It has a blue, armored hide with large, red, plate-like scales on its back. Its jaw is immense and filled with jagged teeth. It is an intimidating and powerful-looking creature.
## Lore
Feraligatr, the Big Jaw Pokémon, is a ferocious and powerful predator. When it bites down with its massive jaws, it shakes its head violently to tear its prey apart. It is a surprisingly fast runner despite its bulk, and it can intimidate its foes just by opening its massive, tooth-filled mouth.

# 161. Sentret
## Look
A small, mammalian Pokémon that resembles a meerkat or flying squirrel. It has a brown, cylindrical body with a cream-colored belly and a ringed tail. Its most notable feature is its ability to stand on its tail to get a better view of its surroundings.
## Lore
Sentret, the Scout Pokémon, is a very cautious and alert creature. It stands on its tail to see far over the tall grass, looking for danger. If it spots a predator, it will cry out loudly and thump its tail on the ground to warn its colony. It is a very nervous Pokémon and cannot sleep unless another Sentret is on lookout duty.

# 162. Furret
## Look
The evolved form of Sentret, Furret has a very long, slender, ferret-like body. Its fur is a mix of brown and cream. It is built for speed and for navigating narrow burrows. It has a cute and playful appearance.
## Lore
Furret, the Long Body Pokémon, has a slim and flexible body that allows it to squeeze through any gap. It is a very fast and agile Pokémon. Its nest is a complex, maze-like burrow that is very difficult for predators to navigate. It uses its speed to corner its prey, such as Rattata.

# 163. Hoothoot
## Look
A round, owl-like Pokémon with a brown body and a cream-colored underside. It has large, red eyes with black, clock-hand-like markings around them. It stands on a single leg, with the other tucked away. It has a small, feathery crest on its head.
## Lore
Hoothoot, the Owl Pokémon, has a special internal organ that allows it to sense the earth's rotation. This gives it a perfect sense of time, and it will always begin hooting at the exact same time every day. It stands on one leg, and it is so fast at switching legs that it is difficult to see.

# 164. Noctowl
## Look
A large, owl-like Pokémon with a majestic and intelligent appearance. It has a brown and beige plumage, large, piercing red eyes, and two large, V-shaped, feathery "eyebrows." It has a sharp beak and talons.
## Lore
Noctowl, the Owl Pokémon, is a creature of the night. Its eyes are specially adapted to see in low light, allowing it to spot prey in near-total darkness. It is a very intelligent Pokémon and is capable of rotating its head 180 degrees to see directly behind it. Its wings are soft and make no sound when it flies, allowing it to silently swoop down on its prey.

# 165. Ledyba
## Look
A small, insectoid Pokémon that resembles a ladybug. It has a red shell with five black spots, a black body, and large, friendly-looking eyes. It has two antennae and walks on two small feet.
## Lore
Ledyba, the Five Star Pokémon, is a very timid and social creature. They often gather in large swarms. They communicate with each other using a special fluid that changes its scent based on their emotions. They are very sensitive to the cold and will huddle together for warmth.

# 166. Ledian
## Look
The evolved form of Ledyba, Ledian is a taller, more humanoid ladybug Pokémon. It has a long, slender body, and its red shell has developed into a cape-like structure. It has four arms. The spots on its back are said to grow larger or smaller depending on the number of stars in the sky.
## Lore
Ledian, the Five Star Pokémon, is a nocturnal creature that uses the light of the stars as its source of energy. The more stars there are in the sky, the more powerful it becomes. It is said that in places with clean air and a clear view of the stars, massive swarms of Ledian will appear. It uses its four arms to deliver a flurry of punches.

# 167. Spinarak
## Look
A small, spider-like Pokémon with a green body. It has a single horn on its head, red eyes, and a pattern on its abdomen that resembles a happy or sad face, depending on its mood. It has six legs instead of the usual eight for a spider.
## Lore
Spinarak, the String Spit Pokémon, is a master of web-spinning. It can create a web that is strong enough to hold prey much larger than itself. It will often wait patiently in its web for unsuspecting prey to wander in. The web it spins is said to be as strong as steel.

# 168. Ariados
## Look
The evolved form of Spinarak, Ariados is a larger and more menacing spider Pokémon. It has a red and purple body, and its legs are yellow. It has a small horn on its head and a sinister-looking face on its abdomen. It can shoot silk from both its mouth and its spinneret.
## Lore
Ariados, the Long Leg Pokémon, is a relentless hunter. It attaches a thread of silk to its prey and then releases it. It will then follow the thread, no matter where the prey goes, until it can strike. It is a very patient and cunning predator. Its silk is also used to create a nest.

# 169. Crobat
## Look
A large, four-winged bat Pokémon. Its body is a deep purple, and it has developed a second pair of wings on its legs. This allows it to fly with incredible speed and silence. It has a small mouth, red eyes, and large, pointed ears.
## Lore
Crobat, the Bat Pokémon, is the final evolution of Zubat. The addition of a second pair of wings has made it an incredibly fast and silent flyer. It can fly so quietly that it can approach its prey without being detected at all. It is a very efficient hunter, capable of draining its victim's blood in an instant.

# 170. Chinchou
## Look
A small, round, aquatic Pokémon. It has a blue body, two small feet, and two large, white eyes with yellow pupils that resemble plus and minus signs. Its most notable feature is the two long antennae that dangle from its head, each ending in a light-bulb-like orb.
## Lore
Chinchou, the Angler Pokémon, lives in the deep, dark ocean where no sunlight can reach. It uses the two orbs on its antennae to communicate with others and to lure in prey. It can generate both positive and negative electrical charges from these antennae, which it can then discharge to shock its foes.

# 171. Lanturn
## Look
The evolved form of Chinchou, Lanturn is a larger, fish-like Pokémon. It has a blue and yellow body and a single, large antenna on its head that splits into two orbs. These orbs are much larger and brighter than Chinchou's. It has a friendly, placid expression.
## Lore
Lanturn, the Light Pokémon, is known as the "Deep-Sea Star." The light it emits from its antennae is so bright that it can illuminate the ocean's surface from a depth of over three miles. It uses this bright light to blind its prey and then swallow it whole. It is a very gentle and beautiful Pokémon.

# 172. Pichu
## Look
A very small, rodent-like Pokémon. It has pale yellow fur, a small, black-tipped tail, and a black collar-like marking around its neck. Its most notable features are its large ears and the pink, circular pouches on its cheeks.
## Lore
Pichu, the Tiny Mouse Pokémon, is the pre-evolved form of Pikachu. It is not yet skilled at storing electricity, and it will often accidentally discharge a shock if it is happy, excited, or startled. It is a very playful and curious Pokémon, but it cannot handle large amounts of electricity without shocking itself.

# 173. Cleffa
## Look
A small, pink, star-shaped Pokémon. It is the pre-evolved form of Clefairy. It has a small, curled tuft of hair on its head and a tiny, curled tail. It has a very cute and innocent appearance.
## Lore
Cleffa, the Star Shape Pokémon, is a mysterious creature that is believed to have arrived on a shooting star. They are often seen in areas where meteorites have fallen. They are most active on nights with meteor showers, when they can be seen dancing in a circle.

# 174. Igglybuff
## Look
A small, round, pink Pokémon. It is the pre-evolved form of Jigglypuff. It has a small tuft of fur on its head and large, red eyes. Its body is extremely soft and bouncy.
## Lore
Igglybuff, the Balloon Pokémon, has a body that is softer and more elastic than any other Pokémon. It loves to sing, but its vocal cords are not yet fully developed, so it can become hoarse if it sings for too long. It moves by bouncing, as its legs are not yet strong enough to walk properly.

# 175. Togepi
## Look
A small Pokémon that is mostly encased in a decorated eggshell. Only its head, arms, and feet are visible. The eggshell has red and blue triangular patterns on it. It has a happy and innocent appearance.
## Lore
Togepi, the Spike Ball Pokémon, is a symbol of good luck. Its shell is said to be filled with happiness, which it shares with those who are kind and caring. It is a very friendly Pokémon, but it can use its charm to put foes to sleep.

# 176. Togetic
## Look
The evolved form of Togepi, Togetic is a small, fairy-like Pokémon. It has a round, white body, small wings, and a long neck. It retains the red and blue triangular patterns on its body. It is almost always seen floating or flying.
## Lore
Togetic, the Happiness Pokémon, is a very rare sight. It is said to appear only before compassionate people, showering them with a special, glowing dust known as "joy dust," which is said to bring good fortune. It can float without flapping its wings and dislikes being in places where there is conflict or strife.

# 177. Natu
## Look
A very small, round, green bird-like Pokémon. It has red and yellow wings, a small, pointed beak, and large, white eyes with small pupils. It has two small, three-toed feet. It is known for its habit of staring intently at things.
## Lore
Natu, the Tiny Bird Pokémon, is a creature with psychic abilities. It has the power to see into the future, but it is still too young to understand what it sees. This causes it to be very cautious and timid. It is known to stare at the sun all day. It is a better hopper than it is a flyer.

# 178. Xatu
## Look
A totem-pole-like, avian Pokémon. It has a green body with black, white, and red patterns that resemble a Native American totem. It has a pair of large wings that it keeps wrapped around its body. Its most notable feature is its two eyes, which are said to see into the past and the future simultaneously.
## Lore
Xatu, the Mystic Pokémon, is a mysterious and stoic creature. It stands motionless all day, staring into the sun. It is believed that it does this because it is watching the past and the future at the same time, and it is terrified by the visions of disaster it sees, rendering it unable to move. It is a very wise and powerful psychic Pokémon.

# 179. Mareep
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, sheep-like Pokémon. Its body is covered in a thick, fluffy, cream-colored wool. It has blue skin, a yellow and black striped tail with a yellow orb at the tip, and orange, cone-shaped ears.
## Lore
Mareep, the Wool Pokémon, has wool that is constantly growing and building up a charge of static electricity. The more it moves, the more electricity it stores. The orb on its tail lights up when it has stored a lot of power. Its wool is a popular material for clothing, but it must be processed to remove the static charge.

# 180. Flaaffy
## Look
The evolved form of Mareep, Flaaffy is a bipedal, sheep-like Pokémon. It has pink skin, and its wool has been shed from its body, now only remaining on its head and around its neck. Its tail has a blue orb at the tip.
## Lore
Flaaffy, the Wool Pokémon, has wool that is of a much higher quality than Mareep's. It has special properties that allow it to store even more electricity. The rubbery parts of its skin are resistant to electricity, preventing it from shocking itself. The orb on its tail can shine so brightly that it can be seen from a great distance.

# 181. Ampharos
## Look
A tall, bipedal, sheep-like Pokémon. It has a yellow body with black stripes on its neck and tail. It has a red orb on its forehead and another on the tip of its long, slender tail. It has lost all of its wool.
## Lore
Ampharos, the Light Pokémon, is a beacon of light. The orb on its tail can shine so brightly that it can be used as a lighthouse, and it has been used to guide lost ships to safety for centuries. It is a very gentle and kind Pokémon. Its light is so powerful that it can be seen from space.

# 182. Bellossom
## Look
A small, bipedal, humanoid Pokémon that resembles a hula dancer. It has a green body and two beautiful, vibrant flowers on its head. It wears what appears to be a skirt made of green leaves. It is almost always seen dancing.
## Lore
Bellossom, the Flower Pokémon, is a rare evolution of Gloom. They are often seen gathering on cloudy days, where they perform a special dance to summon the sun. When the sun comes out, they begin to dance joyfully. The flowers on its head are said to be more beautiful the fouler the Gloom it evolved from was.

# 183. Marill
## Look
A small, round, blue, rodent-like Pokémon. It has a white, spherical belly and a long, thin, black, zig-zagging tail with a blue, ball-shaped buoy at the end. It has large, round ears with red interiors.
## Lore
Marill, the Aqua Mouse Pokémon, has a tail that acts as a flotation device. The ball at the end of its tail is filled with an oil that is lighter than water, which keeps it from drowning even in strong currents. It is a very playful Pokémon that loves to be in the water.

# 184. Azumarill
## Look
A larger, bipedal, egg-shaped, rabbit-like Pokémon. It has a blue body with a large, white, polka-dotted pattern on its belly. It has long, rabbit-like ears. Its fur is water-repellent.
## Lore
Azumarill, the Aqua Rabbit Pokémon, can create air-filled bubbles with its mouth. It uses these bubbles to help young Pokémon who are not yet good swimmers. It can live both on land and in water. It is very good at camouflage in the water, and its polka-dot pattern helps it blend in with the dappled light.

# 185. Sudowoodo
## Look
A Pokémon that perfectly mimics the appearance of a tree. It has a brown, rock-like body that resembles a tree trunk, and its "hands" are green, club-like structures that resemble foliage. It often stands perfectly still, hoping to be mistaken for a plant.
## Lore
Sudowoodo, the Imitation Pokémon, is a Rock-type that pretends to be a tree to avoid being attacked. Its composition is more similar to a rock than a plant. It is very weak to water, so it will flee if it starts to rain. It is a very rare Pokémon, and its disguise is so good that it often fools people.

# 186. Politoed
## Look
A bipedal, green, frog-like Pokémon with a cheerful and friendly appearance. It has a long, curled tuft of hair on its head and two round, pink cheek pouches. It has a small, yellow underbelly.
## Lore
Politoed, the Frog Pokémon, is considered the king of Poliwag and Poliwhirl. When three or more of them gather, they sing in a chorus with a loud, bellowing cry. The curled hair on its head is a sign of its status as a king, and the longer and more curled it is, the more respect it commands from its subjects.

# 187. Hoppip
## Look
A small, round, pink Pokémon with two long, green leaves sprouting from its head. It has large, yellow eyes and two small feet. Its body is very light, and it is often seen being blown about by the wind.
## Lore
Hoppip, the Cottonweed Pokémon, has a body that is so light that it must cling to the ground to avoid being blown away by even a gentle breeze. It is very sensitive to the wind and can sense the approach of a storm. They often gather in fields, and it is a sign that spring is coming when they are seen floating in the air.

# 188. Skiploom
## Look
The evolved form of Hoppip, Skiploom is a green, cat-like Pokémon with a yellow flower blooming on its head. It has no visible arms. The flower on its head opens and closes depending on the temperature.
## Lore
Skiploom, the Cottonweed Pokémon, can control its flight by blooming and closing the flower on its head. It can hover in the air, soaking up sunlight. It is said that when the temperature rises above 64 degrees Fahrenheit, the flower on its head will bloom. It floats on the wind, spreading pollen.

# 189. Jumpluff
## Look
The final evolution, Jumpluff is a round, blue Pokémon with three cotton-like puffs on its body: one on its head and one on each of its hands. It has two small, stubby feet and beady red eyes.
## Lore
Jumpluff, the Cottonweed Pokémon, is a master of flight. It can ride on seasonal winds, even crossing oceans to travel the world. It spreads its cotton-like spores as it flies, which is a sign of the changing of the seasons. It can control its flight path by adjusting its cotton puffs.

# 190. Aipom
## Look
A purple, monkey-like Pokémon. Its most prominent feature is its long, prehensile tail, which ends in a three-fingered, hand-like appendage. It has a wide, mischievous grin and a cowlick on its head.
## Lore
Aipom, the Long Tail Pokémon, uses its tail with incredible skill, as if it were another hand. It can use its tail to pick fruit from tall trees and to anchor itself to branches while it sleeps. It is a very playful and curious Pokémon that loves to pull pranks.

# 191. Sunkern
## Look
A small Pokémon that resembles a sunflower seed. It has a yellow body with brown stripes. On top of its head are two small, green leaves that are just beginning to sprout. It has a perpetually worried expression.
## Lore
Sunkern, the Seed Pokémon, is a very weak creature. It survives by absorbing nutrients from the morning dew on the leaves on its head. It tries to move as little as possible to conserve energy. It is known to suddenly fall from the sky in the morning.

# 192. Sunflora
## Look
The evolved form of Sunkern, Sunflora is a Pokémon that resembles a cheerful, walking sunflower. Its head is a large sunflower with a happy face in the center. Its body is green and acts as the stem, with two leaf-like arms.
## Lore
Sunflora, the Sun Pokémon, is a creature that is completely dependent on sunlight. It is most active during the day, when it converts sunlight into energy. At night, it closes its petals and becomes still. It is known to chase after the sun, always facing it to absorb as much light as possible.

# 193. Yanma
## Look
A large, dragonfly-like Pokémon. It has a red body, two large, compound eyes, and two pairs of transparent wings. Its tail ends in a club-like structure. Its eyes can see in a 360-degree radius.
## Lore
Yanma, the Clear Wing Pokémon, is an incredible flyer. It can flap its wings so fast that it can create powerful shockwaves capable of shattering glass. It is very skilled at hovering in place and making sudden, sharp turns. Its large eyes give it a complete view of its surroundings, making it very difficult to sneak up on.

# 194. Wooper
## Look
A small, amphibious Pokémon with a blue, limbless body. It has a large head, a wide mouth, and small, black eyes. It has two purple, feathery, external gills on its head. The Paldean form is a muddy brown and has bone-shaped gills.
## Lore
Wooper, the Water Fish Pokémon, lives in cold water. It often comes out onto land to search for food. When it is on land, it coats its body in a slimy, poisonous film to keep from drying out. It is a very friendly and carefree Pokémon.

# 195. Quagsire
## Look
The evolved form of Wooper, Quagsire is a bipedal, salamander-like Pokémon. It has a light blue body, a vacant and dopey expression, and a purple dorsal fin running down its back. It is a very laid-back and carefree-looking creature.
## Lore
Quagsire, the Water Fish Pokémon, is a very easygoing creature. It has a sluggish and carefree nature and often bumps its head into things as it swims, though it doesn't seem to mind. It is a very friendly Pokémon and is often seen lazing about in freshwater marshes.

# 196. Espeon
## Look
A quadrupedal, feline Pokémon with a sleek, lilac-colored body. It has long, slender ears, a forked tail, and a red gem embedded in its forehead. It has a very elegant and graceful appearance.
## Lore
Espeon, the Sun Pokémon, is an evolution of Eevee. The fine hairs that cover its body are incredibly sensitive and can be used to predict the weather and its opponent's next move by reading subtle shifts in the air currents. The gem on its forehead glows when it is using its psychic powers. It is fiercely loyal to its Trainer.

# 197. Umbreon
## Look
A sleek, black, feline Pokémon. It has a set of yellow rings on its body: one on its forehead, one on each of its ears and legs, and one on its tail. These rings glow in the dark. It has piercing red eyes.
## Lore
Umbreon, the Moonlight Pokémon, is an evolution of Eevee. It evolved after being exposed to the moon's waves. The rings on its body glow when it is excited or when it is exposed to moonlight. It hides in the darkness, waiting for its prey, and its glowing rings are used to strike fear into its opponents.

# 198. Murkrow
## Look
A small, avian Pokémon that resembles a crow. It has a dark, witch-like appearance, with its head feathers resembling a witch's hat and its tail feathers resembling a broom. It has a yellow beak and a mischievous look in its eyes.
## Lore
Murkrow, the Darkness Pokémon, is a creature that is associated with misfortune. It is attracted to shiny objects and is known to steal them from people, often leading them on a chase into dark forests where they become lost. It is a very clever and tricky Pokémon.

# 199. Slowking
## Look
A bipedal Pokémon that is the result of a Shellder biting onto a Slowpoke's head. The Slowpoke now has a much more intelligent and regal expression. The Shellder has transformed into a crown-like helmet with a red gem. The Galarian form is a Poison/Psychic type and has a darker, more sinister appearance.
## Lore
Slowking, the Royal Pokémon, is a creature of incredible intelligence and intuition. When the Shellder bit down, the venom it injected stimulated the Slowpoke's brain, unlocking a genius-level intellect. It is said to be as intelligent as a human scientist. Every time it yawns, the Shellder injects more venom, making it even smarter.

# 200. Misdreavus
## Look
A ghostly Pokémon with a round, head-like body and long, flowing, hair-like appendages. It has a mischievous look in its red eyes and wears a necklace of red orbs. It floats through the air.
## Lore
Misdreavus, the Screech Pokémon, loves to play pranks on people. It is a nocturnal Pokémon that enjoys startling people by crying and wailing in the dark. It is also known to bite and pull on people's hair. It absorbs the fear of its victims as nourishment.

... I will now continue generating the rest of the entries.

# 201. Unown
## Look
A mysterious, symbolic Pokémon with 28 different forms, each resembling a letter of the Latin alphabet, a question mark, or an exclamation mark. They are flat, black, and have a single, large eye. They are often found attached to walls in ancient ruins.
## Lore
Unown, the Symbol Pokémon, are enigmatic beings that are always found in groups. It is said that when more than one of them gather, a strange power emerges. They are able to communicate with each other telepathically. There is much debate about whether they are a language, a form of life, or something else entirely. Their connection to ancient history is a subject of intense research.

# 202. Wobbuffet
## Look
A tall, blue, amorphous Pokémon with a soft, punch-bag-like body. It has two flipper-like arms and a perpetually surprised or pained expression with closed eyes. Its most curious feature is its black tail, which has two eyes of its own.
## Lore
Wobbuffet, the Patient Pokémon, is a creature that never initiates an attack. It can only endure hits and then retaliate with powerful counter-attacks. It is fiercely protective of its black tail, leading many to believe that the tail is its true body. It often hides in dark caves to keep its tail hidden from view.

# 203. Girafarig
## Look
A giraffe-like Pokémon with a unique second head on its tail. Its front half is yellow with brown spots, and it has a gentle expression. Its back half is a dark brown/black, and the tail-head is a separate, sentient creature with a small brain, sharp teeth, and a mischievous grin.
## Lore
Girafarig, the Long Neck Pokémon, has a tail with a mind of its own. The tail-head does not need to sleep and can keep watch for danger from all directions. It will bite at anything that approaches from behind. The tail-head also has psychic powers, which it uses to help fend off attackers.

# 204. Pineco
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a gray, bagworm-like creature encased in a shell that looks like a pinecone. Its shell is made of hardened bark. Two large, round, white eyes peer out from an opening in the shell. It is almost always seen hanging from tree branches.
## Lore
Pineco, the Bagworm Pokémon, hangs from a tree branch and waits for insect prey to come near. It strengthens its shell by gluing on additional layers of bark with a fluid it produces. It dislikes being disturbed and will shake its body violently, causing it to fall to the ground. If it is shaken while on the ground, it will explode.

# 205. Forretress
## Look
The evolved form of Pineco, Forretress is a large, bivalve-like Pokémon encased in a red, steel shell that is studded with cannon-like protrusions. Its true body is hidden inside the shell, with only its eyes visible.
## Lore
Forretress, the Bagworm Pokémon, has a shell that is as hard as steel. It opens its shell at high speed to capture its prey, and then immediately closes it again. It can also launch sharp spikes from its body to attack foes. It is a very defensive Pokémon that is difficult to damage.

# 206. Dunsparce
## Look
A serpentine, grub-like Pokémon with a yellow and blue-green body. It has a pair of small, feathery wings on its back, a drill-like tail, and eyes that are perpetually closed in a sleepy expression.
## Lore
Dunsparce, the Land Snake Pokémon, is a shy and reclusive creature. It makes its nest deep underground in complex, maze-like tunnels. It uses its drill-like tail to burrow through the earth. It can float slightly off the ground using its small wings, though it is not capable of true flight. It is a very elusive Pokémon.

# 207. Gligar
## Look
A purple, scorpion-like Pokémon with bat-like features. It has large, pincer-like claws, a long, segmented tail with a stinger, and a pair of blue, membranous wings that allow it to glide. It often has its long, red tongue sticking out.
## Lore
Gligar, the Fly Scorpion Pokémon, glides through the air silently, attacking its prey from above. It latches onto its victim's face with its claws and then injects its poison with the stinger on its tail. It is a very mischievous Pokémon that enjoys startling its prey before it strikes.

# 208. Steelix
## Look
A massive, serpentine Pokémon made of solid steel. Its body is a silvery-gray, and its jaw is incredibly large and powerful. Its body is even harder than a diamond due to the immense pressure and heat it endures while living deep underground.
## Lore
Steelix, the Iron Snake Pokémon, is the evolved form of Onix. It is said that its body was compressed to an incredible degree, making it harder than any known metal. It can burrow over half a mile deep into the earth. Its eyes are capable of seeing in total darkness.

# 209. Snubbull
## Look
A small, bipedal, bulldog-like Pokémon. It has pink fur, a blue, polka-dotted frill around its neck, and a prominent underbite with two small fangs. Despite its fierce and grumpy appearance, it is a very playful and affectionate creature.
## Lore
Snubbull, the Fairy Pokémon, has a ferocious-looking face that can scare away smaller Pokémon. However, it is actually a very timid and affectionate creature that enjoys the company of humans. It is popular among women for its cute, albeit grumpy, appearance.

# 210. Granbull
## Look
The evolved form of Snubbull, Granbull is a large and intimidating bulldog Pokémon. Its lower jaw is massive and has two huge, sharp fangs. It has a powerful and muscular build. Despite its terrifying appearance, it is very timid.
## Lore
Granbull, the Fairy Pokémon, is a surprisingly cowardly creature. Its massive fangs and fierce face are its only weapons. It will rarely bite, preferring to scare foes away with its appearance. It is a very loyal and loving Pokémon to those it trusts.

# 211. Qwilfish
## Look
A spherical, pufferfish-like Pokémon. Its body is a teal and cream color. It is covered in sharp, poisonous spines that it can extend when it inflates its body. It has a perpetual, puckered-lip expression. The Hisuian form is dark with magenta spines.
## Lore
Qwilfish, the Balloon Pokémon, is a creature that defends itself by inflating its body to a massive size and shooting out the poisonous spikes that cover it. To do this, it must drink over 2.6 gallons of water. It is not a very good swimmer, so it relies on this defensive mechanism to survive.

# 212. Scizor
## Look
A bipedal, insectoid Pokémon with a sleek, metallic, crimson exoskeleton. It has a pair of wings that are used for flight and temperature regulation. Its most notable features are its two large, powerful pincers, which have eye-like markings on them.
## Lore
Scizor, the Pincer Pokémon, is the evolved form of Scyther. Its body is as hard as steel. It uses its large pincers to crush its foes. The eye-like patterns on its pincers are used to intimidate opponents, making them think it has three heads. It is a very powerful and fast Pokémon.

# 213. Shuckle
## Look
A turtle-like Pokémon whose body is hidden inside a hard, red, porous shell. Its yellow, worm-like head and limbs can protrude from holes in the shell. It is a very slow and defensive creature.
## Lore
Shuckle, the Mold Pokémon, stores berries inside its shell. The juices of the berries mix with its body fluids to create a powerful and delicious juice. It is a very docile Pokémon that prefers to hide in its shell rather than fight. It is often found hiding under rocks.

# 214. Heracross
## Look
A large, bipedal, rhinoceros beetle-like Pokémon. It has a blue exoskeleton and a single, massive, cross-shaped horn on its head. It has powerful claws on its hands and feet that allow it to grip onto trees.
## Lore
Heracross, the Single Horn Pokémon, is incredibly strong. It can effortlessly throw an object 100 times its own weight. It uses its powerful horn to get under its foe, and then hurls them into the air. It is a gentle Pokémon that loves to feed on sweet tree sap.

# 215. Sneasel
## Look
A bipedal, weasel-like Pokémon with a dark, sleek body. It has sharp, retractable claws, a feather-like crest on its head, and another on its back. It has a cunning and mischievous expression. The Hisuian form is white and purple.
## Lore
Sneasel, the Sharp Claw Pokémon, is a cunning and vicious predator. It uses its sharp claws to scale trees and cliffs. It is known to work in pairs to hunt its prey, with one Sneasel distracting the target while the other attacks. It is a very intelligent and ruthless Pokémon.

# 216. Teddiursa
## Look
A small, bipedal, bear-cub-like Pokémon. It has brown fur and a crescent-shaped, cream-colored marking on its face. It is almost always seen licking its paws, which are soaked in honey. It has a very cute and innocent appearance.
## Lore
Teddiursa, the Little Bear Pokémon, loves to eat honey. Its paws are always soaked in it. It has a unique ability to create its own honey using pollen and the fruit it collects. The crescent moon marking on its forehead is said to glow when it finds a good source of honey.

# 217. Ursaring
## Look
The evolved form of Teddiursa, Ursaring is a large and powerful bipedal bear Pokémon. It has a large, ring-shaped marking on its belly. It has a much more intimidating and powerful presence than its pre-evolution, with large claws and a powerful build.
## Lore
Ursaring, the Hibernator Pokémon, is a skilled climber. It is known to snap trees in half with its powerful arms to mark its territory. It is a very protective Pokémon, especially of its young. It has an excellent sense of smell and can find food that is buried deep underground.

# 218. Slugma
## Look
A Pokémon composed entirely of molten magma. It has a bright, orange, amorphous body with large, yellow eyes. It has no discernible limbs. Its body is constantly bubbling and shifting.
## Lore
Slugma, the Lava Pokémon, is a creature of intense heat. Its body is made of magma, and if it stops moving, its body will cool and harden. To prevent this, it must constantly be in motion. It circulates its magma body to transport oxygen and nutrients. It lives in volcanic areas.

# 219. Magcargo
## Look
The evolved form of Slugma, Magcargo is a snail-like Pokémon whose body is still made of magma, but it now has a shell on its back that is made of cooled, hardened magma. The shell is brittle and can break easily.
## Lore
Magcargo, the Lava Pokémon, has a body temperature of over 18,000 degrees Fahrenheit, which is hotter than the surface of the sun. Its shell is just a thin layer of cooled magma, and it often breaks apart, releasing bursts of intense heat. Water vaporizes on contact with its body.

# 220. Swinub
## Look
A small, quadrupedal Pokémon that resembles a pig or a wild boar. It is covered in a thick, shaggy, brown fur that often obscures its eyes. It has a large, pink, pig-like snout.
## Lore
Swinub, the Pig Pokémon, has an excellent sense of smell. It uses its sensitive nose to search for food, and it is capable of finding hot springs and mushrooms that are buried under ice and snow. It is a very resilient Pokémon that can survive in extremely cold temperatures.

# 221. Piloswine
## Look
The evolved form of Swinub, Piloswine is a larger and more powerful boar-like Pokémon. It is covered in a thick, brown fur, and it has two massive, curved tusks made of ice. Its eyes are still covered by its shaggy fur.
## Lore
Piloswine, the Swine Pokémon, is a creature that is well-adapted to cold climates. Its thick fur protects it from the cold, and its large, flat feet allow it to walk on snow without sinking. It uses its powerful tusks to dig for food and to break through ice. It is a very powerful Pokémon, but it is also known to be quite gentle.

# 222. Corsola
## Look
A small, coral-like Pokémon. It has a pink, spherical body with several coral branches growing from its back. It has a gentle and friendly appearance. The Galarian form is a Ghost-type and is pure white, representing bleached coral.
## Lore
Corsola, the Coral Pokémon, lives in warm, clean seas. They often gather in large groups, forming massive coral reefs that provide a home for smaller Pokémon. The branches on its back are very beautiful and are often sought after as jewelry, which has led to a decline in its population. It is a very fragile Pokémon.

# 223. Remoraid
## Look
A small, fish-like Pokémon with a silver-blue body. It resembles a remora or an archerfish. It has large, cartoonish eyes and a fin on its back that resembles a revolver's hammer.
## Lore
Remoraid, the Jet Pokémon, is known for its ability to shoot powerful jets of water from its mouth with incredible accuracy. It uses this to shoot down flying insect prey from the surface of the water. It is often seen attached to the fins of larger Pokémon like Mantine, feeding on their leftover scraps.

# 224. Octillery
## Look
The evolved form of Remoraid, Octillery is a red, octopus-like Pokémon. It has a large, bulbous head, yellow suction cups on its tentacles, and a determined expression. It has a cannon-like snout.
## Lore
Octillery, the Jet Pokémon, is a creature with a rock-hard head that it uses to defend itself. It has a habit of trapping its foes with its tentacles and then finishing them off with a powerful headbutt. It is a very intelligent Pokémon and is known to be a skilled escape artist.

# 225. Delibird
## Look
A penguin-like Pokémon with a red and white body. It carries a large, sack-like tail that is filled with presents. It has a cheerful and festive appearance.
## Lore
Delibird, the Delivery Pokémon, is a creature that is always carrying food in its tail. It is known to share its food with people and Pokémon who are lost in the mountains. It is a very kind and generous Pokémon. It is said that there was once an explorer who was saved from starvation by a Delibird that shared its food with him.

# 226. Mantine
## Look
A large, manta ray-like Pokémon. It has a blue and white body and a massive wingspan. It is often seen with a Remoraid attached to its wing. It has a gentle and friendly appearance.
## Lore
Mantine, the Kite Pokémon, is a graceful and majestic creature. It can soar high into the air if it builds up enough speed in the water. It is a very docile Pokémon and does not mind having Remoraid attached to it. It is a beautiful sight to see a school of Mantine gliding through the ocean.

# 227. Skarmory
## Look
An avian Pokémon with a body that is completely encased in a suit of steel armor. It has sharp, blade-like wings and a pointed, metallic crest on its head. It has a fierce and intimidating appearance.
## Lore
Skarmory, the Armor Bird Pokémon, has a body that is as hard as steel. Its wings are not just for flying; they are also sharp enough to be used as swords. It is a very territorial Pokémon and will defend its nest with its life. Its feathers are sharp and are often used as weapons by warriors.

# 228. Houndour
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, canine Pokémon with a dark, sleek body. It has a skull-like marking on its forehead and rib-like protrusions on its back. It has a mischievous and aggressive look.
## Lore
Houndour, the Dark Pokémon, is a creature that hunts in packs. They use a variety of cries to communicate with each other and to coordinate their attacks. They are very loyal to their pack and will work together to take down prey much larger than themselves.

# 229. Houndoom
## Look
The evolved form of Houndour, Houndoom is a large and menacing canine Pokémon. It has two large, curved horns on its head, a devil-like tail, and a bony collar around its neck. It is a terrifying and powerful-looking creature.
## Lore
Houndoom, the Dark Pokémon, is a creature of legend. The flames it breathes contain a special toxin that, if you are burned by it, will cause a pain that never goes away. The eerie howls of a Houndoom were once thought to be the call of the grim reaper. It is a very loyal Pokémon to its Trainer.

# 230. Kingdra
## Look
The final evolution of the Horsea line, Kingdra is a large, sea dragon-like Pokémon. It has a blue, streamlined body, a powerful, horse-like snout, and small, fin-like wings. It has a regal and powerful presence.
## Lore
Kingdra, the Dragon Pokémon, is a creature that lives in the deep ocean. It is said that its yawns can create massive underwater currents. It is a very powerful Pokémon and is known to cause tornadoes when it surfaces. It is a very rare sight and is often a subject of myths and legends.

# 231. Phanpy
## Look
A small, blue, elephant-like Pokémon. It has large, floppy ears with red markings, a short, trunk-like snout, and a small, stubby tail. It has a playful and clumsy appearance.
## Lore
Phanpy, the Long Nose Pokémon, is a very strong creature for its small size. It can easily send an adult human flying with a playful bump from its snout. It is very affectionate and loves to be petted. It uses its trunk to spray itself with water to cool down.

# 232. Donphan
## Look
The evolved form of Phanpy, Donphan is a large, powerful, elephant-like Pokémon. Its body is covered in a tough, gray, armor-like hide. It can curl up into a ball and roll at high speeds, much like a tire.
## Lore
Donphan, the Armor Pokémon, has a hide that is as tough as rock. It has a habit of curling up into a ball and rolling down mountains, and it is powerful enough to topple a house. The larger its tusks, the higher its rank in the herd. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 233. Porygon2
## Look
An upgraded version of Porygon, Porygon2 has a smoother, more rounded, bird-like appearance. Its body is red and blue, and it has a more streamlined design. It was created for space exploration.
## Lore
Porygon2, the Virtual Pokémon, is an evolution of Porygon that was created by upgrading its programming. It was given artificial intelligence, which allowed it to learn and develop on its own. It was designed to be able to work in space, but it was unable to fly.

# 234. Stantler
## Look
A quadrupedal, deer-like Pokémon. It has a brown coat and a large, cream-colored tail. Its most notable feature is its two large, dark antlers, which have a mysterious, eye-like pattern in the center.
## Lore
Stantler, the Big Horn Pokémon, has antlers that are a source of mystery. The strange orbs in the center of its antlers can create a bizarre and disorienting illusion by subtly changing the flow of air around them. People who stare at them for too long can lose their sense of direction. The antlers are shed and regrown every year.

# 235. Smeargle
## Look
A bipedal, beagle-like Pokémon. It has a white and brown coat, and its long tail ends in a brush-like tip that secretes a special, paint-like fluid. It has a beret-like crest on its head.
## Lore
Smeargle, the Painter Pokémon, is a creature that expresses itself through art. It uses the fluid from its tail to paint mysterious markings all over its territory. There are over 5,000 different types of markings that have been discovered. It is a very popular Pokémon among artists.

# 236. Tyrogue
## Look
A small, humanoid Pokémon with a pinkish-purple body. It has a very energetic and scrappy appearance. It has three small horns on its head. It is known for its incredible fighting spirit.
## Lore
Tyrogue, the Scuffle Pokémon, is a creature that is always full of energy. It loves to fight and will challenge any foe, no matter how large. It is known to be a very stubborn Pokémon and will not give up, even if it is losing. It must be trained with care to ensure it evolves into the desired form.

# 237. Hitmontop
## Look
An evolution of Tyrogue, Hitmontop is a humanoid Pokémon that moves like a spinning top. It has a brown body and a single, large, metallic spike on its head, which it uses to attack. It has a unique, upside-down fighting style.
## Lore
Hitmontop, the Handstand Pokémon, moves by spinning on the spike on its head. It can deliver a flurry of powerful kicks while spinning. It is a master of a unique form of capoeira-like martial arts. The centrifugal force from its spinning adds destructive power to its attacks.

# 238. Smoochum
## Look
A small, humanoid Pokémon with a female appearance. It is the pre-evolved form of Jynx. It has blonde hair, large, pink lips, and a small, round body. It is always trying to kiss things.
## Lore
Smoochum, the Kiss Pokémon, is a creature that is very sensitive about its appearance. It uses its lips to investigate everything it encounters. It is always checking its reflection to make sure it is not dirty. It loves to be the center of attention.

# 239. Elekid
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon that is the pre-evolved form of Electabuzz. It has a yellow body with black stripes. It has two large antennae on its head that resemble electrical plugs. It is a very energetic and playful Pokémon.
## Lore
Elekid, the Electric Pokémon, is a creature that loves to play. It can generate electricity by rotating its arms, but it is not yet very good at storing it. It is often found near power outlets, trying to feed on electricity. It is a very curious Pokémon.

# 240. Magby
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon that is the pre-evolved form of Magmar. Its body appears to be made of magma. It has a small, duck-like beak and a single, small flame on its back.
## Lore
Magby, the Live Coal Pokémon, is a creature that lives in volcanic craters. Its body temperature is over 1,100 degrees Fahrenheit. Its health can be determined by the color of the flames it breathes; yellow flames mean it is healthy, while orange flames mean it is tired.

# 241. Miltank
## Look
A bipedal, bovine Pokémon with a female appearance. It has a pink and white coat with a black pattern. It has a large udder that produces nutritious milk. It has a gentle and motherly appearance.
## Lore
Miltank, the Milk Cow Pokémon, produces milk that is incredibly nutritious and is said to be able to cure any illness. The flavor of the milk changes depending on the season. It is a very gentle and caring Pokémon and is often found on farms.

# 242. Blissey
## Look
The final evolution of the Chansey line, Blissey is a large, pink, ovoid Pokémon. It has a very kind and gentle appearance. It has small, white, wing-like tufts on its shoulders. It holds a single, lucky egg in its pouch.
## Lore
Blissey, the Happiness Pokémon, has a very compassionate nature. It can sense sadness with its fluffy fur, and it will rush to anyone who is feeling down to share its lucky egg, which is said to bring a smile to anyone who eats it. It is a very rare Pokémon that is a symbol of happiness and good fortune.

# 243. Raikou
## Look
A legendary, quadrupedal, feline Pokémon that resembles a saber-toothed tiger. Its body is yellow with black stripes. It has a long, light blue, ribbon-like tail and a purple, cloud-like mane on its back. It has a fierce and powerful presence.
## Lore
Raikou, the Thunder Pokémon, is one of the three legendary beasts. It is said to embody the speed of lightning. It is a very powerful Pokémon that can shoot bolts of lightning from the rain clouds on its back. It is said to have been born when a bolt of lightning struck a tower. It runs across the land, and its roar sounds like thunder.

# 244. Entei
## Look
A massive, leonine, quadrupedal Pokémon. It has a brown coat, a gray, crown-like crest on its head, and shackle-like protrusions on its legs. It has a large, white, cloud-like mane of smoke on its back. It has a noble and powerful appearance.
## Lore
Entei, the Volcano Pokémon, is one of the three legendary beasts. It is said that a new volcano is born every time it roars. It is a very powerful Pokémon that can unleash massive eruptions of fire. It is said to have been born from the fire that burned down a tower. It is a very majestic and powerful creature.

# 245. Suicune
## Look
A sleek, quadrupedal, mammalian Pokémon with a blue and white coat. It has a large, purple, diamond-shaped crest on its forehead and two long, white, ribbon-like streamers that flow from its back. It moves with a graceful and elegant gait.
## Lore
Suicune, the Aurora Pokémon, is one of the three legendary beasts. It is said to be the reincarnation of the north wind. It has the power to purify polluted water with a single touch. It travels across the land in search of a pure reservoir. It is a very beautiful and elusive Pokémon.

# 246. Larvitar
## Look
A small, reptilian Pokémon with a green, rocky hide. It has a single, small horn on its head and a spike on its tail. It has a perpetually frowning expression. It is born deep underground.
## Lore
Larvitar, the Rock Skin Pokémon, is a creature that is born deep underground. It must eat its way through the soil to reach the surface. It is said that it must eat an entire mountain's worth of soil before it is ready to evolve. It is a very strong Pokémon for its small size.

# 247. Pupitar
## Look
The pupal stage of the Larvitar line, Pupitar is a Pokémon encased in a hard, gray, rock-like shell. It has two triangular openings in its shell for its red eyes. It is very hard and durable.
## Lore
Pupitar, the Hard Shell Pokémon, is a creature that is in a state of transformation. Inside its hard shell, its body is undergoing a massive change. It is very strong and can create a powerful force by compressing the gas inside its body, which it uses to propel itself like a jet.

# 248. Tyranitar
## Look
A massive, bipedal, dinosaur-like Pokémon with a green, armor-like hide. It has a powerful jaw, sharp claws, and a series of spikes on its back. It is a terrifying and destructive-looking creature.
## Lore
Tyranitar, the Armor Pokémon, is an incredibly powerful and destructive creature. It has an insolent nature that makes it not care about others. It is so powerful that it can topple mountains and bury rivers. Maps have to be redrawn after a Tyranitar goes on a rampage. It is a very difficult Pokémon to train.

# 249. Lugia
## Look
A massive, draconic, avian Pokémon with a silver-white body. It has large, hand-like wings, a long, slender neck, and two blue, plate-like structures on its back. It has a majestic and powerful appearance. It is the guardian of the seas.
## Lore
Lugia, the Diving Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to be the guardian of the seas. It is so powerful that a single flap of its wings can create a storm that lasts for 40 days. To avoid causing unintentional destruction, it sleeps in the deep sea trenches. It is a very rare and powerful Pokémon.

# 250. Ho-Oh
## Look
A massive, avian Pokémon with a phoenix-like appearance. Its plumage is a brilliant gold and red, and its wings are tipped with green. It has a long, flowing tail of rainbow-colored feathers. It is a beautiful and majestic creature.
## Lore
Ho-Oh, the Rainbow Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to bring eternal happiness to those who see it. It is said to live at the foot of a rainbow. Its feathers are said to glow in seven colors depending on the angle of the light. It is a very rare and mythical Pokémon.

# 251. Celebi
## Look
A small, green, fairy-like Pokémon. It has large, blue eyes, a pair of translucent wings, and two small antennae. It has a playful and mystical appearance.
## Lore
Celebi, the Time Travel Pokémon, is a mythical creature with the ability to travel through time. It is known as the guardian of the forest. It is said that it only appears in times of peace. A healthy and beautiful forest is a sign that a Celebi lives there. It is a very rare and powerful Pokémon.

# 252. Treecko
## Look
A small, bipedal, reptilian Pokémon with a bright green body and a red underbelly. It has large, yellow eyes with long, thin pupils. Its most notable feature is its large, thick, dark green tail, which is shaped like a fern frond. It has three sharp claws on each hand and foot, and small barbs on the soles of its feet.
## Lore
Treecko is known as the Wood Gecko Pokémon. It is a calm and collected creature that never panics under any circumstances. The small hooks on the soles of its feet allow it to scale sheer vertical walls and hang from ceilings with ease. Its large tail is used to sense humidity and can predict the weather of the next day. It is very protective of its territory and will glare at any intruder.

# 253. Grovyle
## Look
A sleek, bipedal Pokémon that is the evolved form of Treecko. Its body is a lime green, with a darker green underside and a red band across its abdomen. It has three large, sharp leaves protruding from its wrists and a single, massive leaf on its head. Its tail is now split into two long, leaf-like fronds.
## Lore
Grovyle, the Wood Gecko Pokémon, is a master of jungle terrain. The powerful muscles in its thighs make it an incredible jumper, allowing it to leap from branch to branch with astounding agility. The leaves on its body help it to camouflage itself within the dense foliage of the forest. It is a skilled hunter that moves through the treetops so quickly that it is rarely seen.

# 254. Sceptile
## Look
The final evolution of the Treecko line, Sceptile is a tall, formidable, bipedal Pokémon. It has a lean, green body with a red underbelly. Two rows of yellow, seed-like orbs run down its back. Its tail has broadened into a massive, pine-tree-like structure with sharp, leafy edges. Two long, sharp, leaf-blades extend from its arms.
## Lore
Sceptile, the Forest Pokémon, is the undisputed king of the jungle. The seeds growing on its back are filled with nutrients that can revitalize trees. It moves through the forest with unrivaled speed and agility, leaping from branch to branch to get above its foes. The leaves on its arms are as sharp as swords and can slice through thick trees with a single swipe. It carefully regulates its body temperature by basking in the sun.

# 255. Torchic
## Look
A small, avian Pokémon chick covered in fluffy, orange and yellow feathers. It has small, undeveloped wings, three small toes on each foot, and a crest of three feathers on its head. A small flame sac is located inside its belly, which keeps it perpetually warm.
## Lore
Torchic, the Chick Pokémon, is a creature that is very attached to its Trainer. It follows them around like a shadow, pecking at the ground. Inside its belly is a small flame that never goes out. When hugged, it feels like a warm water bottle. If it is attacked, it can shoot fireballs at its foe that reach temperatures of 1,800 degrees Fahrenheit. It dislikes the dark.

# 256. Combusken
## Look
A bipedal, avian Pokémon that resembles a fighting cock. It has a yellow body with orange feathers on its head and legs. Its arms have developed into sharp, clawed wings. It has a sharp beak and a powerful, athletic build. It is always ready for a fight.
## Lore
Combusken, the Young Fowl Pokémon, is a dual-type Fire/Fighting Pokémon. It has incredible kicking power, capable of delivering ten kicks in a single second. It can also shoot intensely hot flames from its beak. Its legs are its primary weapon, and it often lets out sharp cries as it attacks to intimidate its opponents. It has a fighting instinct that drives it to train relentlessly.

# 257. Blaziken
## Look
A tall, humanoid, avian Pokémon with a powerful and athletic build. It has a red body with yellow accents and a crest of feathers on its head that resembles flowing hair. Its wrists and ankles are covered in shaggy, gray fur. It can produce flames from its wrists and ankles.
## Lore
Blaziken, the Blaze Pokémon, is a master of martial arts that uses a unique combination of kicks and punches. Its legs are incredibly strong, allowing it to easily leap over a 30-story building. As it battles, it shoots searing flames from its wrists, which it uses to scorch its opponents. The stronger the foe, the more intensely its flames burn.

# 258. Mudkip
## Look
A small, amphibious, quadrupedal Pokémon. It has a blue body with a lighter blue underbelly. Its most prominent features are the large, orange, spiky gills on its cheeks and the large, fin-like crest on its head. It has a large, fin-like tail.
## Lore
Mudkip, the Mud Fish Pokémon, is a creature that is more at home in the water than on land. The fin on its head is a highly sensitive radar that can detect changes in water currents and air pressure, allowing it to sense what is going on around it without using its eyes. Despite its small size, it is very strong and can crush boulders larger than itself.

# 259. Marshtomp
## Look
The bipedal, evolved form of Mudkip. Its body is a grayish-blue, and it has a more upright posture. The orange gills on its cheeks are now smaller and more defined. It has a sturdy, powerful build and a mischievous expression. Its skin is coated in a sticky film.
## Lore
Marshtomp, the Mud Fish Pokémon, lives in muddy swamps. Its body is covered in a thin, sticky film that allows it to live on land without drying out. It is a powerful swimmer and its hind legs are well-developed, giving it a strong footing even in the thickest mud. It weakens when its skin dries out.

# 260. Swampert
## Look
A massive, bipedal Pokémon with a powerful, bulky build. Its body is a deep blue with a white underbelly. It has large, orange, fan-like gills on its cheeks and a massive, fin-like tail that it can swing with incredible force. It has three large claws on each hand.
## Lore
Swampert, the Mud Fish Pokémon, is a creature of immense physical strength. It is powerful enough to easily drag a boulder weighing over a ton, and it can swim as fast as a jet ski. Its vision is so acute that it can see clearly even in murky, muddy water. It can predict storms by sensing the subtle changes in the sounds of the waves and the wind.

# 261. Poochyena
## Look
A quadrupedal, canine Pokémon that resembles a hyena. It has a shaggy, gray coat, a black face and underbelly, and sharp, yellow eyes. It has a prominent underbite with two visible fangs. It often has a bristly, aggressive posture.
## Lore
Poochyena, the Bite Pokémon, is a tenacious and persistent hunter. It will chase after its prey until the quarry is completely exhausted. It has a very powerful sense of smell that allows it to track its prey for miles. It tries to intimidate its foes by baring its fangs and growling, but it will turn tail and run if its opponent fights back.

# 262. Mightyena
## Look
A large, wolf-like Pokémon with a shaggy, black and gray coat. It has a long snout, piercing red eyes, and a powerful, muscular build. It has a dominant and intimidating presence, characteristic of an alpha predator.
## Lore
Mightyena, the Bite Pokémon, is a creature that lives and hunts in packs. It is a highly disciplined Pokémon that will always obey the orders of a skilled Trainer. It is known for its well-coordinated pack attacks, where it will surround its prey and attack in waves. It is a very loyal and formidable partner.

# 263. Zigzagoon
## Look
A small, raccoon-like Pokémon with a long body. It has shaggy fur that is a mix of brown and cream, arranged in a distinct zigzag pattern. It has a pointed snout and a black, mask-like marking around its eyes. The Galarian form is black and white with a more punk-rock aesthetic.
## Lore
Zigzagoon, the Tiny Raccoon Pokémon, is a creature of boundless curiosity. It is known for walking in a zigzag pattern because it is constantly distracted by everything it sees. It is very good at finding items, and it can often be seen rubbing its bristly fur against trees to mark its territory.

# 264. Linoone
## Look
The evolved form of Zigzagoon, Linoone is a sleek, ferret-like Pokémon. It has a long, streamlined body with a white and brown coat. It is built for incredible speed. Its most notable feature is its ability to run in a straight line at extremely high speeds. The Galarian form is black and white.
## Lore
Linoone, the Rushing Pokémon, is capable of running at speeds of over 60 miles per hour, but it can only run in a straight line. It has difficulty with turns and must come to a complete stop to change direction. It often overshoots its prey due to its incredible speed. It is a very energetic and powerful Pokémon.

# 265. Wurmple
## Look
A small, caterpillar-like Pokémon with a red, spiky body. It has a yellow, horn-like stinger on its head and another on its tail. It has large, yellow eyes and small, suction-cup-like feet.
## Lore
Wurmple, the Worm Pokémon, is a creature that is always eating. It uses the spikes on its rear to strip bark from trees and then sips the sap. The suction pads on its feet allow it to cling to surfaces without slipping, even in the rain. It has a very unpredictable evolutionary path.

# 266. Silcoon
## Look
A cocoon Pokémon that is one of the possible evolutions of Wurmple. It is a round, white cocoon that is anchored to tree branches by a thick layer of silk. It has two red eyes that peer out from within the cocoon.
## Lore
Silcoon, the Cocoon Pokémon, conserves its energy for its impending evolution by remaining almost completely still. It hangs from a tree branch, using the silk it produces to anchor itself. It uses this silk to attach leaves to its body as camouflage. It drinks the dew that collects on its silk.

# 267. Beautifly
## Look
An elegant, butterfly-like Pokémon. It has a black body and large, beautifully patterned wings that are a mix of yellow, red, and blue. It has a long, coiled proboscis that it uses to drink nectar. It has large, green, compound eyes.
## Lore
Beautifly, the Butterfly Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful wings. Despite its lovely appearance, it is a very aggressive Pokémon. It will jab its long, needle-like proboscis into its foes to drain their bodily fluids. It is most active when the weather is warm.

# 268. Cascoon
## Look
