A cocoon Pokémon that is the other possible evolution of Wurmple. It is a purple, cocoon-like shell that is covered in sharp, pointed barbs. It has two menacing, red eyes that are always watching.
## Lore
Cascoon, the Cocoon Pokémon, is a creature that is preparing for evolution. The silk that it wraps around its body is thin but very strong. Inside the cocoon, its body is generating a great deal of heat as it transforms. The barbs on its shell are a defense mechanism to ward off predators.

# 269. Dustox
## Look
A nocturnal, moth-like Pokémon. It has a purple body and large, green wings with a distinctive, eye-like pattern. It has two large, feathery antennae and piercing, yellow eyes.
## Lore
Dustox, the Poison Moth Pokémon, is a creature of the night. It is instinctively drawn to the lights of cities. The dust-like scales on its wings are highly toxic. A single flap of its wings can release a cloud of this poison that can cause severe illness. It uses its antennae as a radar to seek out its prey.

# 270. Lotad
## Look
A small, blue, quadrupedal Pokémon that is a cross between a tadpole and a lily pad. It has a large, green lily pad on its back that is so large it has to walk on all fours to support it. It has a duck-like beak.
## Lore
Lotad, the Water Weed Pokémon, is a creature that lives in clean ponds and lakes. The lily pad on its back is said to be able to carry a small child. It is a very buoyant Pokémon and can often be seen floating on the surface of the water. It can survive for long periods on land as long as its lily pad remains wet.

# 271. Lombre
## Look
The bipedal, evolved form of Lotad. It is covered in a green, moss-like coat. The lily pad on its head now resembles a sombrero. It has a mischievous and playful appearance.
## Lore
Lombre, the Jolly Pokémon, is a nocturnal creature that is most active at dusk. It is a very mischievous Pokémon that loves to play pranks on people, such as pulling on their fishing lines. If it sees an angler, it will tug on their line to trick them.

# 272. Ludicolo
## Look
A tall, bipedal Pokémon that resembles a pineapple and a duck, with a festive, dancer-like appearance. It has a green, poncho-like coat and a large, sombrero-like lily pad on its head. It is almost always seen dancing rhythmically.
## Lore
Ludicolo, the Carefree Pokémon, is a creature that loves to dance. The sound of festive music makes its body fill with energy, and it will begin to dance uncontrollably. It is a very cheerful and optimistic Pokémon, and its dancing is said to bring good luck.

# 273. Seedot
## Look
A small, round Pokémon that resembles an acorn. It has a brown, acorn-like body with a cap on its head. It has two beady, yellow eyes. It often hangs from tree branches by the cap on its head.
## Lore
Seedot, the Acorn Pokémon, attaches itself to a tree branch and absorbs nutrients. It is often mistaken for a real acorn by Pokémon like Pidgey. The more moisture it absorbs, the glossier its body becomes. It enjoys being polished with leaves.

# 274. Nuzleaf
## Look
A bipedal, humanoid Pokémon with a wooden body. It has a long, pointed, Pinocchio-like nose and a single, large leaf on its head. It has a mischievous and cunning appearance.
## Lore
Nuzleaf, the Wily Pokémon, lives deep in forests. It is known for its mischievous nature and its skill at navigating the woods. It uses the leaf on its head as a flute to play an eerie, unsettling melody that strikes fear into the hearts of those who are lost in the forest.

# 275. Shiftry
## Look
A tall, intimidating, humanoid Pokémon with a shaggy, white mane that covers its face. It has a long, pointed nose and glowing red eyes that can be seen through its mane. Its arms are large, fan-like leaves with three sharp claws.
## Lore
Shiftry, the Wicked Pokémon, is a mysterious and fearsome creature that is said to be the guardian of the forest. It is a nocturnal Pokémon that lives in the tops of ancient trees. It can read its opponent's thoughts and anticipate their next move. It is said to be able to create powerful gusts of wind with the fans on its arms.

# 276. Taillow
## Look
A small, swift, avian Pokémon that resembles a swallow. It has a blue and black plumage, a red breast, and a forked tail with two long, red-tipped feathers. It has a determined and gutsy expression.
## Lore
Taillow, the Tiny Swallow Pokémon, is a very courageous and resilient creature. It will stand up to any foe, no matter how large. It is known to be very stubborn and will continue to fight even if it is losing. It can be seen flying in flocks in the spring.

# 277. Swellow
## Look
The evolved form of Taillow, Swellow is a large and elegant swallow-like Pokémon. It has a sleek, blue and white plumage, a sharp, forked tail, and a red crest on its head. It is built for incredible speed and aerial acrobatics.
## Lore
Swellow, the Swallow Pokémon, is a master of the skies. It is known for its beautiful, arcing flight patterns. It is a very clean Pokémon and will take great care of its glossy wings. It is a very fast flyer and can dive at incredible speeds to catch its prey.

# 278. Wingull
## Look
A small, seabird Pokémon that resembles a seagull. It has a white body with blue markings on its long, slender wings. It has a yellow beak with a black tip and orange, webbed feet. It is almost always seen near the ocean.
## Lore
Wingull, the Seagull Pokémon, is a creature of the sea. It makes its nest on steep, seaside cliffs. It can ride on updrafts of wind for long periods without flapping its wings. It uses its long beak to catch prey from the surface of the water. It often carries its prey and valuable items in its beak.

# 279. Pelipper
## Look
The evolved form of Wingull, Pelipper is a large, pelican-like Pokémon. It has a massive, yellow beak and a large throat pouch. Its body is white and blue. It is a capable flyer despite its bulk.
## Lore
Pelipper, the Water Bird Pokémon, is a flying delivery service. It can carry small Pokémon and eggs in its large beak to transport them to safety. It is a very protective Pokémon and will do anything to protect what it is carrying. It skims the surface of the ocean to scoop up water and prey in its beak.

# 280. Ralts
## Look
A small, humanoid Pokémon with a white body. It has a green, bowl-cut-like "helmet" that covers its eyes. It has a small, red horn on its head. It is a very timid and shy creature.
## Lore
Ralts, the Feeling Pokémon, is a very empathetic creature. It can sense the emotions of people and Pokémon using the red horns on its head. It is drawn to people with positive emotions and will hide from those who are angry or hostile. It is a very rare Pokémon that is difficult to find.

# 281. Kirlia
## Look
The evolved form of Ralts, Kirlia is a taller, more elegant, humanoid Pokémon that resembles a ballerina. It has a green, hair-like crest, and its white body is shaped like a tutu. It is almost always seen in a graceful, dance-like pose.
## Lore
Kirlia, the Emotion Pokémon, has the power to see into the future. It is said that when its Trainer is happy, Kirlia will become filled with energy and dance with joy. It uses its psychokinetic powers to distort the air around it, which can create mirages and give it a view of events that have not yet happened.

# 282. Gardevoir
## Look
A tall, elegant, humanoid Pokémon with a graceful, flowing, white gown-like body. It has a green crest that extends down its chest. It has a red, fin-like horn embedded in its chest. It has a serene and powerful appearance.
## Lore
Gardevoir, the Embrace Pokémon, is a fiercely loyal and protective creature. It will do anything to protect its Trainer, even at the cost of its own life. It has the ability to see into the future, and it uses this power to protect its Trainer from harm. It can create a small black hole by using all of its psychic power.

# 283. Surskit
## Look
A small, insectoid Pokémon that resembles a water strider. It has a blue, spherical body, a yellow cap on its head, and four long, spindly legs that allow it to skate across the surface of the water.
## Lore
Surskit, the Pond Skater Pokémon, can walk on water as if it were solid ground. It secretes a special, sweet-smelling syrup from the tip of its head that it uses to lure in prey. It is a very agile Pokémon that can move across the water with great speed.

# 284. Masquerain
## Look
The evolved form of Surskit, Masquerain is a moth-like Pokémon with four large, intimidating, eye-like patterns on its wings. These wings are shaped like antennae and are used to scare off predators. It has a blue body and two actual antennae on its head.
## Lore
Masquerain, the Eyeball Pokémon, uses the large, eye-like patterns on its wings to intimidate its foes. It is a nocturnal Pokémon that is most active on rainy nights. Its wings are not very good for flying, so it relies on the wind to carry it. The eye patterns on its wings can also be used to see in the dark.

# 285. Shroomish
## Look
A small, mushroom-like Pokémon with a round, green body and a spotted, beige cap. It has a perpetually frowning expression and two small, stubby feet.
## Lore
Shroomish, the Mushroom Pokémon, lives in damp, dark forests. It releases a toxic powder from the top of its head to defend itself. This powder can cause sickness and paralysis. It is a very timid Pokémon that prefers to stay still and avoid conflict.

# 286. Breloom
## Look
A bipedal, kangaroo-like Pokémon with a mushroom cap for a head. It has a green body, a long tail, and two powerful arms with extendable claws. It has a unique, boxing-like fighting style.
## Lore
Breloom, the Mushroom Pokémon, is a master of a unique form of boxing that uses its extendable arms to deliver a flurry of quick, powerful punches. The seeds on its tail are poisonous. It is a very agile Pokémon and can move with the grace of a professional boxer.

# 287. Slakoth
## Look
A sloth-like Pokémon with a shaggy, brown coat. It has a perpetually drowsy and lazy expression. It is almost always seen lounging about, and it moves very slowly.
## Lore
Slakoth, the Slacker Pokémon, is a creature that is incredibly lazy. It is said that its heart only beats once a minute. It spends over 20 hours a day sleeping. It is so lethargic that it doesn't even bother to eat very often. The only time it moves is to find a new place to sleep.

# 288. Vigoroth
## Look
The evolved form of Slakoth, Vigoroth is a hyperactive, primate-like Pokémon. It has a white, shaggy coat and a red, crest-like tuft of hair on its head. It is always moving and cannot stay still for even a moment.
## Lore
Vigoroth, the Wild Monkey Pokémon, is the polar opposite of its pre-evolution. It is in a constant state of agitation and cannot stay still. The stress of being unable to relax causes its heart to beat at an incredibly fast rate. It will go on a rampage if it cannot find something to do.

# 289. Slaking
## Look
The final evolution, Slaking is a massive, gorilla-like Pokémon that is almost always seen lounging on its side. It has a massive, powerful build, but it is incredibly lazy. It has a pig-like snout and a tuft of white fur around its neck.
## Lore
Slaking, the Lazy Pokémon, is the laziest of all Pokémon. It spends all day lying down and will only move if it has to. It is said to be saving up its energy for a powerful attack. It can unleash an incredible amount of power in a single burst, but it will then immediately become lazy again.

# 290. Nincada
## Look
A small, cicada-like, insectoid Pokémon. It has a white body, small, green wings, and two large, red eyes. It has two long antennae and is almost blind.
## Lore
Nincada, the Trainee Pokémon, is a creature that lives underground for most of its life. It uses its claws to dig through the roots of trees and absorb nutrients. It is a very patient Pokémon and can wait for years for the right moment to emerge.

# 291. Ninjask
## Look
The evolved form of Nincada, Ninjask is a cicada-like Pokémon that is known for its incredible speed. It has a black and yellow body, two pairs of wings that are constantly vibrating, and large, red, compound eyes.
## Lore
Ninjask, the Ninja Pokémon, moves so fast that it can be difficult to see. It is said that hearing its cry can cause a headache that won't go away. It is a very difficult Pokémon to train due to its incredible speed and its tendency to disobey orders.

# 292. Shedinja
## Look
A mysterious Pokémon that is the shed exoskeleton of a Nincada that has evolved into a Ninjask. It is a hollow, bug-like shell with two wings and a halo-like ring floating above its head. It does not move or breathe.
## Lore
Shedinja, the Shed Pokémon, is a bizarre and ghostly creature. It is the empty shell of a Nincada that has come to life. It is said that if you look into the hole on its back, it will steal your spirit. It is immune to most attacks, but it is also incredibly fragile and can be defeated by a single hit that does damage.

# 293. Whismur
## Look
A small, pink, rabbit-like Pokémon. It has two large, floppy ears with yellow, plus-shaped markings, and its eyes are also shaped like plus signs. It has a small, circular mouth and is almost always whispering.
## Lore
Whismur, the Whisper Pokémon, is a very timid and quiet creature. It is usually whispering, but when it is startled, it can let out a cry that is as loud as a jet engine. It is so loud that it can be dangerous to be near it when it cries. It is frightened by its own loud voice.

# 294. Loudred
## Look
The evolved form of Whismur, Loudred is a bipedal Pokémon with a large, cavernous mouth. It has two large, speaker-like ears. Its body is a mix of purple and yellow.
## Lore
Loudred, the Big Voice Pokémon, has a voice that is powerful enough to topple a house. It uses its large, round ears as speakers to amplify the sound. It can also use its voice to create powerful shockwaves. It is a very boisterous and energetic Pokémon.

# 295. Exploud
## Look
The final evolution, Exploud is a massive, bipedal Pokémon with a series of sound-emitting tubes all over its body. Its head is a massive, speaker-like organ. It has a powerful and intimidating presence.
## Lore
Exploud, the Loud Noise Pokémon, can create sounds that are powerful enough to trigger earthquakes. The tubes on its body can generate a variety of sounds, from a deafening roar to a gentle whistle. It can communicate with others over a distance of six miles.

# 296. Makuhita
## Look
A small, bipedal, humanoid Pokémon that resembles a sumo wrestler. It has a yellow, top-knot-like crest on its head and a determined expression. It is always ready to train and fight.
## Lore
Makuhita, the Guts Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible spirit and resilience. It will never give up, no matter how many times it is knocked down. It is a very dedicated Pokémon that trains relentlessly to become stronger. It is said to have the spirit of a true fighter.

# 297. Hariyama
## Look
The evolved form of Makuhita, Hariyama is a massive and powerful sumo wrestler Pokémon. It has a huge, powerful build and massive, open hands. It has a tough and determined look.
## Lore
Hariyama, the Arm Thrust Pokémon, is a master of sumo wrestling techniques. It can send a 10-ton truck flying with a single slap from its open hand. It is known to stand on train tracks and challenge oncoming trains to a test of strength. It is a very powerful and honorable Pokémon.

# 298. Azurill
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Marill, Azurill is a small, round, blue Pokémon. It has a very large, bouncy tail that is almost as big as its body. It has a happy and playful appearance.
## Lore
Azurill, the Polka Dot Pokémon, has a tail that is filled with nutrients. It can be seen spinning its tail around and then throwing it, which it does to build up its muscles. It is a very playful Pokémon that loves to bounce on its large tail.

# 299. Nosepass
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a large, blue, stone moai statue head. It has a massive, red, magnetic nose that always points north. It has two small, blocky feet that it shuffles around on.
## Lore
Nosepass, the Compass Pokémon, has a nose that is a powerful magnet. It is always pointing north, which makes it an excellent guide for travelers. It is said that two Nosepass will repel each other if they get too close. It is a very sturdy Pokémon that is difficult to damage.

# 300. Skitty
## Look
A small, pink, feline Pokémon with a very cute and playful appearance. Its tail is large and fluffy, with a pin-cushion-like section that it chases after. It has a charming and whimsical expression.
## Lore
Skitty, the Kitten Pokémon, is a creature that is fascinated by moving objects. It is known to chase its own tail in a circle until it becomes dizzy. It is a very popular pet due to its cute appearance and its playful nature. It is said that it is very difficult to earn the trust of a Skitty.

# 301. Delcatty
## Look
The evolved form of Skitty, Delcatty is a graceful and elegant feline Pokémon. It has a slender body and a purple, scarf-like ruff around its neck. It has a very stylish and sophisticated appearance.
## Lore
Delcatty, the Prim Pokémon, is a creature that prefers to live a life of leisure. It is not a very good fighter and will avoid conflict if it can. It is a very picky Pokémon and will only eat at certain times and in certain places. It is a very popular Pokémon among female Trainers.

# 302. Sableye
## Look
A small, bipedal, imp-like Pokémon with a dark purple body. Its most notable feature is its eyes, which are made of faceted, crystalline gems. It has sharp claws and a mischievous grin filled with pointed teeth.
## Lore
Sableye, the Darkness Pokémon, is a creature that lives in deep, dark caves. Its diet consists of raw gemstones, which it digs up with its sharp claws. The gems that it eats eventually surface on its body, forming its crystalline eyes. It is a very mischievous Pokémon that enjoys playing pranks on people.

# 303. Mawile
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon with a demure and deceptive appearance. From the front, it looks small and cute. However, on the back of its head is a massive, carnivorous, steel jaw with sharp teeth.
## Lore
Mawile, the Deceiver Pokémon, uses its cute appearance to lure in its prey. Once the prey is close enough, it will turn around and attack with the massive steel jaws on its head. These jaws were once horns that have transformed into a powerful weapon.

# 304. Aron
## Look
A small, quadrupedal Pokémon with a body made of steel. It has a large, steel helmet-like head with two blue eyes peering out. It has a sturdy and resilient appearance.
## Lore
Aron, the Iron Armor Pokémon, is a creature that feeds on iron ore. It is known to eat entire bridges and railroad tracks. It uses the iron it eats to build its steel body. It is a very strong Pokémon for its small size.

# 305. Lairon
## Look
The evolved form of Aron, Lairon is a larger and more powerful steel-armored Pokémon. It has a more complex and detailed suit of armor. It is known to be very territorial.
## Lore
Lairon, the Iron Armor Pokémon, is a creature that is constantly fighting for territory. It is known to smash into mountainsides to claim them as its own. It is a very aggressive Pokémon and will attack anything that enters its territory.

# 306. Aggron
## Look
The final evolution, Aggron is a massive, bipedal, dinosaur-like Pokémon with a body that is completely encased in a suit of thick, steel armor. It has a powerful horn on its nose and a series of sharp, steel plates on its back. It is a terrifying and powerful-looking creature.
## Lore
Aggron, the Iron Armor Pokémon, is a creature that claims an entire mountain as its own territory. It is very protective of its environment and will go on a rampage if its mountain is damaged. It is known to restore the mountain to its original state after a disaster, planting trees and carrying soil.

# 307. Meditite
## Look
A small, humanoid Pokémon with a blue, onion-shaped head. It is always seen in a meditative, yoga-like pose. It has a calm and focused expression.
## Lore
Meditite, the Meditate Pokémon, is a creature that is dedicated to its training. It spends all day meditating to heighten its spiritual energy. It is so focused that it can go for weeks without eating. It is a very disciplined and powerful Pokémon.

# 308. Medicham
## Look
The evolved form of Meditite, Medicham is a taller, more elegant, humanoid Pokémon. It has a headdress-like crest on its head and large, baggy pants. It moves with a graceful, dance-like motion.
## Lore
Medicham, the Meditate Pokémon, has gained powerful psychokinetic powers through its intense meditation. It can use these powers to predict its opponent's moves. It is a master of a unique form of yoga-like martial arts that combines physical and psychic attacks.

# 309. Electrike
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, canine Pokémon with green fur. It has a large, crest-like fin on its head. It is always crackling with static electricity.
## Lore
Electrike, the Lightning Pokémon, stores electricity in its long fur. It can run faster than the human eye can see by stimulating its leg muscles with electricity. It is a very energetic and playful Pokémon.

# 310. Manectric
## Look
The evolved form of Electrike, Manectric is a larger, more sleek, canine Pokémon. It has a blue and yellow coat and a massive, lightning-bolt-shaped, yellow mane. It is built for incredible speed.
## Lore
Manectric, the Discharge Pokémon, is a creature that is constantly discharging electricity from its mane. It is so fast that it is said to be as fast as lightning. It is known to create thunderclouds and is rarely seen by people.

# 311. Plusle
## Look
A small, bipedal, rodent-like Pokémon with a cream-colored body and red markings. It has two long ears with red plus signs on them, and two red plus signs on its cheeks. It is almost always seen with its partner, Minun.
## Lore
Plusle, the Cheering Pokémon, is a creature that loves to cheer on its partners in battle. It can create sparks and pompoms by shorting out the electricity in its body. It is a very supportive and energetic Pokémon.

# 312. Minun
## Look
A small, bipedal, rodent-like Pokémon with a cream-colored body and blue markings. It has two long ears with blue minus signs on them, and two blue minus signs on its cheeks. It is almost always seen with its partner, Plusle.
## Lore
Minun, the Cheering Pokémon, is a creature that is the counterpart to Plusle. It also loves to cheer on its partners and can create sparks. It is known to be more defensive than Plusle.

# 313. Volbeat
## Look
A bipedal, firefly-like Pokémon with a male appearance. It has a blue and black body and a large, glowing tail. It has a mischievous and charming expression.
## Lore
Volbeat, the Firefly Pokémon, is a creature that uses its glowing tail to communicate with others. It can create complex patterns in the night sky with its light. It is known to be a very romantic Pokémon and will perform elaborate light shows to attract a mate.

# 314. Illumise
## Look
A bipedal, firefly-like Pokémon with a female appearance. It has a blue and black body and a sweet, charming expression. It is almost always seen with Volbeat.
## Lore
Illumise, the Firefly Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its sweet scent. It uses this scent to guide Volbeat in their aerial light shows. It is a very graceful and elegant Pokémon.

# 315. Roselia
## Look
A small, bipedal, plant-like Pokémon. It has a green body and two large, rose-like flowers for hands, one red and one blue. It has a leaf-like crest on its head.
## Lore
Roselia, the Thorn Pokémon, is a creature whose flowers are filled with a potent poison. The aroma of the flowers can be either relaxing or deadly, depending on the poison's concentration. It is a very beautiful but dangerous Pokémon.

# 316. Gulpin
## Look
A small, amorphous, green Pokémon with a massive, gaping mouth that is much larger than its body. It has a single, yellow, feather-like crest on its head.
## Lore
Gulpin, the Stomach Pokémon, is a creature that is basically a living stomach. It can swallow objects as large as itself whole. Its body is filled with a special acid that can dissolve anything. It is a very strange and mysterious Pokémon.

# 317. Swalot
## Look
The evolved form of Gulpin, Swalot is a massive, amorphous, purple Pokémon. Its mouth is even larger than Gulpin's, and it has long, whisker-like tendrils.
## Lore
Swalot, the Poison Bag Pokémon, is a creature that can swallow anything whole. It is known to be able to swallow a car tire. It is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon. It can spray a special, poisonous liquid from its pores to immobilize its foes.

# 318. Carvanha
## Look
A small, piranha-like Pokémon with a blue and red body. It has a massive, powerful jaw filled with sharp teeth. It is a very aggressive and ferocious-looking creature.
## Lore
Carvanha, the Savage Pokémon, is a creature that hunts in schools. They are known to be very territorial and will attack any boat that enters their territory. They can strip a boat to its frame in a matter of minutes.

# 319. Sharpedo
## Look
The evolved form of Carvanha, Sharpedo is a torpedo-shaped Pokémon that resembles a shark with its back half cut off. It has a powerful jaw, a dorsal fin, and a star-shaped marking on its snout.
## Lore
Sharpedo, the Brutal Pokémon, is known as the "Bully of the Sea." It can swim at speeds of over 75 miles per hour by shooting water out of its rear. It is a very dangerous and aggressive Pokémon.

# 320. Wailmer
## Look
A large, spherical, whale-like Pokémon. It has a blue and white body and a cheerful, friendly expression. It can be seen spouting water from the blowholes on its head.
## Lore
Wailmer, the Ball Whale Pokémon, is a creature that loves to play. It can store a massive amount of water in its body, which it uses to bounce on the ground like a ball. It is a very friendly and playful Pokémon.

# 321. Wailord
## Look
The evolved form of Wailmer, Wailord is the largest of all Pokémon. It is a massive, whale-like creature with a blue and white body. It is so large that it is difficult to see its entire body at once.
## Lore
Wailord, the Float Whale Pokémon, is the largest Pokémon in the world. It is known to hunt in large groups, herding its prey into a tight ball before swallowing them whole. It is a very majestic and powerful creature.

# 322. Numel
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, camel-like Pokémon. It has a green, volcano-like hump on its back. It has a dopey and placid expression.
## Lore
Numel, the Numb Pokémon, is a creature that is very slow and dull-witted. It can't feel pain. The hump on its back is filled with magma that can reach temperatures of over 2,200 degrees Fahrenheit. It is a very resilient Pokémon.

# 323. Camerupt
## Look
The evolved form of Numel, Camerupt is a large, camel-like Pokémon with two volcano-like humps on its back. It has a red and orange coat and a rugged, powerful build.
## Lore
Camerupt, the Eruption Pokémon, has two active volcanoes on its back. These volcanoes can erupt with incredible force, spewing molten rock and ash. It is said that a massive eruption occurs every 10 years.

# 324. Torkoal
## Look
A large, tortoise-like Pokémon with a black, coal-like shell. It is always seen with smoke billowing from its nostrils and the holes in its shell.
## Lore
Torkoal, the Coal Pokémon, is a creature that uses coal as its source of energy. It is known to dig for coal in mountains. If it is attacked, it can release a thick, black smoke from its shell to blind its foes. It has a very loud, train-like cry.

# 325. Spoink
## Look
A small, pig-like Pokémon that is always bouncing on its spring-like tail. It has a gray body and a pink, pearl-like orb on its head.
## Lore
Spoink, the Bounce Pokémon, is a creature that must always be bouncing. The shock of its bouncing keeps its heart beating. If it stops bouncing, its heart will stop. The pearl on its head is used to amplify its psychic powers.

# 326. Grumpig
## Look
The evolved form of Spoink, Grumpig is a bipedal, pig-like Pokémon. It has a purple body and two black pearls on its forehead. It moves in a strange, dance-like manner.
## Lore
Grumpig, the Manipulate Pokémon, uses the black pearls on its head to control its psychic powers. It can use these powers to control the minds of its foes. The strange dance it performs is said to increase its psychic abilities.

# 327. Spinda
## Look
A bipedal, panda-like Pokémon with a dizzy and perpetually confused expression. It has a cream-colored coat with red, spiral-like spots. The pattern of its spots is unique to each individual.
## Lore
Spinda, the Spot Panda Pokémon, is a creature that is always dizzy. It moves with a staggering, unsteady gait. It is said that no two Spinda have the exact same pattern of spots. There are over four billion different patterns that have been discovered.

# 328. Trapinch
## Look
A small, insectoid Pokémon with a large, orange head and a massive, powerful jaw. It has a small, green body and is almost all head.
## Lore
Trapinch, the Ant Pit Pokémon, is a creature that lives in deserts. It builds a large, cone-shaped pit in the sand to trap its prey. It waits at the bottom of the pit for unsuspecting prey to fall in. Its jaw is powerful enough to crush boulders.

# 329. Vibrava
## Look
The evolved form of Trapinch, Vibrava is a dragonfly-like Pokémon. It has a green body, two pairs of wings that are constantly vibrating, and large, green, compound eyes.
## Lore
Vibrava, the Vibration Pokémon, can create powerful ultrasonic waves by flapping its wings at high speed. These waves can cause headaches and are used to knock out its prey. It is a very agile and fast flyer.

# 330. Flygon
## Look
The final evolution, Flygon is a draconic, insectoid Pokémon. It has a green body, two large, red, goggle-like eye covers, and two pairs of wings that create a sandstorm when they are flapped.
## Lore
Flygon, the Mystic Pokémon, is known as the "Desert Spirit." It is said that the flapping of its wings sounds like a beautiful song. It can whip up massive sandstorms to hide itself from its foes. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 331. Cacnea
## Look
A small, bipedal, cactus-like Pokémon. It has a green, spherical body with yellow, diamond-shaped markings. It has two arm-like appendages and a yellow flower blooming on its head.
## Lore
Cacnea, the Cactus Pokémon, lives in harsh, arid deserts. It can survive for 30 days without water by storing it in its body. The flower on its head releases a strong, pleasant aroma to attract prey.

# 332. Cacturne
## Look
The evolved form of Cacnea, Cacturne is a humanoid, scarecrow-like cactus Pokémon. It has a green body, a hat-like crest on its head, and a series of sharp, diamond-shaped spikes all over its body. It has a menacing, fixed grin.
## Lore
Cacturne, the Scarecrow Pokémon, is a nocturnal creature that is most active at night. It is known to stand motionless in the desert, waiting for travelers to become exhausted by the heat. It will then follow them, waiting for the right moment to strike.

# 333. Swablu
## Look
A small, blue, avian Pokémon with two large, fluffy, cotton-like wings. It has a small, white crest on its head and a cheerful expression.
## Lore
Swablu, the Cotton Bird Pokémon, is a creature that loves to be clean. It is often seen preening its cotton-like wings. If its wings become dirty, it will wash them in a spring. It is a very friendly and social Pokémon.

# 334. Altaria
## Look
The evolved form of Swablu, Altaria is a majestic, draconic, avian Pokémon. Its body is a deep blue, and it is enveloped in a massive, cloud-like plume of cottony feathers. It has a long, flowing tail and a serene expression.
## Lore
Altaria, the Humming Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful, soprano-like singing voice. It is a very gentle and caring Pokémon and will often care for injured people and Pokémon. It can be seen soaring gracefully through the sky, humming a beautiful melody.

# 335. Zangoose
## Look
A bipedal, feline Pokémon that resembles a mongoose. It has a white coat with red, scar-like markings. It has long, sharp claws and a fierce, determined expression. It is the rival of Seviper.
## Lore
Zangoose, the Cat Ferret Pokémon, is a creature that has been locked in a bitter rivalry with Seviper for generations. It is very agile and can dodge most attacks with ease. Its sharp claws are its primary weapon.

# 336. Seviper
## Look
A serpentine Pokémon with a black body and a series of purple and yellow markings. It has two large, red, blade-like fangs and a sword-like tail. It is the rival of Zangoose.
## Lore
Seviper, the Fang Snake Pokémon, is a creature that has a long-standing feud with Zangoose. The blade on its tail is not just for show; it is a deadly weapon that can secrete a powerful venom. It is a very cunning and ruthless Pokémon.

# 337. Lunatone
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a crescent moon made of rock. It has a beige, rocky body and a single, large, red eye. It is always seen floating in the air.
## Lore
Lunatone, the Meteorite Pokémon, is a creature that was discovered at the site of a meteor crash. It is said that its power is linked to the phases of the moon, and it is most powerful during the full moon. The look in its red eyes can cause its foes to become terrified.

# 338. Solrock
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a sun made of rock. It has a round, orange, rocky body with a series of spikes radiating from it. It has two large, red eyes.
## Lore
Solrock, the Meteorite Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have come from space. It gets its power from the sun and is most powerful during the day. It can release an intense heat that can be felt from a great distance. It can also read the minds of others.

# 339. Barboach
## Look
A small, catfish-like Pokémon with a blue and gray body. It has a pair of long, sensitive whiskers that it uses to explore its surroundings. It is almost always covered in a slimy film.
## Lore
Barboach, the Whiskers Pokémon, is a creature that lives in muddy riverbeds. It uses its sensitive whiskers to search for food in the mud. The slimy film on its body allows it to escape from the grip of its foes.

# 340. Whiscash
## Look
The evolved form of Barboach, Whiscash is a large, catfish-like Pokémon. It has a blue and yellow body and a massive, gaping mouth. It has a W-shaped marking on its forehead.
## Lore
Whiscash, the Whiskers Pokémon, is a creature that is very protective of its territory. It is known to cause massive earthquakes by thrashing about. It is said that it can predict when a real earthquake is coming.

# 341. Corphish
## Look
A small, crustacean Pokémon that resembles a crayfish. It has a red and white body and two large, powerful pincers. It has a tough and scrappy appearance.
## Lore
Corphish, the Ruffian Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its hardy and adaptable nature. It can survive in any body of water, no matter how polluted. It is a very aggressive Pokémon and will not hesitate to attack anything that comes near it.

# 342. Crawdaunt
## Look
The evolved form of Corphish, Crawdaunt is a larger and more menacing crustacean Pokémon. It has a massive, powerful pincer that can crush rock. It has a star-shaped marking on its forehead and a very aggressive look.
## Lore
Crawdaunt, the Rogue Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its extremely violent nature. It is a very difficult Pokémon to train and is known to disobey its Trainer. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 343. Baltoy
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a clay spinning top. It has a brown, clay-like body with red markings. It has a single, small foot that it balances on. It is always spinning.
## Lore
Baltoy, the Clay Doll Pokémon, is a mysterious creature that was first discovered in ancient ruins. It moves by spinning on its single foot. It is said that they were first created by ancient people out of clay and brought to life by a mysterious power.

# 344. Claydol
## Look
The evolved form of Baltoy, Claydol is a large, black, clay doll-like Pokémon with multiple eyes. It has two arms that are detached from its body, which it controls with its psychic powers.
## Lore
Claydol, the Clay Doll Pokémon, is a creature that was created by ancient humans. It is said that it was brought to life by a strange light. It can fire powerful beams from both of its hands. It is a very mysterious and powerful Pokémon.

# 345. Lileep
## Look
A prehistoric, plant-like Pokémon that resembles a sea lily. It has a purple, bud-like head with two large, yellow eyes. It has several pink, petal-like tentacles that it uses to capture its prey. It is anchored to the seabed by a root-like structure.
## Lore
Lileep, the Sea Lily Pokémon, is a creature that was resurrected from a fossil. It was thought to be extinct. It lives on the seabed and uses its tentacles to ensnare its prey. It is a very patient and cunning predator.

# 346. Cradily
## Look
The evolved form of Lileep, Cradily is a larger and more formidable sea lily Pokémon. It has a long, flexible neck and a large, saucer-shaped head with eight tentacles. It uses its body as a heavy anchor to keep from being washed away by strong currents.
## Lore
Cradily, the Barnacle Pokémon, is a creature that lives in the shallow seas of warm climates. It is known to come up onto land during low tide to search for food. It can spray a powerful, corrosive acid from its tentacles to melt down its prey.

# 347. Anorith
## Look
A prehistoric, arthropod-like Pokémon that resembles an anomalocaris. It has a gray, armored body, two large, fan-like appendages on its head, and eight feathery, wing-like fins on its sides.
## Lore
Anorith, the Old Shrimp Pokémon, is a creature that was resurrected from a fossil. It was an ancient predator that lived in the warm seas of primordial times. It used its two large claws to capture its prey. It is the ancestor of all modern insect Pokémon.

# 348. Armaldo
## Look
The evolved form of Anorith, Armaldo is a bipedal, armored, arthropod Pokémon. It has a tough, shell-like hide, two massive, powerful claws, and two large, feathery wings on its back.
## Lore
Armaldo, the Plate Pokémon, is a creature that is protected by a suit of thick, durable armor. It is a powerful predator that can extend and retract its massive claws to strike at its prey. It is a very powerful and formidable fighter.

# 349. Feebas
## Look
A fish Pokémon with a shabby and unimpressive appearance. It has a brown, mottled body with several dark spots. It has large, vacant, blue eyes and prominent, pink lips. Its fins are tattered and torn.
## Lore
Feebas, the Fish Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its ragged and unsightly appearance. It is a very resilient Pokémon and can survive in any body of water, no matter how polluted. It is often ignored by Trainers due to its appearance, but it has a secret potential.

# 350. Milotic
## Look
The evolved form of Feebas, Milotic is a long, serpentine, aquatic Pokémon of incredible beauty. Its body is a creamy white, and its tail is a magnificent, fan-like structure with a pattern of blue, pink, and black scales that resemble an eye. It has long, flowing, pink, hair-like fins on its head.
## Lore
Milotic, the Tender Pokémon, is said to be the most beautiful of all Pokémon. It has the power to calm feelings of anger and hostility, and it is known to appear in times of conflict to quell the fighting. It is a very rare and majestic creature.

# 351. Castform
## Look
A small, artificial Pokémon with a gray, cloud-like body. It has a simple, cheerful face and a small tuft on its head. Its form changes depending on the weather.
## Lore
Castform, the Weather Pokémon, is a creature that was created by scientists to help predict the weather. It has the unique ability to change its form and type depending on the weather conditions. It can become a Fire-type in the sun, a Water-type in the rain, and an Ice-type in the snow.

# 352. Kecleon
## Look
A bipedal, chameleon-like Pokémon. It has a green body with a distinctive, red, zigzagging stripe across its belly. It has a long, curled tail and the ability to change the color of its skin to blend in with its surroundings.
## Lore
Kecleon, the Color Swap Pokémon, is a master of camouflage. It can change the color of its body to match its surroundings perfectly, making it almost invisible. The only part of its body that it cannot change the color of is the red stripe on its belly. It is a very cunning and tricky Pokémon.

# 353. Shuppet
## Look
A small, ghost-like Pokémon that resembles a puppet covered in a gray cloth. It has a horn-like protrusion on its head and two large, round eyes. It is almost always seen with its tongue sticking out.
## Lore
Shuppet, the Puppet Pokémon, is a creature that is drawn to feelings of jealousy and vengeance. It is said to feed on these negative emotions. It is a nocturnal Pokémon that is most active at night.

# 354. Banette
## Look
The evolved form of Shuppet, Banette is a marionette-like Pokémon that has come to life. It has a gray, doll-like body with a zipper for a mouth. It is said to be possessed by a powerful grudge.
## Lore
Banette, the Marionette Pokémon, is a doll that was thrown away and has come to life, possessed by a powerful hatred for the child that abandoned it. It is said that if it opens its zipper mouth, its cursed energy will escape. It is a very vengeful and dangerous Pokémon.

# 355. Duskull
## Look
A ghostly Pokémon that resembles a grim reaper. It has a black, hooded body with a single, large, red eye. It has two small, spectral hands that are detached from its body.
## Lore
Duskull, the Requiem Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be able to pass through solid walls. It is a nocturnal Pokémon that is most active at night. It is said that it will relentlessly pursue its target until the sun comes up. It is a very persistent and terrifying Pokémon.

# 356. Dusclops
## Look
The evolved form of Duskull, Dusclops is a larger and more imposing ghostly Pokémon. Its body is said to be hollow, and it can absorb anything into it like a black hole. It has a single, red eye and two large, spectral hands.
## Lore
Dusclops, the Beckon Pokémon, is a creature that is feared by many. Its body is a void, and it is said that anything that is absorbed into it will never be seen again. It uses its single eye to hypnotize its foes and then draws them into its hollow body.

# 357. Tropius
## Look
A massive, sauropod-like Pokémon with a long neck and a body that is a mix of a dinosaur and a tropical tree. It has large, leaf-like wings and a bunch of bananas growing from its chin.
## Lore
Tropius, the Fruit Pokémon, is a creature that loves to eat fruit. The fruit that grows on its neck is a delicacy and is very popular among children. It can fly by flapping its large, leaf-like wings. It is a very gentle and kind Pokémon.

# 358. Chimecho
## Look
A small, wind chime-like Pokémon. It has a blue, spherical body with a long, tassel-like tail. It has a suction cup on its head that it uses to hang from tree branches.
## Lore
Chimecho, the Wind Chime Pokémon, is a creature that communicates by making beautiful, chiming sounds. It can create a variety of different sounds by vibrating its body. It is a very rare Pokémon and is difficult to find.

# 359. Absol
## Look
A quadrupedal, mammalian Pokémon with a dark blue and white coat. It has a single, sickle-shaped horn on its head and a scythe-like tail. It has a majestic and foreboding appearance.
## Lore
Absol, the Disaster Pokémon, is a creature that is often misunderstood. It has the ability to sense impending natural disasters, and it will appear before people to warn them. However, because of its appearance, it is often blamed for the disasters it predicts.

# 360. Wynaut
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Wobbuffet, Wynaut is a small, blue Pokémon with a perpetually happy and cheerful expression. It has two long, ear-like appendages on its head.
## Lore
Wynaut, the Bright Pokémon, is a creature that is always smiling. They often travel in herds and are known to test their endurance by squeezing up against each other. It is a very social and friendly Pokémon.

# 361. Snorunt
## Look
A small, cone-shaped Pokémon that is always covered in a layer of snow. It has a brown, coat-like body and two small, blue eyes. It has a perpetually shivering appearance.
## Lore
Snorunt, the Snow Hat Pokémon, is a creature that lives in cold, snowy regions. It is said that a home that is visited by a Snorunt will prosper for many generations. It is a very hardy Pokémon and can survive in temperatures as low as -150 degrees Fahrenheit.

# 362. Glalie
## Look
An evolution of Snorunt, Glalie is a Pokémon that resembles a floating, demonic hockey mask made of ice. It has two large, horns on its head and a menacing grin.
## Lore
Glalie, the Face Pokémon, has a body that is made of solid rock, which it covers in a thick layer of ice. It has the ability to freeze moisture in the air to create this ice armor. It can also freeze its foes solid with its icy breath.

# 363. Spheal
## Look
A small, round, seal-like Pokémon. It has a blue body with a white, spotted pattern. It has a cheerful and playful appearance.
## Lore
Spheal, the Clap Pokémon, is a creature that moves by rolling its spherical body. It is often seen clapping its fins together when it is happy. It is a very playful and social Pokémon.

# 364. Sealeo
## Look
The evolved form of Spheal, Sealeo is a larger, sea lion-like Pokémon. It has a thick, white mustache and a powerful, muscular build.
## Lore
Sealeo, the Ball Roll Pokémon, has a habit of balancing objects on its nose. It is known to spin a Spheal on its nose for fun. It is a very strong Pokémon and can easily break through thick sheets of ice.

# 365. Walrein
## Look
The final evolution, Walrein is a massive, walrus-like Pokémon. It has two huge, sharp tusks and a thick, blubbery body. It is a powerful and intimidating-looking creature.
## Lore
Walrein, the Ice Break Pokémon, is a creature that is protected by a thick layer of blubber that can repel any attack. Its massive tusks can shatter a 10-ton block of ice with a single blow. It is a very powerful and territorial Pokémon.

# 366. Clamperl
## Look
A bivalve Pokémon that is encased in a hard, blue shell. Its true body is a pink, pearl-like creature with a single, large eye.
## Lore
Clamperl, the Bivalve Pokémon, is a creature that is protected by a shell that is as hard as steel. The pearl it creates is said to be incredibly beautiful and is sought after by many. It is said that the pearl can amplify psychic powers.

# 367. Huntail
## Look
An evolution of Clamperl, Huntail is a long, serpentine, aquatic Pokémon. It has a blue and orange body and a fish-like tail that it uses to lure in its prey.
## Lore
Huntail, the Deep Sea Pokémon, is a creature that lives in the deep ocean. It uses its fish-shaped tail to attract prey and then swallows it whole. Its eyes are specially adapted to see in the dark.

# 368. Gorebyss
## Look
An evolution of Clamperl, Gorebyss is a long, pink, serpentine, aquatic Pokémon. It has a long, slender snout that it uses to suck the bodily fluids from its prey.
## Lore
Gorebyss, the South Sea Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful appearance but its cruel nature. It inserts its thin, needle-like mouth into its prey and drains them of their fluids.

# 369. Relicanth
## Look
A prehistoric, coelacanth-like Pokémon. It has a brown, armored body with a rugged, ancient appearance.
## Lore
Relicanth, the Longevity Pokémon, is a living fossil that has not changed its form in over 100 million years. It was thought to be extinct but was rediscovered on a deep-sea dive. It is a very resilient and powerful Pokémon.

# 370. Luvdisc
## Look
A small, heart-shaped, fish-like Pokémon. It has a pink body and a perpetually puckered-lip expression.
## Lore
Luvdisc, the Rendezvous Pokémon, is a creature that is a symbol of love and romance. It is said that any couple that finds a Luvdisc will be blessed with eternal love. They are often seen in pairs.

# 371. Bagon
## Look
A small, bipedal, reptilian Pokémon with a blue, rocky hide. It has a large, hard, helmet-like head. It has a determined and somewhat sad expression.
## Lore
Bagon, the Rock Head Pokémon, is a creature that dreams of one day being able to fly. It is so obsessed with this dream that it will often throw itself off of cliffs in a desperate attempt to take flight. Its head is as hard as rock, which protects it from these falls.

# 372. Shelgon
## Look
The pupal stage of the Bagon line, Shelgon is a Pokémon encased in a white, bone-like shell. It has four short, stubby legs. Its eyes can be seen peering out from an opening in the shell.
## Lore
Shelgon, the Endurance Pokémon, is a creature that is undergoing a massive transformation inside its hard shell. It is very durable and can repel most attacks. It is said that the stress of being unable to move causes it to generate a great deal of heat.

# 373. Salamence
## Look
The final evolution, Salamence is a large, draconic, quadrupedal Pokémon with massive, red, crescent-shaped wings. It has a sleek, blue body and a fierce, joyful expression.
## Lore
Salamence, the Dragon Pokémon, is the result of Bagon's long-held dream of being able to fly. The intense desire to fly caused a sudden mutation in its cells, resulting in the growth of its magnificent wings. It is a very powerful and joyful Pokémon that is often seen breathing fire as it soars through the sky.

# 374. Beldum
## Look
A robotic, single-limbed Pokémon. It has a blue, metallic body with a single, large, red eye. It floats through the air using magnetic force.
## Lore
Beldum, the Iron Ball Pokémon, is a creature that is made of pure magnetic energy. It communicates with others by sending out magnetic pulses. It is said to be as intelligent as a supercomputer.

# 375. Metang
## Look
The evolved form of Beldum, Metang is composed of two Beldum that have fused together. It has two red eyes and two powerful, claw-like arms.
## Lore
Metang, the Iron Claw Pokémon, is the result of two Beldum merging. It has two brains that are linked telepathically, which makes it incredibly intelligent. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 376. Metagross
## Look
The final evolution, Metagross is a massive, spider-like, robotic Pokémon that is composed of four Beldum that have fused together. It has four powerful legs and a cross-shaped marking on its face.
## Lore
Metagross, the Iron Leg Pokémon, is a creature with four brains that are linked together, making it more intelligent than a supercomputer. It is a very powerful and ruthless Pokémon that can calculate the outcome of a battle before it even begins.

# 377. Regirock
## Look
A legendary Pokémon that resembles a golem made of brown, jagged rocks. It has a pattern of seven orange dots on its face that resemble the letter "H."
## Lore
Regirock, the Rock Peak Pokémon, is one of the three legendary titans. Its body is made of rocks from all over the world. If its body is damaged in battle, it can repair itself by attaching new rocks that it finds.

# 378. Regice
## Look
A legendary Pokémon that resembles a golem made of pure, crystalline ice. It has a pattern of seven yellow dots on its face.
## Lore
Regice, the Iceberg Pokémon, is one of the three legendary titans. Its body is made of Antarctic ice that is so cold it will not melt, even if it is submerged in magma. It can freeze the air around it to temperatures of -328 degrees Fahrenheit.

# 379. Registeel
## Look
A legendary Pokémon that resembles a golem made of a mysterious, otherworldly metal. It has a pattern of seven red dots on its face.
## Lore
Registeel, the Iron Pokémon, is one of the three legendary titans. Its body is made of a strange metal that is not found on Earth. It is harder than any known metal, yet it is also flexible. It is a very mysterious and powerful Pokémon.

# 380. Latias
## Look
A legendary, draconic, avian Pokémon with a red and white body. It has two jet-plane-like wings and a gentle, intelligent expression.
## Lore
Latias, the Eon Pokémon, is a highly intelligent creature that can understand human speech. It is a very gentle and compassionate Pokémon and dislikes fighting. It can use a special down to refract light and make itself invisible.

# 381. Latios
## Look
A legendary, draconic, avian Pokémon with a blue and white body. It has two jet-plane-like wings and a determined, intelligent expression. It is the male counterpart to Latias.
## Lore
Latios, the Eon Pokémon, is a creature that is faster than a jet plane. It can fold in its wings to reduce air resistance and fly at incredible speeds. It is a very protective Pokémon and will do anything to protect its partner, Latias.

# 382. Kyogre
## Look
A massive, whale-like, legendary Pokémon with a deep blue body and red markings. It is the master of the oceans.
## Lore
Kyogre, the Sea Basin Pokémon, is a legendary creature with the power to expand the oceans. It is said that in ancient times, it was locked in a cataclysmic battle with Groudon. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 383. Groudon
## Look
A massive, bipedal, dinosaur-like, legendary Pokémon with a red, armored hide and black markings. It is the master of the continents.
## Lore
Groudon, the Continent Pokémon, is a legendary creature with the power to expand the continents. It is said that it can cause massive droughts by evaporating water with its intense heat. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 384. Rayquaza
## Look
A massive, serpentine, draconic, legendary Pokémon with a green, armored body and yellow, ring-like markings. It is the master of the skies.
## Lore
Rayquaza, the Sky High Pokémon, is a legendary creature that lives in the ozone layer. It is said that it has lived for hundreds of millions of years. It is known to descend from the sky to stop the fighting between Kyogre and Groudon.

# 385. Jirachi
## Look
A small, humanoid, mythical Pokémon with a star-shaped head. It has three blue, tag-like "wishes" on its head. It is almost always sleeping.
## Lore
Jirachi, the Wish Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is said to be able to grant any wish. It is said to sleep for a thousand years and will only awaken for seven days to grant the wishes of those who are pure of heart.

# 386. Deoxys
## Look
A mythical, alien Pokémon that can change its form. Its normal form is a bipedal, humanoid creature with a red and blue body. It has a crystalline organ in its chest.
## Lore
Deoxys, the DNA Pokémon, is a creature that was born from a virus from space that underwent a sudden mutation when it was exposed to a laser beam. It is a very intelligent Pokémon and can change its form to adapt to any situation.

# 387. Turtwig
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, turtle-like Pokémon with a light green body. It has a small sapling growing out of its head. It has a cheerful and gentle appearance.
## Lore
Turtwig, the Tiny Leaf Pokémon, has a shell on its back that is made of soil. The sapling on its head is a sign of its health; it will wilt if the Pokémon is thirsty. It is a very gentle and kind Pokémon.

# 388. Grotle
## Look
The evolved form of Turtwig, Grotle is a larger, more robust, turtle-like Pokémon. The sapling on its head has now grown into a small bush.
## Lore
Grotle, the Grove Pokémon, is a creature that is known to carry smaller Pokémon on its back, mistaking it for a moving grove. It is a very gentle and caring Pokémon.

# 389. Torterra
## Look
The final evolution, Torterra is a massive, tortoise-like Pokémon with a small, living tree growing on its back. It has a powerful and majestic appearance.
## Lore
Torterra, the Continent Pokémon, is a creature that is so large that smaller Pokémon will often make their nests on its back, mistaking it for a small forest. It is a very gentle and peaceful Pokémon.

# 390. Chimchar
## Look
A small, bipedal, chimpanzee-like Pokémon with orange fur. It has a flame burning on its rear. It has a mischievous and playful appearance.
## Lore
Chimchar, the Chimp Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible agility. The flame on its rear is fueled by the gas in its stomach and will never go out, even in the rain.

# 391. Monferno
## Look
The evolved form of Chimchar, Monferno is a bipedal, primate-like Pokémon with a blue and orange coat. It has a larger flame on its tail and a confident, aggressive look.
## Lore
Monferno, the Playful Pokémon, is a master of a unique form of martial arts that uses all of its limbs. It can control the size of the flame on its tail to keep its foes at a distance.

# 392. Infernape
## Look
The final evolution, Infernape is a powerful, humanoid, primate-like Pokémon with a crown of fire on its head. It has a unique fighting style that combines speed and power.
## Lore
Infernape, the Flame Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible speed and its powerful, fiery attacks. It is a master of a unique form of martial arts that is unlike any other.

# 393. Piplup
## Look
A small, penguin-like Pokémon with a blue and white body. It has a proud and somewhat stubborn expression. It is a very clumsy Pokémon.
## Lore
Piplup, the Penguin Pokémon, is a creature that is very proud and dislikes accepting help from others. It is a very skilled swimmer but is very clumsy on land.

# 394. Prinplup
## Look
The evolved form of Piplup, Prinplup is a larger and more elegant penguin Pokémon. It has two crest-like horns on its head and two powerful, flipper-like wings.
## Lore
Prinplup, the Penguin Pokémon, is a creature that lives alone in the cold seas. It is a very powerful swimmer and can hunt for prey in the deep ocean.

# 395. Empoleon
## Look
The final evolution, Empoleon is a massive, emperor penguin-like Pokémon with a powerful and regal appearance. It has three large, trident-like horns on its beak and two massive, steel-tipped flippers.
## Lore
Empoleon, the Emperor Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible pride. It is said that it will not accept food from humans. The three horns on its beak are a symbol of its power, and the leader of the colony will have the largest horns.

# 396. Starly
## Look
A small, avian Pokémon that resembles a starling. It has a gray and black plumage and a single, long feather on its head. It has a sharp and determined look.
## Lore
Starly, the Starling Pokémon, is a creature that travels in large flocks. They are known for their beautiful, albeit noisy, singing. They are very weak on their own, but they are very powerful when they are in a group.

# 397. Staravia
## Look
The evolved form of Starly, Staravia is a larger and more aggressive-looking starling Pokémon. It has a prominent, Elvis-like crest on its head.
## Lore
Staravia, the Starling Pokémon, is a creature that is very aware of its own weakness. They will form large flocks to protect themselves from predators. They are very territorial and will attack any intruder that comes near their nest.

# 398. Staraptor
## Look
The final evolution, Staraptor is a large and powerful bird of prey. It has a massive, powerful crest on its head and a fierce, intimidating look.
## Lore
Staraptor, the Predator Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible bravery. It will challenge any foe, no matter how large. It is a very powerful and formidable fighter.

# 399. Bidoof
## Look
A small, beaver-like Pokémon with a goofy and vacant expression. It has two large, buck teeth and a round, brown body.
## Lore
Bidoof, the Plump Mouse Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its hardy and adaptable nature. It can live in any environment and will eat practically anything. It is a very clumsy Pokémon.

# 400. Bibarel
## Look
The evolved form of Bidoof, Bibarel is a larger and more robust beaver Pokémon. It has a black and brown coat and a large, flat tail.
## Lore
Bibarel, the Beaver Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its skill at building dams. It can build a dam in a single night. It is a very industrious and hardworking Pokémon.

# 401. Kricketot
## Look
A small, insectoid Pokémon that resembles a cricket. It has a red and black body and two large, antenna-like appendages that it rubs together to make a sound like a xylophone.
## Lore
Kricketot, the Cricket Pokémon, is a creature that communicates by making musical sounds. The sound it makes is said to be a sign of autumn.

# 402. Kricketune
## Look
The evolved form of Kricketot, Kricketune is a bipedal, cricket-like Pokémon with a mustache-like appendage and two large, scythe-like arms.
## Lore
Kricketune, the Cricket Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful, melodic cry. It can create a variety of different sounds by rubbing its arms together. It is said that its cry can be used to express a variety of different emotions.

# 403. Shinx
## Look
A small, feline Pokémon that resembles a lion cub. It has a blue and black coat and a small, star-shaped tuft on its tail. It has a cute and playful appearance.
## Lore
Shinx, the Flash Pokémon, is a creature that can generate electricity by flexing its muscles. The light it emits can be blinding to its foes. It is a very playful and curious Pokémon.

# 404. Luxio
## Look
The evolved form of Shinx, Luxio is a larger and more powerful lion-like Pokémon. It has a sleek, blue and black coat and a mane-like ruff of fur around its neck.
## Lore
Luxio, the Spark Pokémon, is a creature that can communicate with others by using a series of electrical pulses. It is known to live in small packs.

# 405. Luxray
## Look
The final evolution, Luxray is a large and powerful lion-like Pokémon. It has a black and blue coat and a massive, spiky mane. It has piercing, yellow eyes that can see through solid objects.
## Lore
Luxray, the Gleam Eyes Pokémon, is a creature that has the ability to see through anything. It can use this power to spot prey that is hiding and to find lost items. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 406. Budew
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Roselia, Budew is a small, bipedal, plant-like Pokémon. It has a green body and a small, closed bud on its head.
## Lore
Budew, the Bud Pokémon, is a creature that is very sensitive to the temperature. It will only open its bud on warm, sunny days. The bud releases a faint, sweet aroma.

# 407. Roserade
## Look
The final evolution of the Roselia line, Roserade is a graceful and elegant, humanoid, plant-like Pokémon. It has two large bouquets of roses for hands and a cape-like set of leaves on its back.
## Lore
Roserade, the Bouquet Pokémon, is a creature that uses a sweet, alluring aroma to attract its prey. It can then attack with a flurry of poisonous whips that are hidden in its bouquets. It is a very beautiful but dangerous Pokémon.

# 408. Cranidos
## Look
A prehistoric, dinosaur-like Pokémon that resembles a pachycephalosaurus. It has a blue and gray body and a massive, dome-shaped, rock-hard head.
## Lore
Cranidos, the Head Butt Pokémon, is a creature that was resurrected from a fossil. It is known for its incredible headbutting power. It can shatter a car with a single headbutt. It is a very powerful and reckless Pokémon.

# 409. Rampardos
## Look
The evolved form of Cranidos, Rampardos is a massive and powerful pachycephalosaurus Pokémon. Its head is even larger and more durable than Cranidos's.
## Lore
Rampardos, the Head Butt Pokémon, is a creature that has a skull that is as hard as iron. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon that can topple a jungle's trees with a single charge.

# 410. Shieldon
## Look
A prehistoric, dinosaur-like Pokémon that resembles a protoceratops. It has a yellow and gray body and a massive, shield-like face.
## Lore
Shieldon, the Shield Pokémon, is a creature that was resurrected from a fossil. Its face is as hard as a diamond and can repel any attack. It is a very docile Pokémon that prefers to avoid conflict.

# 411. Bastiodon
## Look
The evolved form of Shieldon, Bastiodon is a massive and powerful ceratopsian Pokémon. Its face has now grown into a massive, impenetrable shield.
## Lore
Bastiodon, the Shield Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible defensive power. It is said that when they line up, they can form an impenetrable wall. They are very gentle and caring Pokémon.

# 412. Burmy
## Look
A small, bagworm-like Pokémon. Its true body is a small, black, grub-like creature. It is almost always seen wearing a cloak that it has made from materials it has found in its environment.
## Lore
Burmy, the Bagworm Pokémon, is a creature that is very resourceful. It will use anything it can find to make its cloak, from leaves and twigs to sand and trash. The cloak provides it with camouflage and protection.

# 413. Wormadam
## Look
An evolution of the female Burmy, Wormadam is a Pokémon that has fused with its cloak. It has three different forms, depending on the material of its cloak: Plant, Sandy, and Trash.
## Lore
Wormadam, the Bagworm Pokémon, is a creature that is a master of camouflage. Its form and type change depending on the environment in which it evolved. It is a very defensive Pokémon.

# 414. Mothim
## Look
An evolution of the male Burmy, Mothim is a moth-like Pokémon. It has a black and orange body and two large, feathery wings.
## Lore
Mothim, the Moth Pokémon, is a nocturnal creature that is most active at night. It is known to fly from place to place in search of honey. It is a very fast and agile flyer.

# 415. Combee
## Look
A small, bee-like Pokémon that is composed of three individual, hexagonal segments. Each segment has a small face and a pair of wings.
## Lore
Combee, the Tiny Bee Pokémon, is a creature that lives in large colonies. They work together to gather honey and protect their queen. They are very loyal to their queen.

# 416. Vespiquen
## Look
The evolved form of the female Combee, Vespiquen is a large, queen bee-like Pokémon. Its lower body is a massive, honey-comb-like structure that houses its young.
## Lore
Vespiquen, the Beehive Pokémon, is the queen of the Combee colony. She is a very powerful and protective Pokémon that will do anything to protect her young. She can command her colony to attack in a coordinated swarm.

# 417. Pachirisu
## Look
A small, squirrel-like Pokémon with a white and blue coat. It has a large, fluffy tail and two yellow, electrical cheek pouches.
## Lore
Pachirisu, the EleSquirrel Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible speed and agility. It can generate electricity in its cheek pouches and discharge it from its tail. It is a very playful and mischievous Pokémon.

# 418. Buizel
## Look
A weasel-like Pokémon with an orange coat. It has a yellow, inflatable flotation sac around its neck. It has two tails that it can spin like a propeller.
## Lore
Buizel, the Sea Weasel Pokémon, is a creature that is a very skilled swimmer. It can use its two tails as a propeller to move through the water at high speeds. The flotation sac around its neck can be inflated to help it stay afloat.

# 419. Floatzel
## Look
The evolved form of Buizel, Floatzel is a larger and more powerful sea weasel Pokémon. Its flotation sac is now much larger and can be used as a raft.
## Lore
Floatzel, the Sea Weasel Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its heroic nature. It is often seen rescuing people who are drowning. It is a very powerful and reliable Pokémon.

# 420. Cherubi
## Look
A small, cherry-like Pokémon. It has a pink, spherical body and a small, second, undeveloped cherry attached to it.
## Lore
Cherubi, the Cherry Pokémon, is a creature that is very sweet and nutritious. The small ball attached to it is filled with all the nutrients it needs to evolve.

# 421. Cherrim
## Look
The evolved form of Cherubi, Cherrim has two forms: Overcast and Sunshine. In its Overcast Form, it is a closed, purple bud. In its Sunshine Form, it blooms into a beautiful, pink flower.
## Lore
Cherrim, the Blossom Pokémon, is a creature that is very sensitive to the sunlight. It will only bloom in strong sunlight. When it blooms, it is said to be very cheerful and will dance with joy.

# 422. Shellos
## Look
A sea slug-like Pokémon with two different forms, depending on the ocean it is from: West Sea (pink and white) and East Sea (blue and green).
## Lore
Shellos, the Sea Slug Pokémon, is a creature that has a very soft and pliable body. It is said that its form and color change depending on its diet and environment.

# 423. Gastrodon
## Look
The evolved form of Shellos, Gastrodon is a larger and more powerful sea slug Pokémon. It also has two different forms: West Sea and East Sea.
## Lore
Gastrodon, the Sea Slug Pokémon, is a creature that is very resilient. It can regenerate any part of its body that is lost. It is a very powerful and adaptable Pokémon.

# 424. Ambipom
## Look
The evolved form of Aipom, Ambipom is a monkey-like Pokémon with two large, hand-like tails.
## Lore
Ambipom, the Long Tail Pokémon, is a creature that is a master of using its two tails. It can use them to deliver a flurry of powerful punches and to perform complex tasks.

# 425. Drifloon
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a purple balloon. It has two long, string-like arms with yellow, heart-shaped hands. It has a single, cloud-like tuft on its head.
## Lore
Drifloon, the Balloon Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be formed from the spirits of people and Pokémon. It is known to tug on the hands of children and try to carry them away. It is a very mysterious and unsettling Pokémon.

# 426. Drifblim
## Look
The evolved form of Drifloon, Drifblim is a larger, hot-air-balloon-like Pokémon. It has four long, string-like arms.
## Lore
Drifblim, the Blimp Pokémon, is a creature that can be seen floating in the evening sky. It is said that they are carrying clusters of Drifloon. It is a very majestic and beautiful sight.

# 427. Buneary
## Look
A small, rabbit-like Pokémon with brown and cream-colored fur. It has two long, fluffy ears that can be rolled up.
## Lore
Buneary, the Rabbit Pokémon, is a creature that is very cautious and timid. It is known to be very fast and can deliver powerful kicks with its strong legs.

# 428. Lopunny
## Look
The evolved form of Buneary, Lopunny is a bipedal, rabbit-like Pokémon with a slender and elegant appearance. It has long, fluffy ears and a graceful, dancer-like build.
## Lore
Lopunny, the Rabbit Pokémon, is a creature that is very conscious of its appearance. It is always preening its ears. It is a very fast and agile Pokémon and can deliver powerful kicks.

# 429. Mismagius
## Look
The evolved form of Misdreavus, Mismagius is a ghostly Pokémon that resembles a witch. It has a large, pointed hat and a flowing, cloak-like body.
## Lore
Mismagius, the Magical Pokémon, is a creature that can chant incantations that can cause both happiness and misery. It is a very powerful and mischievous Pokémon.

# 430. Honchkrow
## Look
The evolved form of Murkrow, Honchkrow is a large, avian Pokémon that resembles a mafia boss. It has a large, crest-like hat and a chest full of white, fluffy feathers.
## Lore
Honchkrow, the Big Boss Pokémon, is a creature that is the leader of a large flock of Murkrow. It is a very ruthless and cunning Pokémon that will do anything to get what it wants.

# 431. Glameow
## Look
A feline Pokémon with a gray, slender body. It has a coiled, spring-like tail and a crescent-shaped crest on its head. It has a very fickle and capricious appearance.
## Lore
Glameow, the Catty Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its fickle nature. It can be very affectionate one moment and then scratch and bite the next. It is a very difficult Pokémon to please.

# 432. Purugly
## Look
The evolved form of Glameow, Purugly is a large and overweight feline Pokémon. It has a very grumpy and intimidating appearance.
## Lore
Purugly, the Tiger Cat Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its aggressive and territorial nature. It will wrap its two tails around its waist to make itself look larger and more intimidating.

# 433. Chingling
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Chimecho, Chingling is a small, bell-like Pokémon. It has a small, round body with a rope-like appendage that has a small orb at the end.
## Lore
Chingling, the Bell Pokémon, is a creature that communicates by making a ringing sound. The sound it makes is said to be very soothing.

# 434. Stunky
## Look
A small, skunk-like Pokémon with a purple and white coat. It has a mischievous and playful appearance.
## Lore
Stunky, the Skunk Pokémon, is a creature that can spray a foul-smelling liquid from its rear. The stench is so powerful that it can be smelled from over a mile away.

# 435. Skuntank
## Look
The evolved form of Stunky, Skuntank is a larger and more powerful skunk Pokémon. It has a massive, bushy tail that it can use to spray its foul-smelling liquid over a large area.
## Lore
Skuntank, the Skunk Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible stench. The liquid it sprays can be shot over 160 feet. It is a very powerful and intimidating Pokémon.

# 436. Bronzor
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a bronze mirror. It has a blue-green, disc-shaped body with a series of spheres around its edge.
## Lore
Bronzor, the Bronze Pokémon, is a mysterious creature that was first discovered in ancient tombs. It is said that the markings on its back hold a mysterious power.

# 437. Bronzong
## Look
The evolved form of Bronzor, Bronzong is a large, bell-like Pokémon. It has a blue-green, bell-shaped body with two arm-like appendages.
## Lore
Bronzong, the Bronze Bell Pokémon, is a creature that is revered for its ability to bring rain. It is said that ancient people would pray to it to bring rain for their crops.

# 438. Bonsly
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Sudowoodo, Bonsly is a Pokémon that resembles a small, potted plant. It has a brown, rock-like body and three green, club-like appendages on its head. It is always seen crying.
## Lore
Bonsly, the Bonsai Pokémon, is a creature that is very sensitive. It is always crying, and it is said that its tears are a way of adjusting its body's fluid levels.

# 439. Mime Jr.
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Mr. Mime, Mime Jr. is a small, humanoid Pokémon with a clown-like appearance. It has a blue, jester-like hat.
## Lore
Mime Jr., the Mime Pokémon, is a creature that loves to mimic others. It is a very playful and mischievous Pokémon.

# 440. Happiny
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Chansey, Happiny is a small, pink, ovoid Pokémon. It carries a small, white, egg-shaped stone in a pouch on its belly.
## Lore
Happiny, the Playhouse Pokémon, is a creature that loves to imitate Chansey. It will carry a small, round stone in its pouch as if it were an egg.

# 441. Chatot
## Look
A small, avian Pokémon that resembles a parrot. It has a black head that is shaped like a musical eighth note. It has a colorful, vibrant plumage.
## Lore
Chatot, the Music Note Pokémon, is a creature that can perfectly mimic human speech. It is a very intelligent Pokémon and can learn a variety of different words and phrases.

# 442. Spiritomb
## Look
A ghostly Pokémon that is composed of 108 spirits that are bound to an Odd Keystone. It has a swirling, purple, ethereal body with a menacing, green face.
## Lore
Spiritomb, the Forbidden Pokémon, is a creature that was formed from 108 spirits that were bound to a stone for their misdeeds. It is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon.

# 443. Gible
## Look
A small, bipedal, draconic Pokémon that resembles a land shark. It has a blue body and a large, powerful jaw.
## Lore
Gible, the Land Shark Pokémon, is a creature that lives in small, heated caves. It is known to be very clumsy and will often bump into things. It is a very powerful Pokémon for its small size.

# 444. Gabite
## Look
The evolved form of Gible, Gabite is a larger and more powerful land shark Pokémon. It has a sleek, aerodynamic body and two large, scythe-like claws.
## Lore
Gabite, the Cave Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its love of shiny things. It is known to hoard treasure in its nest. It is a very fast and agile Pokémon.

# 445. Garchomp
## Look
The final evolution, Garchomp is a massive, bipedal, draconic Pokémon with a body that is a mix of a shark and a jet plane. It has two large, scythe-like appendages on its arms.
## Lore
Garchomp, the Mach Pokémon, is a creature that can fly at the speed of sound. It is said that when it flies, its wings create blades of wind that can fell trees. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 446. Munchlax
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Snorlax, Munchlax is a small, gluttonous Pokémon. It has a dark blue-green body and a perpetually hungry expression.
## Lore
Munchlax, the Big Eater Pokémon, is a creature that is always hungry. It will eat almost anything it can find. It is known to hoard food in its long fur.

# 447. Riolu
## Look
A small, bipedal, canine Pokémon with a blue and black coat. It has a confident and determined expression. It can sense the auras of other living things.
## Lore
Riolu, the Emanation Pokémon, is a creature that has the ability to see the auras of people and Pokémon. It can use this power to understand their feelings and to predict their moves.

# 448. Lucario
## Look
The evolved form of Riolu, Lucario is a bipedal, canine Pokémon with a sleek, powerful build. It has a blue and black coat and a series of spikes on its chest and hands.
## Lore
Lucario, the Aura Pokémon, is a creature that can understand human speech. It can also read the thoughts and feelings of others by sensing their auras. It is a very powerful and honorable Pokémon.

# 449. Hippopotas
## Look
A small, hippopotamus-like Pokémon with a brown, sandy body. It is almost always seen with its snout sticking out of the sand.
## Lore
Hippopotas, the Hippo Pokémon, is a creature that lives in deserts. It spends most of its time with its body submerged in the sand to avoid the heat. It can expel sand from its nostrils to create a sandstorm.

# 450. Hippowdon
## Look
The evolved form of Hippopotas, Hippowdon is a massive, powerful hippopotamus Pokémon. It has a large, cavernous mouth and a body that is as tough as rock.
## Lore
Hippowdon, the Heavyweight Pokémon, is a creature that can create massive sandstorms by opening its mouth. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 451. Skorupi
## Look
A small, scorpion-like Pokémon with a purple and blue body. It has two large, powerful pincers and a tail with two stingers.
## Lore
Skorupi, the Scorpion Pokémon, is a creature that lives in deserts. It is a very patient and cunning predator. It will bury itself in the sand and wait for unsuspecting prey to come near.

# 452. Drapion
## Look
The evolved form of Skorupi, Drapion is a massive and intimidating scorpion Pokémon. It has a purple and gray body and two massive, powerful pincers.
## Lore
Drapion, the Ogre Scorp Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength and its violent nature. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 453. Croagunk
## Look
A bipedal, frog-like Pokémon with a blue and gray body. It has two orange, poisonous cheek pouches and a mischievous, confident expression.
## Lore
Croagunk, the Toxic Mouth Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its unique fighting style. It uses its poisonous fingers to jab at its foes. It is a very cunning and tricky Pokémon.

# 454. Toxicroak
## Look
The evolved form of Croagunk, Toxicroak is a larger and more powerful frog-like Pokémon. It has a large, poisonous sac on its throat and a sharp, poisonous claw on its hand.
## Lore
