Toxicroak, the Toxic Mouth Pokémon, is a creature that can create a powerful poison by mixing the toxins in its body. The poison is so potent that even a scratch can be fatal.

# 455. Carnivine
## Look
A plant-like Pokémon that resembles a Venus flytrap. It has a large, green, maw-like head and two vine-like arms.
## Lore
Carnivine, the Bug Catcher Pokémon, is a creature that lures in its prey with a sweet, alluring aroma. It can then swallow its prey whole. It is a very patient and cunning predator.

# 456. Finneon
## Look
A small, fish-like Pokémon with a blue and black body. It has two large, butterfly-like fins on its tail.
## Lore
Finneon, the Wing Fish Pokémon, is known as the "Beautifly of the Sea" for its beautiful, wing-like fins. It is a very graceful and elegant swimmer.

# 457. Lumineon
## Look
The evolved form of Finneon, Lumineon is a larger and more elegant fish-like Pokémon. It has two massive, butterfly-like fins that it uses to walk along the seabed.
## Lore
Lumineon, the Neon Pokémon, is a creature that lives in the deep ocean. It can use the patterns on its fins to communicate with others. It is a very beautiful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 458. Mantyke
## Look
The pre-evolved form of Mantine, Mantyke is a small, manta ray-like Pokémon. It has a cheerful and playful appearance.
## Lore
Mantyke, the Kite Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its curious nature. It is often seen leaping out of the water. The pattern on its back is unique to each individual.

# 459. Snover
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a small, snow-covered evergreen tree. It has a brown, trunk-like body and two green, leaf-like arms.
## Lore
Snover, the Frosted Tree Pokémon, is a creature that lives in snowy mountains. It is a very curious Pokémon and is known to approach humans. It is said that it can bear fruit in the spring.

# 460. Abomasnow
## Look
The evolved form of Snover, Abomasnow is a massive, yeti-like Pokémon with a body that is a mix of a tree and a snow monster.
## Lore
Abomasnow, the Frosted Tree Pokémon, is a creature that can whip up massive blizzards. It is known as the "Ice Monster." It is a very powerful and territorial Pokémon.

# 461. Weavile
## Look
The evolved form of Sneasel, Weavile is a taller and more menacing weasel-like Pokémon. It has a large, red, feather-like crest on its head and a series of sharp, retractable claws.
## Lore
Weavile, the Sharp Claw Pokémon, is a creature that lives in cold, snowy regions. It is known to hunt in packs of four or five. They are very cunning and will use their coordinated attacks to take down prey much larger than themselves.

# 462. Magnezone
## Look
The evolved form of Magneton, Magnezone is a large, UFO-like Pokémon. It is composed of three individual Magneton-like entities.
## Lore
Magnezone, the Magnet Area Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have been created when a special magnetic field caused a Magneton to evolve. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 463. Lickilicky
## Look
The evolved form of Lickitung, Lickilicky is a larger and more rotund Pokémon. It has a massive, prehensile tongue that can be extended to great lengths.
## Lore
Lickilicky, the Licking Pokémon, is a creature that can wrap its tongue around anything. Its saliva is a powerful anesthetic that can numb anything it touches.

# 464. Rhyperior
## Look
The evolved form of Rhydon, Rhyperior is a massive and powerful, bipedal, rhinoceros-like Pokémon. Its body is covered in a thick, orange and gray, armor-like hide. It has a drill-like horn and a series of geyser-like holes on its hands.
## Lore
Rhyperior, the Drill Pokémon, is a creature that can insert rocks and boulders into the holes on its hands and then fire them like cannonballs. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 465. Tangrowth
## Look
The evolved form of Tangela, Tangrowth is a massive, tangled mass of blue vines. It has two long, vine-like arms that it can extend to great lengths.
## Lore
Tangrowth, the Vine Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible regenerative abilities. It can regrow its arms in a matter of seconds if they are cut off. It is a very powerful and resilient Pokémon.

# 466. Electivire
## Look
The evolved form of Electabuzz, Electivire is a massive and powerful, humanoid Pokémon with a yellow and black coat. It has two long, whip-like tails that it can use to shock its foes.
## Lore
Electivire, the Thunderbolt Pokémon, is a creature that can generate over 20,000 volts of electricity. It can create a powerful electrical barrier to protect itself from attacks.

# 467. Magmortar
## Look
The evolved form of Magmar, Magmortar is a larger and more powerful, humanoid Pokémon with a body that is a mix of a cannon and a magma monster. It has two massive, cannon-like arms.
## Lore
Magmortar, the Blast Pokémon, is a creature that can fire massive fireballs from its cannon-like arms. The fireballs can reach temperatures of over 3,600 degrees Fahrenheit.

# 468. Togekiss
## Look
The final evolution of the Togepi line, Togekiss is a majestic, avian Pokémon with a large, white, jet-plane-like wingspan.
## Lore
Togekiss, the Jubilee Pokémon, is a creature that is known to visit peaceful regions. It is a symbol of good luck and happiness.

# 469. Yanmega
## Look
The evolved form of Yanma, Yanmega is a massive, dragonfly-like Pokémon with a powerful jaw and a pair of large, feathery wings.
## Lore
Yanmega, the Ogre Darner Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible jaw strength. It can easily bite through thick branches. It is a very powerful and formidable predator.

# 470. Leafeon
## Look
An evolution of Eevee, Leafeon is a feline Pokémon with a body that is a mix of a plant and an animal. It has several leaf-like appendages on its body.
## Lore
Leafeon, the Verdant Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have a cellular structure that is similar to a plant. It can perform photosynthesis to create its own energy.

# 471. Glaceon
## Look
An evolution of Eevee, Glaceon is a feline Pokémon with a body that is made of ice. It has two long, diamond-shaped, ear-like appendages on its head.
## Lore
Glaceon, the Fresh Snow Pokémon, is a creature that can control its body temperature at will. It can freeze the moisture in the air to create a fine, diamond-dust-like snow.

# 472. Gliscor
## Look
The evolved form of Gligar, Gliscor is a large, scorpion-like Pokémon with bat-like features. It has a long, prehensile tail with a stinger and two large, powerful pincers.
## Lore
Gliscor, the Fang Scorp Pokémon, is a creature that can fly through the air without making a sound. It can hang upside down from tree branches and wait for unsuspecting prey to pass by.

# 473. Mamoswine
## Look
The final evolution of the Swinub line, Mamoswine is a massive, mammoth-like Pokémon. It has two huge, sharp tusks made of ice and a thick, shaggy, brown coat.
## Lore
Mamoswine, the Twin Tusk Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have been around since the ice age. It is a very powerful and resilient Pokémon that can survive in extremely cold temperatures.

# 474. Porygon-Z
## Look
The final evolution of the Porygon line, Porygon-Z is a glitched and erratic version of Porygon2. Its head is detached from its body, and it moves in a bizarre, unpredictable manner.
## Lore
Porygon-Z, the Virtual Pokémon, is a creature that was created when Porygon2 was updated with a dubious program. It is said that its strange behavior is due to the faulty programming.

# 475. Gallade
## Look
An evolution of a male Kirlia, Gallade is a humanoid Pokémon that resembles a gladiator. It has two large, sword-like blades for arms.
## Lore
Gallade, the Blade Pokémon, is a master of swordsmanship. It can extend and retract the blades on its arms at will. It is a very courteous and honorable Pokémon.

# 476. Probopass
## Look
The evolved form of Nosepass, Probopass is a larger and more complex moai-statue-like Pokémon. It has a massive, red, magnetic nose and three small, satellite-like "Mini-Noses" that it can control.
## Lore
Probopass, the Compass Pokémon, is a creature that has a powerful magnetic field. It can control its three Mini-Noses to attack its foes from all directions.

# 477. Dusknoir
## Look
The final evolution of the Duskull line, Dusknoir is a large and powerful, cyclopean, ghostly Pokémon. It has a large, antenna-like appendage on its head and a massive, gaping mouth on its stomach.
## Lore
Dusknoir, the Gripper Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be able to receive transmissions from the spirit world with the antenna on its head. It is said that it can take people to the spirit world by opening the mouth on its stomach.

# 478. Froslass
## Look
An evolution of a female Snorunt, Froslass is a ghostly, kimono-clad Pokémon. It has a hollow body and two large, ice-like horns on its head.
## Lore
Froslass, the Snow Land Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be the spirit of a woman who was lost in a blizzard. It is known to freeze its prey with its icy breath and then take them back to its lair.

# 479. Rotom
## Look
A small, plasma-based Pokémon. It has a reddish-orange, ethereal body and can possess various electronic appliances.
## Lore
Rotom, the Plasma Pokémon, is a creature that can inhabit and control various electronic devices. It is a very mischievous Pokémon that loves to play pranks.

# 480. Uxie
## Look
A legendary, pixie-like Pokémon with a gray body and a yellow, helmet-like head. It is one of the three lake guardians.
## Lore
Uxie, the Knowledge Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to have given humans the intelligence to solve problems. It is a very wise and powerful Pokémon.

# 481. Mesprit
## Look
A legendary, pixie-like Pokémon with a gray body and a pink, magenta-haired head. It is one of the three lake guardians.
## Lore
Mesprit, the Emotion Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to have taught humans the nobility of sorrow, pain, and joy. It is a very compassionate Pokémon.

# 482. Azelf
## Look
A legendary, pixie-like Pokémon with a gray body and a blue, helmet-like head. It is one of the three lake guardians.
## Lore
Azelf, the Willpower Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to have given humans the determination to face any challenge. It is a very courageous and powerful Pokémon.

# 483. Dialga
## Look
A massive, sauropod-like, legendary Pokémon with a blue and gray, metallic body. It has a large, diamond-like crest on its chest. It is the master of time.
## Lore
Dialga, the Temporal Pokémon, is a legendary creature that has the power to control time. It is said that time began to move when it was born. It is a very powerful and majestic Pokémon.

# 484. Palkia
## Look
A massive, bipedal, theropod-like, legendary Pokémon with a pink and white body. It has two large, pearl-like orbs on its shoulders. It is the master of space.
## Lore
Palkia, a Legendary Pokémon, is said to live in a gap in the spatial dimension parallel to ours. It has the ability to distort space.

# 485. Heatran
## Look
A tortoise-like, legendary Pokémon with a brown, metallic body. It has a magma-like substance flowing through the cracks in its body.
## Lore
Heatran, the Lava Dome Pokémon, is a legendary creature that lives in volcanic caves. It is said that its blood is like boiling magma.

# 486. Regigigas
## Look
A massive, golem-like, legendary Pokémon. It has a white and gray body with a series of black, metallic plates. It has three colored gems on its chest.
## Lore
Regigigas, the Colossal Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to have pulled the continents into place with ropes. It is the master of the three legendary titans.

# 487. Giratina
## Look
A massive, draconic, legendary Pokémon with two forms: Altered and Origin. Its Altered Form is a six-legged, centipede-like creature with a gray, metallic body. Its Origin Form is a serpentine, ethereal creature.
## Lore
Giratina, the Renegade Pokémon, is a legendary creature that was banished to the Distortion World for its violence. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 488. Cresselia
## Look
A legendary, swan-like Pokémon with a blue and yellow body. It has a crescent-moon-shaped crest on its head and two beautiful, wing-like appendages.
## Lore
Cresselia, the Lunar Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to bring beautiful dreams. Its feathers are said to be a charm against nightmares.

# 489. Phione
## Look
A mythical, sea-angel-like Pokémon. It has a blue, spherical body and a single, large antenna on its head.
## Lore
Phione, the Sea Drifter Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is born in warm seas. It is known to drift in the ocean, and it will always return to the place where it was born.

# 490. Manaphy
## Look
A mythical, sea-angel-like Pokémon. It has a blue, spherical body and two long, antenna-like appendages. It has a red gem on its chest.
## Lore
Manaphy, the Seafaring Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is born with a special power that allows it to bond with any Pokémon. It is a very rare and mysterious Pokémon.

# 491. Darkrai
## Look
A mythical, shadow-like Pokémon with a dark, ethereal body. It has a long, white, flowing "hair" and a red, jagged collar around its neck.
## Lore
Darkrai, the Pitch-Black Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is known to cause nightmares. It is not malicious, but its presence can cause people and Pokémon to have terrible dreams.

# 492. Shaymin
## Look
A mythical Pokémon with two forms: Land and Sky. Its Land Forme is a small, hedgehog-like creature with a grassy coat and two pink flowers on its head. Its Sky Forme is a sleek, deer-like creature with a pair of large, petal-like wings.
## Lore
Shaymin, the Gratitude Pokémon, is a mythical creature that has the power to purify polluted land. It is a very gentle and kind Pokémon.

# 493. Arceus
## Look
A mythical, equine-like Pokémon with a white body and a golden, cross-like wheel on its back. It has a majestic and powerful appearance. It is said to be the creator of the Pokémon world.
## Lore
Arceus, the Alpha Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is said to have been born from an egg in a vortex of chaos before the universe existed. It is said to have created the entire Pokémon world with its thousand arms. It is the most powerful of all Pokémon.

# 494. Victini
## Look
A small, bipedal Pokémon with a rabbit-like appearance. It has large, pointed ears that form the letter "V," cream-colored fur, and a peachy-orange head and hands. It has large, blue eyes and small, fanged teeth. Two small, wing-like appendages grow from its lower back. It is a very energetic and cheerful-looking creature.
## Lore
Victini, the Victory Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is said to bring victory to any Trainer who befriends it. It can create an infinite amount of energy within its body, which it can then share with those it touches. It is a very rare Pokémon that is said to only appear before those who have a pure heart and a strong desire to win.

# 495. Snivy
## Look
A reptilian, snake-like Pokémon with a primarily green and cream-colored body. It has a large, three-pronged leaf at the tip of its tail. It stands on two small legs but has two leaf-shaped arms that it holds behind its back. Its expression is typically calm, arrogant, and aloof.
## Lore
Snivy, the Grass Snake Pokémon, is a creature known for its intelligence and calm demeanor. The leaf on its tail is capable of photosynthesis, which makes it more energetic when it bathes in sunlight. It is a very agile Pokémon and can move with incredible speed. It is known to be a bit of a show-off and will often look down on its opponents.

# 496. Servine
## Look
The evolved form of Snivy, Servine is a longer, more serpentine Pokémon. It has a sleek, green body with a crest of leaves on its head and back. Its arms have become small, vestigial appendages. It moves with a swift, slithering grace, and its expression is one of confident superiority.
## Lore
Servine, the Grass Snake Pokémon, is a master of hiding in dense vegetation. It moves with a speed that is difficult to follow, as if it were gliding across the ground. It uses its sharp leaves to attack its foes and will often use its speed to confuse and outmaneuver them. It is a very proud Pokémon.

# 497. Serperior
## Look
The final evolution, Serperior is a majestic, regal, serpentine Pokémon. It has a long, elegant body with a green, white, and yellow coloration. It has two large, leaf-like appendages on its collar that resemble a royal mantle. Its head is held high, and its expression is noble and intimidating.
## Lore
Serperior, the Regal Pokémon, is a creature of immense power and presence. It can stop its opponent's movements with just a single, intense glare. It is said that it will only give its all in battle for a Trainer that it deems worthy and is unfazed by powerful foes. It can amplify solar energy within its body, a power that rivals the legendary dragons.

# 498. Tepig
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, pig-like Pokémon. It has an orange body with black markings on its head and lower body. Its most notable feature is the red, ball-shaped orb at the tip of its coiled tail. It has a cheerful and energetic appearance.
## Lore
Tepig, the Fire Pig Pokémon, is a creature that can shoot fireballs from its snout. It loves to eat roasted berries, which it cooks itself using its own flames. The flame it shoots is a sign of its health; it will shoot black smoke if it is feeling unwell. It is a very nimble Pokémon and can dodge attacks with ease.

# 499. Pignite
## Look
The evolved form of Tepig, Pignite is a bipedal, pig-like Pokémon with a sturdy, sumo-wrestler-like build. It has a fiery crest on its head and a pattern on its chest that resembles a wrestling singlet.
## Lore
Pignite, the Fire Pig Pokémon, is a dual-type Fire/Fighting Pokémon. The intensity of the fire in its belly increases when it is in a pinch, making its attacks more powerful. It is a very dedicated Pokémon that trains relentlessly to master its fighting techniques.

# 500. Emboar
## Look
The final evolution, Emboar is a massive, bipedal, boar-like Pokémon with a fiery "beard" of flames around its neck and shoulders. It has a powerful and intimidating presence, with large, sharp tusks and a stocky, muscular build.
## Lore
Emboar, the Mega Fire Pig Pokémon, is a creature of immense power and passion. It can set its fists on fire to deliver a devastating flurry of fiery punches. It is a very caring Pokémon that is very protective of its friends. It is known for its incredible speed and its powerful, explosive attacks.

# 501. Oshawott
## Look
A small, bipedal, sea-otter-like Pokémon. It has a light blue body, a white head, and a small, shell-like object called a "scalchop" on its belly. It has a playful and somewhat naive expression.
## Lore
Oshawott, the Sea Otter Pokémon, uses the scalchop on its belly as its primary tool and weapon. It is made of the same material as its claws and can be used to break open hard berries and to fight. It is a very resourceful Pokémon that will always find a way to use its scalchop to its advantage.

# 502. Dewott
## Look
The evolved form of Oshawott, Dewott is a bipedal, sea-otter-like Pokémon with a more mature and disciplined appearance. It now carries two scalchops on its hips, which it wields like a samurai's dual blades.
## Lore
Dewott, the Discipline Pokémon, is a creature that has mastered the art of using its two scalchops. It is said that each Dewott has its own unique style of fighting. It is a very dedicated Pokémon that trains relentlessly to perfect its skills.

# 503. Samurott
## Look
The final evolution, Samurott is a large, quadrupedal, sea-lion-like Pokémon with a regal and powerful presence. It has a dark blue, armored hide and a helmet-like shell on its head. It has two large, sword-like seamitars sheathed in its forelegs.
## Lore
Samurott, the Formidable Pokémon, is a creature that can silence its foes with a single glare. It can unsheathe the seamitars on its forelegs in an instant to attack its foes. It is a very powerful and honorable Pokémon that is a master of swordsmanship.

# 504. Patrat
## Look
A small, bipedal, rodent-like Pokémon that resembles a meerkat. It has a brown body, a cream-colored face, and large, red eyes with a yellow sclera. It is almost always seen standing on its hind legs, on the lookout.
## Lore
Patrat, the Scout Pokémon, is a creature that is always on guard. It is known to store food in its cheek pouches and then stand guard for days on end. It is a very cautious and alert Pokémon. It will use its tail to communicate with others.

# 505. Watchog
## Look
The evolved form of Patrat, Watchog is a taller, more slender, meerkat-like Pokémon. It has a series of yellow, luminescent stripes on its body. It has a serious and vigilant expression.
## Lore
Watchog, the Lookout Pokémon, is a creature that has the ability to make the patterns on its body glow. It uses this to intimidate its foes and to communicate with its allies. It has incredible eyesight and can see in the dark.

# 506. Lillipup
## Look
A small, canine Pokémon that resembles a Yorkshire Terrier. It has a scruffy, brown and cream-colored coat that covers its eyes. It has a very cute and friendly appearance.
## Lore
Lillipup, the Puppy Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its bravery and its loyalty. It is a very intelligent Pokémon and can learn a variety of different commands. It is a very popular pet.

# 507. Herdier
## Look
The evolved form of Lillipup, Herdier is a larger and more robust terrier-like Pokémon. It has a shaggy, black "cape" of fur on its back. It has a very loyal and dependable appearance.
## Lore
Herdier, the Loyal Dog Pokémon, is a creature that has been a partner to humans for centuries. It is known for its incredible loyalty and its willingness to help its Trainer. It is a very powerful and reliable Pokémon.

# 508. Stoutland
## Look
The final evolution, Stoutland is a massive and majestic, St. Bernard-like Pokémon. It has a long, flowing, mustache-like mane of fur. It has a very kind and gentle appearance.
## Lore
Stoutland, the Big-Hearted Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible kindness and its heroic nature. It is often used as a rescue Pokémon in cold, snowy regions. It is a very powerful and reliable partner.

# 509. Purrloin
## Look
A small, feline Pokémon with a purple coat. It has a mischievous and cunning expression. It is almost always seen with a playful, devious grin.
## Lore
Purrloin, the Devious Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its thieving habits. It will often steal things from people just for the fun of it. It is a very cunning and tricky Pokémon.

# 510. Liepard
## Look
The evolved form of Purrloin, Liepard is a sleek and graceful, leopard-like Pokémon. It has a purple coat with yellow, rosette-like spots. It is a very beautiful but dangerous-looking creature.
## Lore
Liepard, the Cruel Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible speed and its beautiful, sleek body. It is a very powerful and ruthless predator.

# 511. Pansage
## Look
A small, monkey-like Pokémon with a green and cream-colored coat. It has a tuft of leaves on its head that resembles a broccoli floret. It has a cheerful and friendly appearance.
## Lore
Pansage, the Grass Monkey Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its skill at finding berries. It is said that the leaves on its head can cure fatigue.

# 512. Simisage
## Look
The evolved form of Pansage, Simisage is a bipedal, monkey-like Pokémon with a large, pompadour-like crest of leaves on its head. It has a cool and confident expression.
## Lore
Simisage, the Thorn Monkey Pokémon, is a creature that can attack its foes with the thorny vines that grow from its tail. It is a very powerful and agile Pokémon.

# 513. Pansear
## Look
A small, monkey-like Pokémon with a red and cream-colored coat. It has a tuft of fire-like fur on its head. It has a cheerful and energetic appearance.
## Lore
Pansear, the High Temp Pokémon, is a creature that can roast berries with the fire from its head. The tuft on its head can reach temperatures of over 600 degrees Fahrenheit.

# 514. Simisear
## Look
The evolved form of Pansear, Simisear is a bipedal, monkey-like Pokémon with a large, plume-of-fire-like crest on its head. It is always seen with a wide, cheerful grin.
## Lore
Simisear, the Ember Pokémon, is a creature that can scatter embers from its head and tail to scorch its foes. It is a very powerful and energetic Pokémon.

# 515. Panpour
## Look
A small, monkey-like Pokémon with a blue and cream-colored coat. It has a tuft of water-like fur on its head that resembles a fountain. It has a cheerful and playful appearance.
## Lore
Panpour, the Spray Pokémon, is a creature that can store water in the tuft on its head. It can then shoot this water out with great force.

# 516. Simipour
## Look
The evolved form of Panpour, Simipour is a bipedal, monkey-like Pokémon with a long, flowing, dreadlock-like crest of water-like fur on its head.
## Lore
Simipour, the Geyser Pokémon, is a creature that can shoot powerful jets of water from its tail. The water is so powerful that it can shatter rock.

# 517. Munna
## Look
A small, tapir-like Pokémon with a pink, floral-patterned body. It is almost always seen floating in the air, asleep.
## Lore
Munna, the Dream Eater Pokémon, is a creature that feeds on the dreams of people and Pokémon. It is said that if it eats a good dream, it will release a pink, dream-like mist.

# 518. Musharna
## Look
The evolved form of Munna, Musharna is a larger and more powerful, tapir-like Pokémon. It is always seen sleeping and has a trail of dream mist emanating from its forehead.
## Lore
Musharna, the Drowsing Pokémon, is a creature that can make the dreams of others a reality. The dream mist it releases contains the dreams it has eaten.

# 519. Pidove
## Look
A small, pigeon-like Pokémon with a gray and black plumage. It has a heart-shaped marking on its chest and a cheerful, friendly expression.
## Lore
Pidove, the Tiny Pigeon Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its poor memory. It is a very friendly and social Pokémon that is not afraid of people.

# 520. Tranquill
## Look
The evolved form of Pidove, Tranquill is a larger and more elegant pigeon Pokémon. It has a sleek, gray and black plumage and a confident, determined look.
## Lore
Tranquill, the Wild Pigeon Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible sense of direction. It is said that it can always find its way back to its Trainer, no matter how far away it may be.

# 521. Unfezant
## Look
The final evolution, Unfezant has two different forms for males and females. The male has a large, red, fleshy wattle that covers its face, while the female has a more plain, gray head. Both are powerful and majestic-looking birds.
## Lore
Unfezant, the Proud Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible flying speed. The males are very proud of their beautiful head crests and will use them to attract a mate.

# 522. Blitzle
## Look
A small, zebra-like Pokémon with a black coat and a series of white, lightning-bolt-shaped stripes. It has a spiky, electric-blue mane.
## Lore
Blitzle, the Electrified Pokémon, is a creature that can generate electricity in its mane. When it is startled, it will release a flash of light to blind its foes.

# 523. Zebstrika
## Look
The evolved form of Blitzle, Zebstrika is a larger and more powerful zebra Pokémon. It has a sleek, black and white coat and a massive, spiky, electric mane.
## Lore
Zebstrika, the Thunderbolt Pokémon, is a creature that can run at the speed of lightning. It is a very powerful and unpredictable Pokémon.

# 524. Roggenrola
## Look
A small, geode-like Pokémon. It has a blue, rocky body with a single, large, hexagonal, ear-like cavity.
## Lore
Roggenrola, the Mantle Pokémon, is a creature that was born deep underground. It has a special energy core in its body. It is a very sturdy and resilient Pokémon.

# 525. Boldore
## Look
The evolved form of Roggenrola, Boldore is a larger and more powerful, crystalline Pokémon. It has a series of orange, energy-filled crystals protruding from its body.
## Lore
Boldore, the Ore Pokémon, is a creature whose energy crystals are so bright that they can be seen from a great distance. It is said that if its crystals are glowing, it is a sign that it is about to evolve.

# 526. Gigalith
## Look
The final evolution, Gigalith is a massive, quadrupedal, crystalline Pokémon. Its body is covered in a series of massive, red, energy-filled crystals.
## Lore
Gigalith, the Compressed Pokémon, is a creature that can absorb solar energy and fire it as a powerful beam from the crystals on its body. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 527. Woobat
## Look
A small, bat-like Pokémon with a blue, fluffy body. It has a single, heart-shaped nose that it uses to stick to cave walls.
## Lore
Woobat, the Bat Pokémon, is a creature that lives in dark caves. It is said that if a Woobat leaves a heart-shaped mark on you, it is a sign of good luck.

# 528. Swoobat
## Look
The evolved form of Woobat, Swoobat is a larger and more elegant bat Pokémon. It has a heart-shaped nose and a large, fluffy tail.
## Lore
Swoobat, the Courting Pokémon, is a creature that can produce ultrasonic waves that can shatter rock. It is a very powerful and elegant Pokémon.

# 529. Drilbur
## Look
A small, mole-like Pokémon with a blue and black body. It has two massive, drill-like claws.
## Lore
Drilbur, the Mole Pokémon, is a creature that can dig through the ground at a speed of 30 miles per hour. It is a very powerful and efficient digger.

# 530. Excadrill
## Look
The evolved form of Drilbur, Excadrill is a larger and more powerful, mole-like Pokémon. Its head and claws have fused to form a massive, powerful drill.
## Lore
Excadrill, the Subterrene Pokémon, is a creature that can dig through a steel plate. It is known to be a very helpful Pokémon and will often help with construction projects.

# 531. Audino
## Look
A bipedal, fairy-like Pokémon with a pink and cream-colored coat. It has two large, stethoscope-like feelers on its ears.
## Lore
Audino, the Hearing Pokémon, is a creature that can use the feelers on its ears to hear the heartbeats of other living things. It can use this power to tell if someone is sick or if an egg is about to hatch.

# 532. Timburr
## Look
A small, humanoid Pokémon with a gray body. It is always seen carrying a large, wooden log.
## Lore
Timburr, the Muscular Pokémon, is a creature that is always carrying a log to train its muscles. It is said that if it can carry a log without any trouble, it is a sign that it is about to evolve.

# 533. Gurdurr
## Look
The evolved form of Timburr, Gurdurr is a larger and more muscular, humanoid Pokémon. It is always seen carrying a large, steel I-beam.
## Lore
Gurdurr, the Muscular Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength. It is said that a group of Gurdurr can build a house in a single day.

# 534. Conkeldurr
## Look
The final evolution, Conkeldurr is a massive and powerful, humanoid Pokémon. It has two massive, concrete pillars that it uses as crutches.
## Lore
Conkeldurr, the Muscular Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have taught humans the art of making concrete. It is a very powerful and wise Pokémon.

# 535. Tympole
## Look
A small, tadpole-like Pokémon with a large, round head. It has two speaker-like bumps on its cheeks.
## Lore
Tympole, the Tadpole Pokémon, is a creature that communicates by vibrating its cheeks to create a sound that is too high-pitched for humans to hear.

# 536. Palpitoad
## Look
The evolved form of Tympole, Palpitoad is a bipedal, toad-like Pokémon. It has two large, speaker-like bumps on its head.
## Lore
Palpitoad, the Vibration Pokémon, is a creature that can create powerful vibrations by croaking. The vibrations are so powerful that they can cause earthquakes.

# 537. Seismitoad
## Look
The final evolution, Seismitoad is a massive and powerful, toad-like Pokémon. It has a series of large, speaker-like bumps all over its body.
## Lore
Seismitoad, the Vibration Pokémon, is a creature that can create powerful vibrations by using the bumps on its body. The vibrations are so powerful that they can shatter rock.

# 538. Throh
## Look
A humanoid Pokémon with a red, rocky body. It is always seen wearing a white, karate-like gi.
## Lore
Throh, the Judo Pokémon, is a creature that is a master of judo. It is said that a group of five Throh can tie a knot in a river.

# 539. Sawk
## Look
A humanoid Pokémon with a blue, rocky body. It is always seen wearing a black, karate-like gi.
## Lore
Sawk, the Karate Pokémon, is a creature that is a master of karate. It is said that a single Sawk can split the ocean with a single chop.

# 540. Sewaddle
## Look
A small, caterpillar-like Pokémon with a green, leaf-like cloak.
## Lore
Sewaddle, the Sewing Pokémon, is a creature that is very skilled at sewing. It can make a cloak for itself out of leaves in a single night.

# 541. Swadloon
## Look
The evolved form of Sewaddle, Swadloon is a Pokémon that is completely wrapped in a cloak of leaves.
## Lore
Swadloon, the Leaf-Wrapped Pokémon, is a creature that is very shy and reclusive. It is almost always seen hiding in its cloak of leaves.

# 542. Leavanny
## Look
The final evolution, Leavanny is a bipedal, insectoid Pokémon with a body that is a mix of a praying mantis and a leaf. It has two large, leaf-like arms that it uses to cut leaves.
## Lore
Leavanny, the Nurturing Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its motherly nature. It is known to make clothes out of leaves for smaller Pokémon.

# 543. Venipede
## Look
A small, centipede-like Pokémon with a purple and green body. It has a pair of large, feeler-like antennae and a sharp, poisonous stinger on its tail.
## Lore
Venipede, the Centipede Pokémon, is a creature that is very aggressive and will not hesitate to attack anything that moves. It is a very venomous Pokémon.

# 544. Whirlipede
## Look
The evolved form of Venipede, Whirlipede is a Pokémon that is completely encased in a hard, tire-like shell.
## Lore
Whirlipede, the Curlipede Pokémon, is a creature that moves by rolling its body like a wheel. It can move at speeds of over 60 miles per hour.

# 545. Scolipede
## Look
The final evolution, Scolipede is a massive and intimidating, centipede-like Pokémon. It has a series of sharp, poisonous claws on its neck.
## Lore
Scolipede, the Megapede Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible speed and its powerful, poisonous attacks. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 546. Cottonee
## Look
A small, fairy-like Pokémon with a body that is a mix of a sheep and a cotton ball. It has two large, leaf-like ears.
## Lore
Cottonee, the Cotton Puff Pokémon, is a creature that can be seen floating on the wind. It is said that when a large group of Cottonee gather, it is a sign that spring is coming.

# 547. Whimsicott
## Look
The evolved form of Cottonee, Whimsicott is a bipedal, fairy-like Pokémon with a large, cotton-like mane.
## Lore
Whimsicott, the Windveiled Pokémon, is a creature that can slip through any gap, no matter how small. It is a very mischievous Pokémon that loves to play pranks.

# 548. Petilil
## Look
A small, plant-like Pokémon that resembles a tulip bulb. It has a green, leaf-like crest on its head.
## Lore
Petilil, the Bulb Pokémon, is a creature that is known for the leaves on its head, which are said to have healing properties.

# 549. Lilligant
## Look
The evolved form of Petilil, Lilligant is a graceful and elegant, humanoid, plant-like Pokémon. It has a large, beautiful flower on its head.
## Lore
Lilligant, the Flowering Pokémon, is a creature that is very popular among high-class celebrities. The flower on its head releases a sweet, relaxing aroma.

# 550. Basculin
## Look
A fish Pokémon with two different forms: Red-Striped and Blue-Striped. Both are very aggressive and have powerful jaws.
## Lore
Basculin, the Hostile Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its extremely violent nature. The Red-Striped and Blue-Striped forms are always fighting with each other.

# 551. Sandile
## Look
A small, crocodile-like Pokémon with a brown and black body. It has a pair of large, goggle-like eyes. It is almost always seen with its snout sticking out of the sand.
## Lore
Sandile, the Desert Croc Pokémon, is a creature that lives in deserts. It spends most of its time with its body submerged in the sand to avoid the heat.

# 552. Krokorok
## Look
The evolved form of Sandile, Krokorok is a larger and more powerful, crocodile-like Pokémon. It has a special membrane that covers its eyes to protect them from sandstorms.
## Lore
Krokorok, the Desert Croc Pokémon, is a creature that is known to live in small packs. They are very skilled hunters and will work together to take down prey much larger than themselves.

# 553. Krookodile
## Look
The final evolution, Krookodile is a massive and powerful, bipedal, crocodile-like Pokémon. It has a powerful jaw that can crush a car.
## Lore
Krookodile, the Intimidation Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible eyesight. It can see things that are over 30 miles away. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 554. Darumaka
## Look
A small, round, doll-like Pokémon that resembles a daruma doll. It has a red body and a perpetually cheerful expression.
## Lore
Darumaka, the Zen Charm Pokémon, is a creature that is known to be a symbol of good luck. It is said that if you fill in one of its eyes, your wish will come true.

# 555. Darmanitan
## Look
The evolved form of Darumaka, Darmanitan has two forms: Standard and Zen. Its Standard Mode is a large, ape-like Pokémon with a fiery crest on its head. Its Zen Mode is a blue, statue-like Pokémon.
## Lore
Darmanitan, the Blazing Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength. It can destroy a car with a single punch. When it is injured, it enters a meditative state known as Zen Mode.

# 556. Maractus
## Look
A cactus-like Pokémon that resembles a maraca. It has a green body and two large, flower-like appendages on its head.
## Lore
Maractus, the Cactus Pokémon, is a creature that can create a rhythmic, maraca-like sound by shaking its body. It uses this sound to scare away predators.

# 557. Dwebble
## Look
A small, hermit-crab-like Pokémon. It has an orange, crab-like body and carries a large, hollowed-out rock on its back.
## Lore
Dwebble, the Rock Inn Pokémon, is a creature that is very skilled at finding a suitable home. If its rock is broken, it will secrete a liquid from its mouth that can melt a new hole in a rock.

# 558. Crustle
## Look
The evolved form of Dwebble, Crustle is a larger and more powerful, crab-like Pokémon. It carries a massive, layered slab of rock on its back.
## Lore
Crustle, the Stone Home Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength. It can carry a rock that is over 100 times its own weight. It is a very powerful and territorial Pokémon.

# 559. Scraggy
## Look
A bipedal, reptilian Pokémon that is always seen holding up its loose, yellow, shed skin like a pair of baggy pants.
## Lore
Scraggy, the Shedding Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its aggressive and territorial nature. It will headbutt anything that moves.

# 560. Scrafty
## Look
The evolved form of Scraggy, Scrafty is a bipedal, reptilian Pokémon with a large, mohawk-like crest. It is always seen holding up its loose, yellow skin.
## Lore
Scrafty, the Hoodlum Pokémon, is a creature that is known to live in small gangs. They are very territorial and will attack any intruder that comes near their turf.

# 561. Sigilyph
## Look
A bizarre, avian Pokémon with a round, black body and a single, large, red eye. It has two wing-like appendages with a colorful, Nazca-line-like pattern.
## Lore
Sigilyph, the Avianoid Pokémon, is a mysterious creature that was said to have been the guardian of an ancient city. It is a very powerful and psychic Pokémon.

# 562. Yamask
## Look
A ghostly Pokémon with a black, ethereal body. It is always seen carrying a mask that was once its face when it was human.
## Lore
Yamask, the Spirit Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be the spirit of a human who has passed away. It is known to cry when it looks at its mask, as it remembers its former life.

# 563. Cofagrigus
## Look
The evolved form of Yamask, Cofagrigus is a Pokémon that resembles a sarcophagus. It has four shadowy arms that it can extend from its body.
## Lore
Cofagrigus, the Coffin Pokémon, is a creature that is said to trap anyone who gets too close and turn them into a mummy. It is a very dangerous and feared Pokémon.

# 564. Tirtouga
## Look
A prehistoric, turtle-like Pokémon. It has a blue, reptilian body and a hard, rocky shell.
## Lore
Tirtouga, the Prototurtle Pokémon, is a creature that was resurrected from a fossil. It is an ancient Pokémon that lived in the seas of primordial times.

# 565. Carracosta
## Look
The evolved form of Tirtouga, Carracosta is a massive and powerful, bipedal, turtle-like Pokémon. It has a powerful jaw that can crush rock.
## Lore
Carracosta, the Prototurtle Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength. It can survive both on land and in the water. It is a very powerful and formidable predator.

# 566. Archen
## Look
A prehistoric, avian Pokémon that resembles an archaeopteryx. It has a colorful, reptilian body and two small, feathered wings.
## Lore
Archen, the First Bird Pokémon, is a creature that was resurrected from a fossil. It is the ancestor of all modern bird Pokémon. It is not a very good flyer and is known to be very clumsy.

# 567. Archeops
## Look
The evolved form of Archen, Archeops is a larger and more powerful, archaeopteryx-like Pokémon. It has two large, feathered wings that it can use to fly.
## Lore
Archeops, the First Bird Pokémon, is a creature that is a much better flyer than its pre-evolution. It is known to hunt in flocks and is a very intelligent Pokémon.

# 568. Trubbish
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a green, leaky garbage bag. It has two large, oval eyes and a small, fanged mouth.
## Lore
Trubbish, the Trash Bag Pokémon, is a creature that was born from a chemical reaction between industrial waste and a discarded garbage bag. It is known to follow people who litter.

# 569. Garbodor
## Look
The evolved form of Trubbish, Garbodor is a massive pile of garbage that has come to life. It has two large, arm-like appendages made of trash.
## Lore
Garbodor, the Trash Heap Pokémon, is a creature that can absorb garbage into its body to make itself larger. It can also spray a poisonous liquid from its fingertips.

# 570. Zorua
## Look
A small, fox-like Pokémon with a gray and black coat. It has a mischievous and playful expression. It can create illusions.
## Lore
Zorua, the Tricky Fox Pokémon, is a creature that can change its form to look like other people and Pokémon. It is a very mischievous Pokémon that loves to play pranks.

# 571. Zoroark
## Look
The evolved form of Zorua, Zoroark is a bipedal, fox-like Pokémon with a long, flowing, red and black mane.
## Lore
Zoroark, the Illusion Fox Pokémon, is a creature that is a master of illusions. It can create illusions that are so realistic that they are indistinguishable from reality. It is a very powerful and protective Pokémon.

# 572. Minccino
## Look
A small, chinchilla-like Pokémon with a gray coat. It has a large, fluffy tail that it uses to clean things.
## Lore
Minccino, the Chinchilla Pokémon, is a creature that is obsessed with cleanliness. It is always cleaning its tail and will use it to clean anything that is dirty.

# 573. Cinccino
## Look
The evolved form of Minccino, Cinccino is a larger and more elegant, chinchilla-like Pokémon. It has a long, white, scarf-like boa of fur.
## Lore
Cinccino, the Scarf Pokémon, is a creature whose fur is so soft that it is said to be able to deflect any attack. It is a very popular Pokémon among celebrities.

# 574. Gothita
## Look
A small, humanoid Pokémon with a gothic-lolita-like appearance. It has a black, bow-like appendage on its head and a series of white bows on its body.

Gothita, the Fixation Pokémon, is a creature that is known to stare intently at things. It is said that it is trying to see the future.

# 575. Gothorita
## Look
The evolved form of Gothita, Gothorita is a taller, more elegant, gothic-lolita-like Pokémon.
## Lore
Gothorita, the Manipulate Pokémon, is a creature that can use its psychic powers to manipulate the stars. It is said that it can create a map of the stars with its starlight.

# 576. Gothitelle
## Look
The final evolution, Gothitelle is a tall and powerful, gothic-lolita-like Pokémon. It has a massive, bow-like appendage on its head and a series of floating, ribbon-like appendages.
## Lore
Gothitelle, the Astral Body Pokémon, is a creature that can see the lifespan of a person. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 577. Solosis
## Look
A small, cell-like Pokémon. It has a green, gelatinous body with a single, dark nucleus.
## Lore
Solosis, the Cell Pokémon, is a creature that communicates with others by using telepathy. It is a very intelligent Pokémon.

# 578. Duosion
## Look
The evolved form of Solosis, Duosion is a Pokémon that is composed of two Solosis that have fused together.
## Lore
Duosion, the Mitosis Pokémon, is a creature that has two brains that are linked together. It is said that if its two brains think of the same thing at the same time, its psychic powers will be amplified.

# 579. Reuniclus
## Look
The final evolution, Reuniclus is a Pokémon that is composed of three Solosis that have fused together. It has two large, arm-like appendages made of a gelatinous substance.
## Lore
Reuniclus, the Multiplying Pokémon, is a creature that can create a network of its brains to increase its psychic powers. It is a very powerful and intelligent Pokémon.

# 580. Ducklett
## Look
A small, duck-like Pokémon with a blue body and a small, white tuft of feathers on its head.
## Lore
Ducklett, the Water Bird Pokémon, is a creature that is a much better diver than it is a flyer. It is known to coat its feathers in a special oil to keep them from getting wet.

# 581. Swanna
## Look
The evolved form of Ducklett, Swanna is a graceful and elegant, swan-like Pokémon. It has a long, slender neck and two large, powerful wings.
## Lore
Swanna, the White Bird Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful, dance-like movements. It is said that the leader of the flock is the one with the most beautiful dance.

# 582. Vanillite
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a small, vanilla ice cream cone. It has a blue, crystalline body and a small, white, snow-like crest on its head.
## Lore
Vanillite, the Fresh Snow Pokémon, is a creature that was born from an icicle that was bathed in the morning sun. It is a very rare Pokémon.

# 583. Vanillish
## Look
The evolved form of Vanillite, Vanillish is a Pokémon that resembles a larger, more detailed ice cream cone.
## Lore
Vanillish, the Icy Snow Pokémon, is a creature that can create a fine, diamond-dust-like snow by freezing the moisture in the air.

# 584. Vanilluxe
## Look
The final evolution, Vanilluxe is a Pokémon that resembles two ice cream cones that have fused together. It has two large, straw-like appendages on its heads.
## Lore
Vanilluxe, the Snowstorm Pokémon, is a creature that can create a massive blizzard by swallowing a large amount of water and then freezing it.

# 585. Deerling
## Look
A small, deer-like Pokémon with four different forms, depending on the season: Spring (pink), Summer (green), Autumn (orange), and Winter (brown).
## Lore
Deerling, the Season Pokémon, is a creature whose appearance changes with the seasons. It is a very gentle and timid Pokémon.

# 586. Sawsbuck
## Look
The evolved form of Deerling, Sawsbuck is a larger and more majestic, deer-like Pokémon. It also has four different forms, depending on the season. Its antlers will have different foliage based on the season.
## Lore
Sawsbuck, the Season Pokémon, is a creature that is known as the "harbinger of spring." It is said that when it changes its form, it is a sign that the seasons are changing.

## Look
A small, rodent-like Pokémon that resembles a flying squirrel. It has a white and black coat and two yellow, electrical cheek pouches.

Emolga, the Sky Squirrel Pokémon, is a creature that can glide through the air by using the membrane between its arms and legs. It can also discharge electricity from its cheek pouches to shock its foes.

# 588. Karrablast
## Look
A small, beetle-like Pokémon with a blue, armored shell. It is the rival of Shelmet.
## Lore
Karrablast, the Clamping Pokémon, is a mysterious creature that is said to evolve when it is exposed to the electrical energy of a Shelmet.

# 589. Escavalier
## Look
The evolved form of Karrablast, Escavalier is a bipedal, knight-like Pokémon. It is encased in a suit of steel armor and wields two large, lance-like appendages.
## Lore
Escavalier, the Cavalry Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible speed and its powerful, jousting-like attacks. It is a very powerful and honorable Pokémon.

# 590. Foongus
## Look
A small, mushroom-like Pokémon with a Poké-Ball-like pattern on its cap.

Foongus, the Mushroom Pokémon, is a creature that lures in its prey by using the Poké-Ball-like pattern on its cap. It will then release a poisonous powder to immobilize its foes.

# 591. Amoonguss
## Look
The evolved form of Foongus, Amoonguss is a larger and more powerful, mushroom-like Pokémon. It has two large, Poké-Ball-like shields for hands.

Amoonguss, the Mushroom Pokémon, is a creature that is known to dance in a strange, rhythmic manner. It is a very dangerous Pokémon that will not hesitate to attack anything that comes near it.

# 592. Frillish
## Look
A jellyfish-like Pokémon with two different forms for males and females. The male is blue and has a crown-like crest, while the female is pink and has a more frilly appearance.
## Lore
Frillish, the Floating Pokémon, is a creature that lives in the deep ocean. It is known to paralyze its prey with its poisonous tentacles and then drag them down to its lair.

# 593. Jellicent
## Look
The evolved form of Frillish, Jellicent is a larger and more powerful, jellyfish-like Pokémon. The male has a large, mustache-like appendage, while the female has a more regal, queen-like appearance.
## Lore
Jellicent, the Floating Pokémon, is a creature that is known as the "king of the sea." It is said that it can create a massive whirlpool by spinning its body.

# 594. Alomomola
## Look
A fish-like Pokémon with a pink, heart-shaped body. It has a gentle and caring appearance.
## Lore
Alomomola, the Caring Pokémon, is a creature that is known to heal injured Pokémon. It has a special membrane that can be used to heal wounds.

# 595. Joltik
## Look
A very small, insectoid Pokémon that resembles a tick. It has a yellow, furry body and four small, blue eyes.
## Lore
Joltik, the Attaching Pokémon, is a creature that is so small that it cannot generate its own electricity. It will attach itself to larger Pokémon and feed on their static electricity.

# 596. Galvantula
## Look
The evolved form of Joltik, Galvantula is a large, tarantula-like Pokémon. It has a yellow and blue body and can shoot electrified webs from its mouth.
## Lore
Galvantula, the EleSpider Pokémon, is a creature that can create a web of electrified silk to trap its prey. It is a very cunning and dangerous predator.

# 597. Ferroseed
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a metallic, spiky seed pod. It can attach itself to cave walls with its spikes.
## Lore
Ferroseed, the Thorn Seed Pokémon, is a creature that can absorb minerals from the rocks it attaches to. It will then fire its spikes at any foe that comes near it.

# 598. Ferrothorn
## Look
The evolved form of Ferroseed, Ferrothorn is a large, metallic, durian-like Pokémon. It has three long, whip-like appendages with mace-like tips.
## Lore
Ferrothorn, the Thorn Pod Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible defensive power. It can attach itself to the ceiling of a cave and then drop down on unsuspecting prey.

# 599. Klink
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles two small, interlocking gears.
## Lore
Klink, the Gear Pokémon, is a creature that is composed of two individual gears that are always spinning. It is said that if they stop spinning, it will lose its life force.

# 600. Klang
## Look
The evolved form of Klink, Klang is a Pokémon that is composed of three interlocking gears.
## Lore
Klang, the Gear Pokémon, is a creature that is a more complex version of Klink. The third gear is said to be the one that stores up energy.

# 601. Klinklang
## Look
The final evolution, Klinklang is a Pokémon that is composed of four interlocking gears. It has a large, red, energy-filled core.
## Lore
Klinklang, the Gear Pokémon, is a creature that can fire a powerful beam of energy from its red core. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 602. Tynamo
## Look
A small, eel-like Pokémon with a white body. It has a single, large, red eye.
## Lore
Tynamo, the EleFish Pokémon, is a creature that is so small that it can't generate its own electricity. It is known to travel in large schools.

# 603. Eelektrik
## Look
The evolved form of Tynamo, Eelektrik is a larger, more powerful, eel-like Pokémon. It can generate its own electricity.

Eelektrik, the EleFish Pokémon, is a creature that can wrap itself around its prey and then shock them with a powerful electrical charge.

# 604. Eelektross
## Look
The final evolution, Eelektross is a massive and powerful, eel-like Pokémon. It has two large, arm-like appendages with three-fingered claws.
## Lore
Eelektross, the EleFish Pokémon, is a creature that can crawl onto land to attack its prey. It is a very powerful and formidable predator.

# 605. Elgyem
## Look
A small, alien-like Pokémon with a large, bulbous head. It has two large, colorful eyes.
## Lore
Elgyem, the Cerebral Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have come from space. It has powerful psychic powers and can squeeze its foe's brain to give them a headache.

# 606. Beheeyem
## Look
The evolved form of Elgyem, Beheeyem is a taller and more powerful, alien-like Pokémon. It has three colorful fingers on each hand.
## Lore
Beheeyem, the Cerebral Pokémon, is a creature that can manipulate the memories of others. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 607. Litwick
## Look

Litwick, the Candle Pokémon, is a creature that is said to guide the spirits of the dead. It is known to absorb the life force of people and Pokémon.

## Look

Lampent, the Lamp Pokémon, is a creature that is known to hang around hospitals, waiting for people to pass away.

# 609. Chandelure
## Look

Chandelure, the Luring Pokémon, is a creature that can hypnotize its foes with its eerie, dancing flames. It is said that the spirits that are burned by its flames are doomed to wander the world forever.

## Look

## Look

## Look

Haxorus, the Axe Jaw Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredibly sharp and durable tusks. It can cut through a steel tower with a single swipe. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

## Look
A small, bear-cub-like Pokémon with a light blue coat. It has a single, large drop of snot hanging from its nose.

Cubchoo, the Chill Pokémon, is a creature that is always sick with a cold. The snot it produces is the source of its ice attacks.

## Look
The evolved form of Cubchoo, Beartic is a massive and powerful, polar-bear-like Pokémon. It has a long, beard-like icicle hanging from its chin.

Beartic, the Freezing Pokémon, is a creature that can freeze the moisture in the air to create sharp, icy fangs and claws.

# 615. Cryogonal
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a large, hexagonal snowflake made of ice.
## Lore
Cryogonal, the Crystallizing Pokémon, is a creature that is born in snow clouds. It can create a chain of its own kind to trap its prey.

# 616. Shelmet
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a snail. It has a gray, helmet-like shell. It is the rival of Karrablast.
## Lore
Shelmet, the Snail Pokémon, is a creature that is said to evolve when it is exposed to the electrical energy of a Karrablast.

## Look

Accelgor, the Shell Out Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible speed. It is said to be as fast as lightning.

## Look
A flat, fish-like Pokémon with a brown and yellow body. It has a perpetually dopey and vacant expression.

## Look
A small, weasel-like Pokémon with a martial-arts-like stance. It has a confident and determined expression.

Mienshao, the Martial Arts Pokémon, is a creature that uses the long fur on its arms as whips to attack its foes. It is a very powerful and graceful fighter.

## Look
A draconic, gargoyle-like Pokémon with a blue and red, rocky hide. It has a series of sharp, blade-like wings.

Druddigon, the Cave Pokémon, is a creature that lives in dark caves. It is known for its incredible strength and its sharp, durable claws.

## Look

Golett, the Automaton Pokémon, is a mysterious creature that was created by an ancient civilization. It is said that it can be brought to life by a mysterious energy.

## Look

Golurk, the Automaton Pokémon, is a creature that can fly through the air at the speed of Mach. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

A small, bipedal Pokémon with a body that is covered in sharp, blade-like appendages. It has a helmet-like crest on its head.

## Look

Bisharp, the Sword Blade Pokémon, is a creature that is the leader of a large pack of Pawniard. It is a very powerful and ruthless Pokémon that will do anything to win.

## Look
A bovine Pokémon that resembles a buffalo. It has a massive, afro-like mane of fur and two large, powerful horns.

Bouffalant, the Bash Buffalo Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible headbutting power. It can shatter a car with a single headbutt.

# 627. Rufflet
## Look
A small, eaglet-like Pokémon with a blue and white coat. It has a single, large, feather-like crest on its head.

Rufflet, the Eaglet Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible bravery. It will challenge any foe, no matter how large.

## Look
The evolved form of Rufflet, Braviary is a massive and majestic, eagle-like Pokémon. It has a large, headdress-like crest of red, white, and blue feathers.

Braviary, the Valiant Pokémon, is a creature that is known as the "hero of the sky." It is a very powerful and honorable Pokémon that will do anything to protect its friends.

## Look
A small, vulture-chick-like Pokémon. It has a pink, featherless body and a skull-like diaper that it wears.

Vullaby, the Diapered Pokémon, is a creature that is always looking for a suitable skull to wear. It is a very weak Pokémon and is often targeted by predators.

## Look

Mandibuzz, the Bone Vulture Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its cunning and its ruthless nature. It is a very powerful and formidable predator.

## Look
A Pokémon that resembles an anteater. It has a red and orange, furnace-like body and a long, whip-like tongue. It is the rival of Durant.

Heatmor, the Anteater Pokémon, is a creature that can breathe fire from its mouth. It is known to melt the steel exoskeletons of its prey, Durant.

# 632. Durant
## Look
An insectoid Pokémon that resembles an ant. It has a body that is covered in a suit of steel armor. It is the rival of Heatmor.

Durant, the Iron Ant Pokémon, is a creature that lives in large, complex colonies. They are known to be very territorial and will attack any intruder that comes near their nest.

# 633. Deino
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, draconic Pokémon with a blue body. It has a shaggy, black mane of fur that covers its eyes. It is always seen biting at things.

Deino, the Irate Pokémon, is a creature that is blind and will bite at anything that moves. It is a very aggressive and dangerous Pokémon.

# 634. Zweilous
## Look
The evolved form of Deino, Zweilous is a draconic Pokémon with two heads. The two heads are always fighting with each other.
