Zweilous, the Hostile Pokémon, is a creature that has two brains that are always in conflict. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 635. Hydreigon
## Look
The final evolution, Hydreigon is a massive and terrifying, three-headed, draconic Pokémon. It has six wings and a dark, malevolent appearance.
## Lore
Hydreigon, the Brutal Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible destructive power. It will attack anything that moves. It is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon.

# 636. Larvesta
## Look
A small, larval Pokémon with a white, furry body. It has five red, horn-like appendages on its head.
## Lore
Larvesta, the Torch Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have been born from the sun. It can shoot fire from its horns to defend itself.

# 637. Volcarona
## Look
The evolved form of Larvesta, Volcarona is a massive and majestic, moth-like Pokémon with six large, fiery wings.
## Lore
Volcarona, the Sun Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be able to replace the sun when it is covered by volcanic ash. It is a very powerful and legendary Pokémon.

# 638. Cobalion
## Look
A legendary, quadrupedal, goat-like Pokémon with a blue, metallic body. It is one of the Swords of Justice.
## Lore
Cobalion, the Iron Will Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is known for its calm and composed demeanor. It is the leader of the Swords of Justice.

# 639. Terrakion
## Look
A legendary, quadrupedal, ox-like Pokémon with a gray, rocky body. It is one of the Swords of Justice.
## Lore
Terrakion, the Cavern Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is known for its incredible strength. It is said that it can smash through a castle wall with a single charge.

# 640. Virizion
## Look
A legendary, quadrupedal, gazelle-like Pokémon with a green, leafy body. It is one of the Swords of Justice.
## Lore
Virizion, the Grassland Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is known for its incredible speed. It is said that it can move with the speed of the wind.

# 641. Tornadus
## Look
A legendary, genie-like Pokémon with two forms: Incarnate and Therian. Its Incarnate Forme is a green, muscular, humanoid creature that is always seen riding on a cloud. Its Therian Forme is a large, avian creature.
## Lore
Tornadus, the Cyclone Pokémon, is a legendary creature that can create massive storms by flapping its tail. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 642. Thundurus
## Look
A legendary, genie-like Pokémon with two forms: Incarnate and Therian. Its Incarnate Forme is a blue, muscular, humanoid creature that is always seen riding on a cloud. Its Therian Forme is a large, reptilian creature.
## Lore
Thundurus, the Bolt Strike Pokémon, is a legendary creature that can shoot bolts of lightning from its tail. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 643. Reshiram
## Look
A massive, draconic, legendary Pokémon with a white, feathery body. It has a long, flowing mane and two large, wing-like appendages. It represents the concept of truth.
## Lore
Reshiram, the Vast White Pokémon, is a legendary creature that can scorch the world with its fire. It is said that it will help those who seek to build a world of truth.

# 644. Zekrom
## Look
A massive, draconic, legendary Pokémon with a black, metallic body. It has a large, generator-like tail. It represents the concept of ideals.
## Lore
Zekrom, the Deep Black Pokémon, is a legendary creature that can scorch the world with its lightning. It is said that it will help those who seek to build a world of ideals.

# 645. Landorus
## Look
A legendary, genie-like Pokémon with two forms: Incarnate and Therian. Its Incarnate Forme is an orange, muscular, humanoid creature that is always seen riding on a cloud. Its Therian Forme is a large, feline creature.
## Lore
Landorus, the Abundance Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to bring bountiful harvests to the land. It is a very powerful and benevolent Pokémon.

# 646. Kyurem
## Look
A massive, draconic, legendary Pokémon with a gray, icy body. It has two large, ice-like wings and a powerful, generator-like tail. It can fuse with Reshiram or Zekrom.
## Lore
Kyurem, the Boundary Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to be an empty shell that is waiting to be filled with either truth or ideals. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 647. Keldeo
## Look
A mythical, colt-like Pokémon with two forms: Ordinary and Resolute. It has a blue and white coat and a single, large horn on its head. It is the fourth member of the Swords of Justice.
## Lore
Keldeo, the Colt Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is known for its incredible speed. It is said that it can run across the surface of the water.

# 648. Meloetta
## Look
A mythical, humanoid Pokémon with two forms: Aria and Pirouette. Its Aria Forme is a graceful, singer-like creature with long, flowing, green hair. Its Pirouette Forme is a nimble, dancer-like creature with orange hair.
## Lore
Meloetta, the Melody Pokémon, is a mythical creature that can sing beautiful melodies that can control the emotions of others. It is a very rare and mysterious Pokémon.

# 649. Genesect
## Look
A mythical, insectoid Pokémon with a purple, metallic body. It has a large, cannon-like appendage on its back that can be equipped with different drives.
## Lore
Genesect, the Paleozoic Pokémon, is a mythical creature that was revived from a fossil and then modified by Team Plasma. It is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon.

# 650. Chespin
## Look
A small, bipedal, mammalian Pokémon that resembles a hedgehog. It has a green, hood-like shell on its head and a brown, chipmunk-like body.
## Lore
Chespin, the Spiny Nut Pokémon, is a creature that has a hard, woody shell on its head that can protect it from most attacks. It is a very cheerful and optimistic Pokémon.

# 651. Quilladin
## Look
The evolved form of Chespin, Quilladin is a bipedal, armadillo-like Pokémon. It has a large, round, green, armored shell on its back.
## Lore
Quilladin, the Spiny Armor Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible defensive power. It can curl up into a ball to protect itself from attacks.

# 652. Chesnaught
## Look
The final evolution, Chesnaught is a massive and powerful, bipedal, mammalian Pokémon with a suit of spiky, green armor.
## Lore
Chesnaught, the Spiny Armor Pokémon, is a creature that can create a powerful shield by putting its arms together. It is a very powerful and protective Pokémon.

# 653. Fennekin
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, fox-like Pokémon with a yellow and orange coat. It has two large, flame-like tufts of fur in its ears.
## Lore
Fennekin, the Fox Pokémon, is a creature that can eat twigs to replenish its energy. It can also shoot hot air from its ears that can reach temperatures of over 390 degrees Fahrenheit.

# 654. Braixen
## Look
The evolved form of Fennekin, Braixen is a bipedal, fox-like Pokémon that resembles a witch. It has a stick in its tail that it can ignite to use as a wand.
## Lore
Braixen, the Fox Pokémon, is a creature that can use its fiery wand to send signals to its allies. It is a very intelligent and resourceful Pokémon.

# 655. Delphox
## Look
The final evolution, Delphox is a tall and elegant, bipedal, fox-like Pokémon that resembles a mage. It has a long, flowing, robe-like coat and a fiery wand in its hand.
## Lore
Delphox, the Fox Pokémon, is a creature that can see into the future by staring into the flame on its wand. It is a very powerful and psychic Pokémon.

# 656. Froakie
## Look
A small, frog-like Pokémon with a light blue body. It has a large, fluffy, cloud-like ruff of bubbles around its neck.
## Lore
Froakie, the Bubble Frog Pokémon, is a creature that can create a sticky, elastic bubble-like substance from its back. It can use this to protect itself from attacks.

# 657. Frogadier
## Look
The evolved form of Froakie, Frogadier is a more slender and agile, frog-like Pokémon. It has a dark blue body and a confident, ninja-like appearance.
## Lore
Frogadier, the Bubble Frog Pokémon, is a creature that can confuse its foes by jumping off of ceilings and walls. It is a very fast and agile Pokémon.

# 658. Greninja
## Look
The final evolution, Greninja is a tall and powerful, frog-like Pokémon with a ninja-like appearance. It has a long, scarf-like tongue that it wraps around its neck.
## Lore
Greninja, the Ninja Pokémon, is a creature that can create throwing stars out of compressed water. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 659. Bunnelby
## Look
A small, rabbit-like Pokémon with a brown and gray coat. It has two massive, powerful ears that it can use to dig.
## Lore
Bunnelby, the Digging Pokémon, is a creature that can dig a hole that is over 30 feet deep in a single night. It is a very industrious and hardworking Pokémon.

# 660. Diggersby
## Look
The evolved form of Bunnelby, Diggersby is a larger and more powerful, rabbit-like Pokémon. It has two massive, powerful ears that it can use to smash rock.
## Lore
Diggersby, the Digging Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength. It can easily lift an object that is over a ton.

# 661. Fletchling
## Look
A small, avian Pokémon that resembles a robin. It has a gray and orange plumage and a cheerful, friendly expression.
## Lore
Fletchling, the Tiny Robin Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful, melodic singing. It is a very friendly and social Pokémon.

# 662. Fletchinder
## Look
The evolved form of Fletchling, Fletchinder is a larger and more powerful, falcon-like Pokémon. It has a sleek, aerodynamic body and a fiery crest on its head.
## Lore
Fletchinder, the Ember Pokémon, is a creature that can shoot embers from its beak. It is a very fast and agile flyer.

# 663. Talonflame
## Look
The final evolution, Talonflame is a massive and powerful, falcon-like Pokémon. It has a fiery, red and orange plumage and a massive wingspan.
## Lore
Talonflame, the Scorching Pokémon, is a creature that can fly at speeds of over 310 miles per hour. It is a very powerful and formidable predator.

# 664. Scatterbug
## Look
A small, caterpillar-like Pokémon with a black, furry body. It has a single, large, pixelated eye.
## Lore
Scatterbug, the Scatterdust Pokémon, is a creature that can release a poisonous powder from its body to defend itself.

# 665. Spewpa
## Look
The evolved form of Scatterbug, Spewpa is a cocoon-like Pokémon with a white, furry body.
## Lore
Spewpa, the Scatterdust Pokémon, is a creature that is very durable and can withstand most attacks. It is preparing for its impending evolution.

# 666. Vivillon
## Look
The final evolution, Vivillon is a beautiful, butterfly-like Pokémon with a variety of different wing patterns, depending on the region it is from.
## Lore
Vivillon, the Scale Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful, vibrant wings. It is said that there are over 20 different patterns of Vivillon in the world.

# 667. Litleo
## Look
A small, lion-cub-like Pokémon with a brown and black coat. It has a small, red, mohawk-like tuft of fur on its head.
## Lore
Litleo, the Lion Cub Pokémon, is a creature that is very curious and playful. It is known to be very quick to start a fight.

# 668. Pyroar
## Look
The evolved form of Litleo, Pyroar has two different forms for males and females. The male has a massive, fiery, lion-like mane, while the female has a long, flowing, ponytail-like mane.
## Lore
Pyroar, the Royal Pokémon, is a creature that is the leader of a large pride of Litleo. The male with the largest mane of fire is the leader of the pack.

# 669. Flabébé
## Look
A very small, fairy-like Pokémon that is always seen holding onto a flower. It has a white, teardrop-shaped body and a pair of large, green eyes.
## Lore
Flabébé, the Single Bloom Pokémon, is a creature that is born without a flower. It will spend its life searching for a flower that it likes, and it will then care for it for its entire life.

# 670. Floette
## Look
The evolved form of Flabébé, Floette is a larger and more elegant, fairy-like Pokémon. It is always seen floating in the air, holding its flower.
## Lore
Floette, the Single Bloom Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful, dance-like movements. It is said that the flower it holds has healing properties.

# 671. Florges
## Look
The final evolution, Florges is a majestic and powerful, fairy-like Pokémon. It has a body that is a mix of a plant and a fairy. It has a large, bouquet-like crest of flowers on its head.
## Lore
Florges, the Garden Pokémon, is a creature that is known to claim beautiful flower gardens as its own territory. It is a very powerful and protective Pokémon.

# 672. Skiddo
## Look
A small, goat-like Pokémon with a brown and white coat. It has a leafy mane around its neck.
## Lore
Skiddo, the Mount Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its ability to understand the feelings of its rider. It is a very gentle and friendly Pokémon.

# 673. Gogoat
## Look
The evolved form of Skiddo, Gogoat is a larger and more powerful, goat-like Pokémon. It has a large, leafy mane and two massive, curved horns.
## Lore
Gogoat, the Mount Pokémon, is a creature that is very attuned to the feelings of its rider. It is said that it can tell what its rider is thinking just by the way they are holding onto its horns.

# 674. Pancham
## Look
A small, panda-like Pokémon with a playful and mischievous expression. It is always seen with a small, green leaf in its mouth.
## Lore
Pancham, the Playful Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its tough-guy attitude. It is always trying to look intimidating, but it is not very good at it.

# 675. Pangoro
## Look
The evolved form of Pancham, Pangoro is a massive and powerful, panda-like Pokémon. It has a dark, intimidating appearance and a bamboo stalk in its mouth.
## Lore
Pangoro, the Daunting Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its violent temper and its incredible strength. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 676. Furfrou
## Look
A poodle-like Pokémon with a white, fluffy coat. It can be groomed into a variety of different styles.
## Lore
Furfrou, the Poodle Pokémon, is a creature that is very proud of its beautiful coat. It is a very popular Pokémon among celebrities and is often seen in dog shows.

# 677. Espurr
## Look
A small, bipedal, feline Pokémon with a gray coat and a perpetually dazed and vacant expression. It has two large, folded ears that conceal its psychic organs.
## Lore
Espurr, the Restraint Pokémon, is a creature that has an incredible amount of psychic power that it cannot control. It has to keep its ears folded to keep its power from leaking out.

# 678. Meowstic
## Look
The evolved form of Espurr, Meowstic has two different forms for males and females. The male is blue and has a more supportive and defensive role, while the female is white and has a more aggressive and offensive role.
## Lore
Meowstic, the Constraint Pokémon, is a creature that can unleash a powerful psychic blast that can shatter a 10-ton truck. The male and female have different psychic abilities.

# 679. Honedge
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a medieval sword. It has a blue, ethereal, ribbon-like appendage that it uses as a hand.
## Lore
Honedge, the Sword Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be the spirit of a person who has passed away. It is known to wrap its ribbon-like appendage around the arm of anyone who tries to wield it, and then drain their life force.

# 680. Doublade
## Look
The evolved form of Honedge, Doublade is a Pokémon that is composed of two individual Honedge that are linked together.
## Lore
Doublade, the Sword Pokémon, is a creature that can attack its foes with a complex, coordinated, two-sword-style attack.

# 681. Aegislash
## Look
The final evolution, Aegislash has two forms: Shield and Blade. Its Shield Forme is a large, ornate shield with a sword sheathed inside. Its Blade Forme is a powerful, royal sword.
## Lore
Aegislash, the Royal Sword Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have been wielded by a king. It can use its psychic powers to manipulate people and Pokémon.

# 682. Spritzee
## Look
A small, bird-like, fairy Pokémon with a pink, fluffy body. It has a long, beak-like appendage that it uses to release its scent.
## Lore
Spritzee, the Perfume Pokémon, is a creature that can release a variety of different scents from its body. The scent it releases can change depending on its diet.

# 683. Aromatisse
## Look
The evolved form of Spritzee, Aromatisse is a bipedal, flamingo-like Pokémon with a large, fluffy, can-can-dancer-like dress.
## Lore
Aromatisse, the Fragrance Pokémon, is a creature that can create a variety of different scents, from a sweet, alluring aroma to a foul, unbearable stench.

# 684. Swirlix
## Look
A small, fairy-like Pokémon with a white, fluffy body that resembles a cotton candy. It has a long, prehensile tongue.
## Lore
Swirlix, the Cotton Candy Pokémon, is a creature that loves to eat sweets. It is said that its fur is as sweet as cotton candy.

# 685. Slurpuff
## Look
The evolved form of Swirlix, Slurpuff is a bipedal, dog-like Pokémon with a body that is a mix of a meringue and a cupcake.
## Lore
Slurpuff, the Meringue Pokémon, is a creature that has an incredible sense of smell. It can smell the faintest of scents from over 100 miles away.

# 686. Inkay
## Look
A small, squid-like Pokémon that is almost always seen floating upside down. It has a series of light-emitting organs on its head.
## Lore
Inkay, the Revolving Pokémon, is a creature that can drain the will to fight from its foes by flashing the lights on its head.

# 687. Malamar
## Look
The evolved form of Inkay, Malamar is a large and powerful, squid-like Pokémon. It has a dark, intimidating appearance and a pair of hypnotic, glowing eyes.
## Lore
Malamar, the Overturning Pokémon, is a creature that has the most powerful hypnotic abilities of all Pokémon. It is said that it can control the minds of others.

# 688. Binacle
## Look
A Pokémon that is composed of two individual, barnacle-like creatures that are attached to a single rock.
## Lore
Binacle, the Two-Handed Pokémon, is a creature that is two individuals in one body. The two individuals are always fighting with each other.

# 689. Barbaracle
## Look
The evolved form of Binacle, Barbaracle is a large and powerful, golem-like Pokémon. It is composed of a head and four limbs, each of which has its own brain.
## Lore
Barbaracle, the Collective Pokémon, is a creature that is a colony of seven individual Binacle. Each of its limbs can act independently.

# 690. Skrelp
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a seahorse that is camouflaged as a piece of kelp.
## Lore
Skrelp, the Mock Kelp Pokémon, is a creature that is a master of camouflage. It is almost impossible to spot in a kelp forest.

# 691. Dragalge
## Look
The evolved form of Skrelp, Dragalge is a large and powerful, draconic, leafy-sea-dragon-like Pokémon.
## Lore
Dragalge, the Mock Kelp Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredibly powerful poison. It can melt the hull of a ship with a single spray of its poison.

# 692. Clauncher
## Look
A crustacean Pokémon that resembles a pistol shrimp. It has a single, massive, cannon-like claw.
## Lore
Clauncher, the Water Gun Pokémon, is a creature that can shoot a powerful jet of water from its massive claw. The jet of water is so powerful that it can shatter rock.

# 693. Clawitzer
## Look
The evolved form of Clauncher, Clawitzer is a larger and more powerful, pistol-shrimp-like Pokémon. It has a massive, cannon-like claw that is almost as big as its body.
## Lore
Clawitzer, the Howitzer Pokémon, is a creature that can shoot a cannonball of water that is so powerful that it can sink a tanker ship.

# 694. Helioptile
## Look
A small, lizard-like Pokémon with a yellow and black body. It has two large, frill-like appendages on its head.
## Lore
Helioptile, the Generator Pokémon, is a creature that can generate its own electricity by basking in the sun.

# 695. Heliolisk
## Look
The evolved form of Helioptile, Heliolisk is a taller, more powerful, lizard-like Pokémon. It can spread its frill-like appendages to create a massive, solar-panel-like crest.
## Lore
Heliolisk, the Generator Pokémon, is a creature that can generate enough electricity to power a skyscraper.

# 696. Tyrunt
## Look
A prehistoric, dinosaur-like Pokémon that resembles a tyrannosaurus rex. It has a brown, rocky hide and a powerful jaw.
## Lore
Tyrunt, the Royal Heir Pokémon, is a creature that was resurrected from a fossil. It is known to be very selfish and will throw a tantrum if it doesn't get what it wants.

# 697. Tyrantrum
## Look
The evolved form of Tyrunt, Tyrantrum is a massive and powerful, tyrannosaurus-rex-like Pokémon. It has a massive, powerful jaw that can crush a car.
## Lore
Tyrantrum, the Despot Pokémon, is a creature that was the undisputed king of the ancient world. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 698. Amaura
## Look
A prehistoric, sauropod-like Pokémon with a blue body. It has two large, sail-like appendages on its neck.
## Lore
Amaura, the Tundra Pokémon, is a creature that was resurrected from a fossil. It is a very gentle and peaceful Pokémon that lived in cold, snowy regions.

# 699. Aurorus
## Look
The evolved form of Amaura, Aurorus is a massive and majestic, sauropod-like Pokémon. It has a massive, sail-like crest that glows with a beautiful, aurora-like light.
## Lore
Aurorus, the Tundra Pokémon, is a creature that can create a wall of ice to protect itself from attacks. It is a very gentle and beautiful Pokémon.

# 700. Sylveon
## Look
An evolution of Eevee, Sylveon is a quadrupedal, feline Pokémon with a pink and white coat. It has two large, ribbon-like appendages that it uses as feelers.
## Lore
Sylveon, the Intertwining Pokémon, is a creature that can use its ribbon-like feelers to calm the feelings of others. It is a very gentle and compassionate Pokémon.

# 701. Hawlucha
## Look
A bipedal, avian Pokémon that resembles a luchador. It has a colorful, vibrant plumage and a confident, determined expression.
## Lore
Hawlucha, the Wrestling Pokémon, is a creature that is a master of a unique form of aerial wrestling. It is a very powerful and skilled fighter.

# 702. Dedenne
## Look
A small, rodent-like Pokémon with an orange coat. It has two large, whisker-like antennae and two electrical cheek pouches.
## Lore
Dedenne, the Antenna Pokémon, is a creature that can use its whiskers to send and receive electrical signals. It is known to plug its tail into power outlets to steal electricity.

# 703. Carbink
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a small, gray rock with a series of blue, crystalline gems embedded in it. It has a fluffy, white, collar-like ruff.
## Lore
Carbink, the Jewel Pokémon, is a creature that is born deep underground. It is said that there is a massive, diamond-like Carbink that is the queen of the colony.

# 704. Goomy
## Look
A small, slug-like Pokémon with a purple, gelatinous body. It is known as the weakest of all Dragon-type Pokémon.
## Lore
Goomy, the Soft Tissue Pokémon, is a creature that has a body that is mostly made of water. It is very weak and is often targeted by predators.

# 705. Sliggoo
## Look
The evolved form of Goomy, Sliggoo is a larger, more powerful, snail-like Pokémon. It has two large, antenna-like horns.
## Lore
Sliggoo, the Soft Tissue Pokémon, is a creature that is blind. It uses its four horns to sense its surroundings.

# 706. Goodra
## Look
The final evolution, Goodra is a massive and powerful, bipedal, dragon-like Pokémon with a gooey, gelatinous body.
## Lore
Goodra, the Dragon Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength and its friendly, affectionate nature. It is a very powerful and reliable partner.

# 707. Klefki
## Look
A small, fairy-like Pokémon that resembles a keychain. It is known to collect keys.
## Lore
Klefki, the Key Ring Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its habit of collecting keys. It is a very mischievous Pokémon that loves to play pranks.

# 708. Phantump
## Look
A ghostly Pokémon that resembles a tree stump. It has a single, red eye and two small, branch-like arms.
## Lore
Phantump, the Stump Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be the spirit of a child who got lost in the forest. It is known to make a sound that sounds like a crying child.

# 709. Trevenant
## Look
The evolved form of Phantump, Trevenant is a massive and powerful, tree-like Pokémon. It has a single, large, red eye.
## Lore
Trevenant, the Elder Tree Pokémon, is a creature that is the guardian of the forest. It can control the trees in the forest and will attack anyone who harms it.

# 710. Pumpkaboo
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a pumpkin. It has four different sizes: Small, Average, Large, and Super Size.
## Lore
Pumpkaboo, the Pumpkin Pokémon, is a creature that is said to carry the spirits of the dead. It is most active on nights with a new moon.

# 711. Gourgeist
## Look
The evolved form of Pumpkaboo, Gourgeist is a larger and more powerful, pumpkin-like Pokémon. It also has four different sizes.
## Lore
Gourgeist, the Pumpkin Pokémon, is a creature that is said to sing in an eerie, unsettling voice on nights with a new moon.

# 712. Bergmite
## Look
A small, iceberg-like Pokémon. It has a single, large, yellow eye.
## Lore
Bergmite, the Ice Chunk Pokémon, is a creature that is known to live in groups on the backs of Avalugg.

# 713. Avalugg
## Look
The evolved form of Bergmite, Avalugg is a massive, aircraft-carrier-like Pokémon made of solid ice.
## Lore
Avalugg, the Iceberg Pokémon, is a creature that is so large that smaller Pokémon will often make their nests on its back.

# 714. Noibat
## Look
A small, bat-like Pokémon with two massive, speaker-like ears.
## Lore
Noibat, the Sound Wave Pokémon, is a creature that can produce ultrasonic waves that are powerful enough to shatter rock.

# 715. Noivern
## Look
The evolved form of Noibat, Noivern is a massive and powerful, wyvern-like Pokémon. It has two massive, speaker-like ears.
## Lore
Noivern, the Sound Wave Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible speed and its powerful, sound-based attacks.

# 716. Xerneas
## Look
A legendary, deer-like Pokémon with two massive, rainbow-colored antlers. It is the master of life.
## Lore
Xerneas, the Life Pokémon, is a legendary creature that has the power to grant eternal life. It is said that when it is at the end of its life, it will release all of its life energy and then transform into a tree.

# 717. Yveltal
## Look
A massive, avian, legendary Pokémon with a "Y" shape. It has a dark, malevolent appearance. It is the master of destruction.
## Lore
Yveltal, the Destruction Pokémon, is a legendary creature that can absorb the life force of other living things. It is said that when it is at the end of its life, it will absorb the life force of everything around it and then transform into a cocoon.

# 718. Zygarde
## Look
A legendary Pokémon with multiple forms. Its 50% Forme is a serpentine, draconic creature with a green and black body. Its other forms are composed of Zygarde Cells and Cores.
## Lore
Zygarde, the Order Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to be the guardian of the ecosystem. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 719. Diancie
## Look
A mythical, fairy-like Pokémon with a body that is a mix of a rock and a crystal. It has a large, pink, diamond-like crystal on its head.
## Lore
Diancie, the Jewel Pokémon, is a mythical creature that can create diamonds out of thin air by compressing the carbon in the atmosphere.

# 720. Hoopa
## Look
A mythical, genie-like Pokémon with two forms: Confined and Unbound. Its Confined Forme is a small, mischievous creature with a ring-like appendage. Its Unbound Forme is a massive and powerful, six-armed creature.
## Lore
Hoopa, the Mischief Pokémon, is a mythical creature that can use its rings to warp space and teleport objects. It is a very powerful and mischievous Pokémon.

# 721. Volcanion
## Look
A mythical, quadrupedal, turtle-like Pokémon with a red and orange body. It has a large, ring-like appendage on its back that can fire powerful blasts of steam.
## Lore
Volcanion, the Steam Pokémon, is a mythical creature that can create massive explosions by converting water into steam. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 722. Rowlet
## Look
A small, avian Pokémon resembling a round owlet. Its body is covered in beige and white feathers, with a distinctive green, two-leafed bowtie-like structure on its chest. Its face is a white, heart-shaped disc with two large, black eyes and a stubby beak. It can rotate its head almost 180 degrees.
## Lore
Rowlet, the Grass Quill Pokémon, is a creature of the night, storing energy during the day through photosynthesis with its leaves. It is a silent flyer, able to approach opponents without making a sound. It can launch the sharp-quilled leaves from its bowtie with surprising speed and accuracy. It is known to be very cautious, but can become flustered easily.

# 723. Dartrix
## Look
The evolved form of Rowlet, Dartrix is a larger, more stylish owl Pokémon. It has a sleek, white and brown plumage and a long, green fringe of leaves that it meticulously preens. It has a somewhat narcissistic personality, often concerned with its appearance. Two sharp leaves are tucked under its wings.
## Lore
Dartrix, the Blade Quill Pokémon, is highly sensitive to dirt and messiness, and will spend a great deal of time grooming its leafy fringe. It is a skilled thrower, able to launch the blade-like feathers hidden under its wings with deadly precision. It has a sharp intuition, allowing it to detect opponents even when it cannot see them.

# 724. Decidueye
## Look
The final evolution, Decidueye is a tall, humanoid, owl-like Pokémon resembling an archer. It has a green, hooded cloak made of leaves, with a drawstring of vines. Its face is dark with piercing, orange-red eyes. Its wings have become powerful arms that it uses to draw its vine-like bowstring.
## Lore
Decidueye, the Arrow Quill Pokémon, is a master of archery. It can pluck one of the arrow-like quills from its wings and fire it from its vine bowstring in a tenth of a second. It is a silent and swift hunter that can hit a target from over half a mile away. It can move through the forest without a trace, earning it the nickname "the ghost of the forest."

# 725. Litten
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, feline Pokémon. It has black fur with red stripes on its legs and a red, symbol-like marking on its forehead. Its eyes are large and yellow with red pupils. It has a calm and collected, yet somewhat aloof, expression.
## Lore
Litten, the Fire Cat Pokémon, is a logical and solitary creature that does not show its emotions easily. Its fur is rich in flammable oils, and it grooms itself by licking its fur and then setting the collected fur ablaze in fiery hairballs. When it is time to shed its old fur, it burns it all away in a glorious blaze.

# 726. Torracat
## Look
The evolved form of Litten, Torracat is a larger, more muscular feline Pokémon. A small, bell-like, flame-producing organ hangs from its neck. It has powerful forelegs and a confident, feisty attitude.
## Lore
Torracat, the Fire Cat Pokémon, possesses a flame sac that produces a constant flame. The bell-like organ on its neck rings when it spouts fire. It has a great passion for battle and will attack relentlessly. Its powerful front legs can bend an iron bar with a single blow. It has a wild and sometimes selfish nature.

# 727. Incineroar
## Look
The final evolution, Incineroar is a massive, bipedal, humanoid feline that resembles a professional wrestler. It has a muscular build, with red and black fur. A belt of flames erupts from its waist. It has a fierce and intimidating presence.
## Lore
Incineroar, the Heel Pokémon, is a dual-type Fire/Dark Pokémon. As its fighting spirit rises, the flames around its waist burn hotter and more intensely. It is a violent and aggressive fighter that loves to put on a show for the crowd. However, it can lose the will to fight if its audience is not impressed. It can be surprisingly caring towards young Pokémon and children.

# 728. Popplio
## Look
A small, pinniped Pokémon that resembles a sea lion. It has a blue body, a white snout, and a large, pink, clown-like nose. It has a frilly, light blue ruff around its neck. It is a playful and energetic creature.
## Lore
Popplio, the Sea Lion Pokémon, can create water balloons from its nose. It is a very agile Pokémon and can perform a variety of acrobatic feats with these balloons. It is a very dedicated Pokémon that works hard to perfect its balloon-making skills. It is known to get carried away in battle, putting on a show even when it is at a disadvantage.

# 729. Brionne
## Look
The evolved form of Popplio, Brionne is a bipedal, sea-lion-like Pokémon with a more elegant, dancer-like appearance. It wears a frilly, blue, tutu-like garment. It is almost always seen in a cheerful, dance-like pose.
## Lore
Brionne, the Pop Star Pokémon, is a creature that learns its dances by imitating the other members of its colony. It is a very cheerful and optimistic Pokémon, and its dancing can fill those who watch it with joy. It can create a series of exploding water balloons to attack its foes.

# 730. Primarina
## Look
The final evolution, Primarina is a majestic and beautiful, mermaid-like Pokémon. It has a long, flowing, fish-like tail and a crest of pink, pearl-adorned "hair." It moves with a graceful, singer-like elegance.
## Lore
Primarina, the Soloist Pokémon, is a creature that uses its beautiful singing voice to control water balloons. It is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon. The melody it sings has been passed down for generations. It is known as the "diva of the sea."

# 731. Pikipek
## Look
A small, avian Pokémon that resembles a woodpecker. It has a black and white plumage, a sharp, pointed beak, and a red crest on its head.
## Lore
Pikipek, the Woodpecker Pokémon, can peck at a rate of 16 times a second. It can use its powerful beak to shatter rock. It is known to drill holes in trees to store food.

# 732. Trumbeak
## Look
The evolved form of Pikipek, Trumbeak is a larger, toucan-like Pokémon. It has a massive, multi-colored beak.
## Lore
Trumbeak, the Bugle Beak Pokémon, can produce a variety of different sounds by using its beak like a bugle. It can use these sounds to communicate with its allies and to intimidate its foes.

# 733. Toucannon
## Look
The final evolution, Toucannon is a massive and powerful, toucan-like Pokémon. Its beak can be heated to temperatures of over 212 degrees Fahrenheit.
## Lore
Toucannon, the Cannon Pokémon, is a creature that can launch seeds from its beak with enough force to shatter rock. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon.

# 734. Yungoos
## Look
A small, mongoose-like Pokémon with a brown and yellow coat. It has a row of sharp, prominent teeth.
## Lore
Yungoos, the Loitering Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its insatiable appetite. It is always on the hunt for food.

# 735. Gumshoos
## Look
The evolved form of Yungoos, Gumshoos is a larger and more powerful, mongoose-like Pokémon with a detective-like appearance.
## Lore
Gumshoos, the Stakeout Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible patience. It will wait for its prey for days on end. It is a very cunning and skilled hunter.

# 736. Grubbin
## Look
A small, larval Pokémon with a white, grub-like body. It has a large, powerful, pincer-like jaw.
## Lore
Grubbin, the Larva Pokémon, is a creature that lives underground. It uses its powerful jaw to dig through the soil. It is very attracted to electricity.

# 737. Charjabug
## Look
The evolved form of Grubbin, Charjabug is a Pokémon that resembles a bus-shaped, green battery.
## Lore
Charjabug, the Battery Pokémon, is a creature that can store a massive amount of electricity in its body. It is often seen with a Grubbin on its back, providing it with electricity.

# 738. Vikavolt
## Look
The final evolution, Vikavolt is a massive and powerful, stag-beetle-like Pokémon. It has a pair of massive, pincer-like mandibles that can generate a powerful electrical charge.
## Lore
Vikavolt, the Stag Beetle Pokémon, is a creature that can fly through the forest at incredible speeds. It can use its massive pincers to fire a powerful beam of electricity.

# 739. Crabrawler
## Look
A crustacean Pokémon that resembles a boxer crab. It has a purple and blue body and two massive, powerful pincers that it holds up like boxing gloves.
## Lore
Crabrawler, the Boxing Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its competitive nature. It will try to climb to the highest point it can find to assert its dominance.

# 740. Crabominable
## Look
The evolved form of Crabrawler, Crabominable is a massive and powerful, yeti-crab-like Pokémon. Its pincers have become massive, icy hammers.
## Lore
Crabominable, the Woolly Crab Pokémon, is a creature that can be found in snowy mountains. It is a very powerful and destructive Pokémon that can create avalanches with its powerful punches.

# 741. Oricorio
## Look
An avian Pokémon with four different forms, depending on the nectar it has consumed: Baile Style (red, flamenco), Pom-Pom Style (yellow, cheerleader), Pa'u Style (pink, hula), and Sensu Style (purple, traditional Japanese dance).
## Lore
Oricorio, the Dancing Pokémon, is a creature that can change its form and type by sipping the nectar of certain flowers. Each form has its own unique dance style.

# 742. Cutiefly
## Look
A very small, insectoid Pokémon that resembles a bee fly. It has a yellow and black body and two large, translucent wings.
## Lore
Cutiefly, the Bee Fly Pokémon, is a creature that can sense the auras of living things. It can use this power to predict the movements of its opponents.

# 743. Ribombee
## Look
The evolved form of Cutiefly, Ribombee is a small, fairy-like Pokémon with a scarf-like appendage made of pollen.
## Lore
Ribombee, the Bee Fly Pokémon, is a creature that can create puffs of pollen that have a variety of different effects, from paralysis to relaxation.

# 744. Rockruff
## Look
A small, canine Pokémon that resembles a rock-type puppy. It has a brown coat and a fluffy, rock-like ruff around its neck.
## Lore
Rockruff, the Puppy Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its courage and its loyalty. It is a very popular pet.

# 745. Lycanroc
## Look
The evolved form of Rockruff, Lycanroc has three different forms: Midday, Midnight, and Dusk. Midday Form is a sleek, quadrupedal wolf. Midnight Form is a bipedal, werewolf-like creature. Dusk Form is a mix of the two.
## Lore
Lycanroc, the Wolf Pokémon, is a creature whose form is determined by the time of day it evolves. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 746. Wishiwashi
## Look
A small, weak, fish-like Pokémon. It has a perpetually sad expression. It has two forms: Solo and School.
## Lore
Wishiwashi, the Small Fry Pokémon, is a creature that is known as the "demon of the sea." When it is in danger, it will release a distress signal that will call all of its allies to form a massive, powerful, whale-like Pokémon.

# 747. Mareanie
## Look
A small, urchin-like Pokémon with a blue and purple body. It has a series of poisonous spines on its head. It is the rival of Corsola.
## Lore
Mareanie, the Brutal Star Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its cruel nature. It is known to hunt Corsola and will leave behind a trail of their broken branches.

# 748. Toxapex
## Look
The evolved form of Mareanie, Toxapex is a larger and more powerful, urchin-like Pokémon. It has a massive, dome-like shell that it can use to protect itself from attacks.
## Lore
Toxapex, the Brutal Star Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible defensive power. It is said that its poison is so potent that it can cause a pain that will last for three days and three nights.

# 749. Mudbray
## Look
A small, donkey-like Pokémon with a brown and black coat. It is almost always covered in mud.
## Lore
Mudbray, the Donkey Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength. It can carry a load that is over 50 times its own weight.

# 750. Mudsdale
## Look
The evolved form of Mudbray, Mudsdale is a massive and powerful, draft-horse-like Pokémon. Its legs are covered in a thick layer of protective mud.
## Lore
Mudsdale, the Draft Horse Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible stamina. It can run for three days and three nights without stopping.

# 751. Dewpider
## Look
A small, spider-like Pokémon that is always seen with a bubble of water over its head.
## Lore
Dewpider, the Water Bubble Pokémon, is a creature that can breathe both on land and in the water. It uses the bubble on its head to store air.

# 752. Araquanid
## Look
The evolved form of Dewpider, Araquanid is a larger and more powerful, water-spider-like Pokémon. It has a massive, bubble-like abdomen.
## Lore
Araquanid, the Water Bubble Pokémon, is a creature that can trap its prey in a bubble of water and then drown them.

# 753. Fomantis
## Look
A small, plant-like Pokémon that resembles a praying mantis. It has a pink and white body and two large, scythe-like leaves for arms.
## Lore
Fomantis, the Sickle Grass Pokémon, is a nocturnal creature that is most active at night. It is known to store up energy during the day through photosynthesis.

# 754. Lurantis
## Look
The evolved form of Fomantis, Lurantis is a graceful and elegant, humanoid, plant-like Pokémon. It has two large, scythe-like appendages that it uses to attack its foes.
## Lore
Lurantis, the Bloom Sickle Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its beautiful, vibrant colors. It is said that the most beautiful Lurantis is the one that is the most well-cared for.

# 755. Morelull
## Look
A small, mushroom-like Pokémon that is always seen in a group of three. It has a small, white, spore-like body and a large, mushroom-like cap.
## Lore
Morelull, the Illuminating Pokémon, is a nocturnal creature that can release a cloud of spores that will glow in the dark.

# 756. Shiinotic
## Look
The evolved form of Morelull, Shiinotic is a taller, more slender, mushroom-like Pokémon. It has a large, umbrella-like cap and two long, root-like legs.
## Lore
Shiinotic, the Illuminating Pokémon, is a creature that can lure in its prey with its strange, hypnotic lights.

# 757. Salandit
## Look
A small, reptilian Pokémon with a black and orange body. It has a mischievous and cunning expression. Only the females can evolve.
## Lore
Salandit, the Toxic Lizard Pokémon, is a creature that can release a poisonous gas that can cause dizziness and fainting. It is known to live in a reverse harem, with a single female being served by a group of males.

# 758. Salazzle
## Look
The evolved form of a female Salandit, Salazzle is a tall and powerful, bipedal, reptilian Pokémon. It has a dark, intimidating appearance and a confident, regal posture.
## Lore
Salazzle, the Toxic Lizard Pokémon, is a creature that is the queen of a large harem of male Salandit. She is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon that can create a powerful, corrosive poison.

# 759. Stufful
## Look
A small, bear-cub-like Pokémon with a pink and white coat. It has a small, tag-like appendage on its rear. Despite its cute appearance, it is incredibly strong.
## Lore
Stufful, the Flailing Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength. It can easily snap a tree in half with a single flail of its arms. It dislikes being touched.

# 760. Bewear
## Look
The evolved form of Stufful, Bewear is a massive and powerful, bear-like Pokémon. It has a pink and black coat and a friendly, yet somewhat unsettling, smile.
## Lore
Bewear, the Strong Arm Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its friendly nature and its incredible strength. It is known to give powerful, bone-crushing hugs to show its affection.

# 761. Bounsweet
## Look
A small, fruit-like Pokémon that resembles a mangosteen. It has a cheerful and sweet appearance.
## Lore
Bounsweet, the Fruit Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its sweet, alluring aroma. It is often targeted by bird Pokémon.

# 762. Steenee
## Look
The evolved form of Bounsweet, Steenee is a bipedal, plant-like Pokémon with a calyx-like headdress.
## Lore
Steenee, the Fruit Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its powerful kicks. It is a very cheerful and tomboyish Pokémon.

# 763. Tsareena
## Look
The final evolution, Tsareena is a tall and elegant, humanoid, plant-like Pokémon with a regal and powerful presence.
## Lore
Tsareena, the Fruit Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its powerful and graceful kicks. It is a very proud and noble Pokémon that will only accept a Trainer that it deems worthy.

# 764. Comfey
## Look
A small, fairy-like Pokémon that is always seen with a lei of flowers.
## Lore
Comfey, the Posy Picker Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its healing abilities. It can create a special oil from its flowers that can be used to heal wounds.

# 765. Oranguru
## Look
A large, orangutan-like Pokémon with a white and purple coat. It is always seen with a large, fan-like leaf.
## Lore
Oranguru, the Sage Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible intelligence. It is said that it is so smart that it can even use human tools.

# 766. Passimian
## Look
A bipedal, lemur-like Pokémon with a black and white coat. It is always seen with a large, berry-like ball.
## Lore
Passimian, the Teamwork Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible teamwork. They live in large troops and will work together to protect their territory.

# 767. Wimpod
## Look
A small, crustacean Pokémon that resembles a trilobite. It is known for its cowardly nature.
## Lore
Wimpod, the Turn Tail Pokémon, is a creature that is very cowardly and will run away at the first sign of danger. It is known to eat anything it can find.

# 768. Golisopod
## Look
The evolved form of Wimpod, Golisopod is a massive and powerful, bipedal, arthropod-like Pokémon with a suit of thick, samurai-like armor.
## Lore
Golisopod, the Hard Scale Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength and its sharp, durable claws. It is a very powerful and formidable Pokémon.

# 769. Sandygast
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a sand castle that has come to life. It has a shovel-like appendage on its head.
## Lore
Sandygast, the Sand Heap Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be the ghost of a person who has passed away on a beach. It can control sand and will suck the life force out of anyone who touches its shovel.

# 770. Palossand
## Look
The evolved form of Sandygast, Palossand is a massive and powerful, sand-castle-like Pokémon.
## Lore
Palossand, the Sand Castle Pokémon, is a creature that can create a massive sandstorm to trap its prey. It is known as the "nightmare of the beach."

# 771. Pyukumuku
## Look
A small, sea-cucumber-like Pokémon. It has a black, amorphous body and two large, pink, spike-like appendages.
## Lore
Pyukumuku, the Sea Cucumber Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its strange habit of staying in one spot for its entire life. It can also shoot its internal organs out of its mouth to attack its foes.

# 772. Type: Null
## Look
A synthetic Pokémon created to be a weapon. It has a chimeric appearance, with parts from a variety of different Pokémon. It wears a large, heavy helmet to control its power.
## Lore
Type: Null, the Synthetic Pokémon, is a creature that was created by the Aether Foundation to be a weapon that could fight against the Ultra Beasts. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 773. Silvally
## Look
The evolved form of Type: Null, Silvally has broken free from its helmet. It has a sleek, powerful build and a confident, determined expression.
## Lore
Silvally, the Synthetic Pokémon, is a creature that can change its type by inserting different memory drives into the port on its head. It is a very powerful and adaptable Pokémon.

# 774. Minior
## Look
A Pokémon that resembles a meteor. It has two forms: Meteor and Core. Its Meteor Form is a brown, rocky shell. Its Core Form is a small, brightly colored, star-like creature.
## Lore
Minior, the Meteor Pokémon, is a creature that lives in the stratosphere. It is protected by a hard, rocky shell. When its shell is broken, it reveals its brightly colored core.

# 775. Komala
## Look
A koala-like Pokémon that is always seen sleeping. It is always holding onto a small, log-like pillow.
## Lore
Komala, the Drowsing Pokémon, is a creature that is born asleep and dies asleep. It is said that its saliva can be used to create a powerful medicine that can cure any illness.

# 776. Turtonator
## Look
A tortoise-like Pokémon with a massive, spiky, explosive shell.
## Lore
Turtonator, the Blast Turtle Pokémon, is a creature that has a shell that is covered in a layer of explosive material. It can detonate its shell to create a massive explosion.

# 777. Togedemaru
## Look
A small, spherical, rodent-like Pokémon that resembles a hedgehog. It has a series of sharp, metallic spines on its back.
## Lore
Togedemaru, the Roly-Poly Pokémon, is a creature that can curl up into a ball to protect itself from attacks. It can also discharge electricity from the spines on its back.

# 778. Mimikyu
## Look
A small, ghostly Pokémon that is always seen wearing a ragged, Pikachu-like disguise. Its true form is unknown.
## Lore
Mimikyu, the Disguise Pokémon, is a creature that is so lonely that it has created a disguise to look like Pikachu so that it can make friends. It is said that anyone who sees its true form will be struck down by a mysterious illness.

# 779. Bruxish
## Look
A fish Pokémon with a colorful, vibrant, psychedelic pattern. It has a large, powerful jaw and a protuberance on its head.
## Lore
Bruxish, the Gnash Teeth Pokémon, is a creature that can release a powerful psychic energy from the protuberance on its head. This energy can cause intense headaches.

# 780. Drampa
## Look
A draconic, serpentine Pokémon with a long, flowing, white mane. It has a gentle and kind expression.
## Lore
Drampa, the Placid Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its love of children. It is a very gentle and caring Pokémon that will do anything to protect its friends.

# 781. Dhelmise
## Look
A Pokémon that is composed of a haunted anchor and a piece of seaweed. It has a single, large, red eye.
## Lore
Dhelmise, the Sea Creeper Pokémon, is a creature that is said to be the spirit of a sailor who has passed away. It is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon that can sink a ship with a single swing of its anchor.

# 782. Jangmo-o
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, draconic Pokémon with a body that is covered in a series of heart-shaped scales. It has a single, large, scale-like crest on its head.
## Lore
Jangmo-o, the Scaly Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its pride and its fighting spirit. It will never turn its back on a foe.

# 783. Hakamo-o
## Look
The evolved form of Jangmo-o, Hakamo-o is a bipedal, draconic Pokémon with a body that is covered in a suit of metallic scales.
## Lore
Hakamo-o, the Scaly Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its unique, dance-like fighting style. It can create a powerful shockwave by clanging its scales together.

# 784. Kommo-o
## Look
The final evolution, Kommo-o is a massive and powerful, bipedal, draconic Pokémon with a body that is covered in a suit of thick, golden, metallic scales.
## Lore
Kommo-o, the Scaly Pokémon, is a creature that is known as the "champion of the mountains." It is a very powerful and honorable Pokémon.

# 785. Tapu Koko
## Look
A legendary Pokémon with a body that is encased in a large, rooster-like, tiki mask. It is one of the four guardian deities of Alola.
## Lore
Tapu Koko, the Land Spirit Pokémon, is the guardian of Melemele Island. It is a very curious and fickle Pokémon.

# 786. Tapu Lele
## Look
A legendary Pokémon with a body that is encased in a butterfly-like, tiki mask. It is one of the four guardian deities of Alola.
## Lore
Tapu Lele, the Land Spirit Pokémon, is the guardian of Akala Island. It is known to scatter glowing scales that can heal the sick and injured.

# 787. Tapu Bulu
## Look
A legendary Pokémon with a body that is encased in a bull-like, tiki mask. It is one of the four guardian deities of Alola.
## Lore
Tapu Bulu, the Land Spirit Pokémon, is the guardian of Ula'ula Island. It can command the vegetation to grow and will attack anyone who harms it.

# 788. Tapu Fini
## Look
A legendary Pokémon with a body that is encased in a marlin-like, tiki mask. It is one of the four guardian deities of Alola.
## Lore
Tapu Fini, the Land Spirit Pokémon, is the guardian of Poni Island. It can create a special water that can purify the body and soul.

# 789. Cosmog
## Look
A small, nebular, mythical Pokémon. It has a gaseous, purple and blue body with a starry pattern.
## Lore
Cosmog, the Nebula Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is said to have come from another world. It is a very rare and mysterious Pokémon.

# 790. Cosmoem
## Look
The evolved form of Cosmog, Cosmoem is a Pokémon that is encased in a hard, golden, cocoon-like shell.
## Lore
Cosmoem, the Protostar Pokémon, is a creature that is in a state of deep slumber. It is said that it is gathering a massive amount of energy for its impending evolution.

# 791. Solgaleo
## Look
A legendary Pokémon that is the final evolution of Cosmog. It is a massive, lion-like Pokémon with a white, metallic body and a sun-like mane. It is the emissary of the sun.
## Lore
Solgaleo, the Sunne Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to be able to open a portal to another world. It is a very powerful and majestic Pokémon.

# 792. Lunala
## Look
A legendary Pokémon that is the final evolution of Cosmog. It is a massive, bat-like Pokémon with a pair of large, crescent-moon-shaped wings. It is the emissary of the moon.
## Lore
Lunala, the Moone Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to be able to absorb all the light in the world. It is a very powerful and mysterious Pokémon.

# 793. Nihilego
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a jellyfish. It has a translucent, bell-shaped body and a series of long, tentacle-like appendages.
## Lore
Nihilego, the Parasite Pokémon, is a creature that is a parasite that can latch onto people and Pokémon and control their minds. It is a very dangerous and feared Pokémon.

# 794. Buzzwole
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a massive, muscular mosquito. It has a red and black body and a series of powerful, bulging muscles.
## Lore
Buzzwole, the Swollen Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible strength. It is said that it can smash a truck with a single punch.

# 795. Pheromosa
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a graceful and elegant, cockroach-like creature. It has a white, slender body and two long, antenna-like appendages.
## Lore
Pheromosa, the Lissome Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible speed. It is said that it can run at speeds of over 120 miles per hour.

# 796. Xurkitree
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a bundle of electrical wires. It has a black, tree-like body and a series of colorful, wire-like appendages.
## Lore
Xurkitree, the Glowing Pokémon, is a creature that can generate a massive amount of electricity. It is said that it can drain the electricity from an entire city.

# 797. Celesteela
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a massive, bamboo-like rocket. It has a green, metallic body and two large, arm-like appendages.
## Lore
Celesteela, the Launch Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible defensive power. It is said that its body is as hard as steel.

# 798. Kartana
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a small, origami-like samurai. It has a body that is made of a sharp, paper-like material.
## Lore
Kartana, the Drawn Sword Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible cutting power. It is said that it can cut through a steel tower with a single swipe.

# 799. Guzzlord
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a massive, black, gluttonous monster. It has a massive, gaping mouth with two large, pincer-like appendages.
## Lore
Guzzlord, the Junkivore Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its insatiable appetite. It is said that it can eat an entire mountain in a single sitting.

# 800. Necrozma
## Look
A legendary Pokémon with a body that is made of a black, crystalline material. It has a prismatic, rainbow-like core. It can absorb the light of Solgaleo or Lunala.
## Lore
Necrozma, the Prism Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to have come from another world. It is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon that is known to be very aggressive.

# 801. Magearna
## Look
A mythical, artificial Pokémon that was created 500 years ago. It has a metallic, doll-like body and a Poké-Ball-like core in its chest.
## Lore
Magearna, the Artificial Pokémon, is a mythical creature that can understand human speech. It has a special ability called Soul-Heart that allows it to feel the emotions of others.

# 802. Marshadow
## Look
A mythical, humanoid Pokémon with a gray, shadow-like body. It has a helmet-like crest on its head and two glowing, green eyes.
## Lore
Marshadow, the Gloomdweller Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is said to be able to hide in the shadows of others. It is a very shy and reclusive Pokémon.

# 803. Poipole
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a small, purple, reptilian creature. It has a large, needle-like head and a mischievous expression.
## Lore
Poipole, the Poison Pin Pokémon, is a creature that can shoot a poisonous liquid from the needle on its head. It is known to be very friendly and will often laugh when it is petted.

# 804. Naganadel
## Look
The evolved form of Poipole, Naganadel is a massive and powerful, draconic, wasp-like Pokémon. It has a massive, abdomen-like stinger that is filled with a thousand needles.
## Lore
Naganadel, the Poison Pin Pokémon, is a creature that can shoot a poisonous liquid that is so potent that it can dissolve metal. It is a very powerful and dangerous Pokémon.

# 805. Stakataka
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a massive, walking tower. It has a body that is composed of a hundred individual, stone-like lifeforms.
## Lore
Stakataka, the Rampart Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible defensive power. It is said that its body is as hard as a diamond.

# 806. Blacephalon
## Look
An Ultra Beast that resembles a strange, clown-like creature. It has a disco-ball-like head that it can remove and detonate.
## Lore
Blacephalon, the Fireworks Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its strange, explosive attacks. It is a very unpredictable and dangerous Pokémon.

# 807. Zeraora
## Look
A mythical, bipedal, feline Pokémon with a yellow and black coat. It has two long, whisker-like appendages and a powerful, lightning-bolt-shaped tail.
## Lore
Zeraora, the Thunderclap Pokémon, is a mythical creature that can create a powerful magnetic field by emitting a massive electrical current from its paws. It is a very powerful and fast Pokémon.

# 808. Meltan
## Look
A mythical Pokémon with a body that is made of liquid metal. It has a single, large, hexagonal nut for a head.
## Lore
Meltan, the Hex Nut Pokémon, is a mythical creature that is known to absorb metal into its body. It is a very curious and playful Pokémon.

# 809. Melmetal
## Look
The evolved form of Meltan, Melmetal is a massive and powerful, golem-like Pokémon. It is composed of a hundred individual Meltan that have fused together.

Melmetal, the Hex Nut Pokémon, is a creature that is said to have been worshiped in ancient times. It is a very powerful and resilient Pokémon.

## Look

Grookey, the Chimp Pokémon, is a creature that can create a special energy by drumming with its stick. This energy can be used to revitalize plants.

## Look

## Lore
Thwackey, the Beat Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its incredible sense of rhythm. It can create a variety of different beats by drumming with its two sticks.

Rillaboom, the Drummer Pokémon, is a creature that can control the roots of its drum by drumming. It is a very powerful and honorable Pokémon.

Raboot, the Rabbit Pokémon, is a creature that is known for its powerful, fiery kicks. It is a very skilled and agile fighter.

A legendary, horse-like Pokémon with a body that is made of pure spectral energy.
## Lore
Spectrier, the Swift Horse Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is known for its incredible speed and its powerful, ghostly attacks.

# 898. Calyrex
## Look
A legendary, bipedal, rabbit-like Pokémon with a large, bud-like crown. It has a regal and powerful presence. It can ride on Glastrier or Spectrier.
## Lore
Calyrex, the King Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to have ruled over the Galar region in ancient times. It is a very powerful and wise Pokémon. 

# 899. Wyrdeer
## Look
An evolution of Stantler native to the ancient Hisui region. It is a large, majestic deer-like Pokémon with a thick, shaggy white and gray coat, a prominent beard, and a bushy tail. Its most striking features are its large, dark antlers which have black orbs at the base that can generate psychic energy.
## Lore
Wyrdeer, the Big Horn Pokémon, is revered by the people of Hisui for its deep connection to the land. The black orbs at the base of its antlers can generate and unleash powerful psychic energy, capable of distorting space. It uses its thick, warm fur to create clothing, and it is a trusted mount for traversing the harsh, cold wilderness.

# 900. Kleavor
## Look
An evolution of Scyther native to the ancient Hisui region. It is a large, bipedal, insectoid Pokémon with a body partially made of rock. Its most prominent features are its two massive, axe-like arms, which are made of a special, hardened stone. It has a rugged and aggressive appearance.
## Lore
Kleavor, the Axe Pokémon, is a formidable and aggressive creature. The stone axes on its arms are harder than steel and can fell even the sturdiest of trees with a single chop. It is incredibly territorial and will attack any intruder without hesitation. The stone it is made of is a rare mineral found only in the Hisui region.

# 901. Ursaluna
## Look
An evolution of Ursaring native to the ancient Hisui region. It is a massive, quadrupedal bear Pokémon with a stocky, powerful build. It has a large, moon-like marking on its forehead and is often covered in mud. Its fur is shaggy and brown.
## Lore
Ursaluna, the Peat Pokémon, is a creature that is most active on nights with a full moon. It has the unique ability to find anything that is buried deep underground. It is said to have gained its sturdy body and its ability to manipulate peat from the unique soil of the Hisui region's Crimson Mirelands.

# 902. Basculegion
## Look
An evolution of Basculin native to the Hisui region, with two forms based on its gender. It is a large, powerful fish Pokémon with a spectral aura. Its body is surrounded by the souls of its fallen comrades. The male has red accents, while the female has blue accents.
## Lore
Basculegion, the Big Fish Pokémon, evolves from a Basculin that has been possessed by the souls of other Basculin from its school that could not withstand the harsh journey upstream. It fights alongside these souls, drawing power from their collective grudge. It is a tireless swimmer and can swim for days on end without needing to rest.

# 903. Sneasler
## Look
An evolution of the Hisuian Sneasel. It is a tall, slender, bipedal weasel-like Pokémon with a dark gray and purple body. It has extremely long arms and massive, sickle-like claws that it uses for climbing. It has a feather-like crest on its head.
## Lore
Sneasler, the Free Climb Pokémon, is a master of climbing sheer cliffs and icy walls. It uses its powerful, curved claws to latch onto any surface. The tips of its claws secrete a potent poison that can paralyze its foes. It is a solitary Pokémon that lives in the harsh, cold mountains of Hisui.

# 904. Overqwil
## Look
An evolution of the Hisuian Qwilfish. It is a massive, intimidating, pufferfish-like Pokémon. Its body is dark and covered in massive, menacing, black spikes. It has a fierce and aggressive appearance.
## Lore
Overqwil, the Pin Cluster Pokémon, is known for its extremely aggressive and violent nature. Its diet consists of poison, which it stores in the spikes on its body. It is a very feared Pokémon in the seas of Hisui, known for its indiscriminate and powerful attacks.

# 905. Enamorus
## Look
A legendary, genie-like Pokémon with two forms: Incarnate and Therian. Its Incarnate Forme is a feminine, humanoid creature riding on a cloud, with a snake-like tail. Its Therian Forme is a massive, flying, tortoise-like creature with a snake coiled around its shell.
## Lore
Enamorus, the Love-Hate Pokémon, is a legendary creature that is said to herald the end of winter and the arrival of spring. It is a very fickle Pokémon whose love can quickly turn to a wrathful fury, capable of delivering terrible storms to those who disrespect life.

# 906. Sprigatito
## Look
A small, quadrupedal, feline Pokémon with a green, leaf-patterned coat. It has a fluffy tail and large, expressive eyes. A leaf-shaped marking is present on its face. It has a very charming and playful appearance.
## Lore
Sprigatito, the Grass Cat Pokémon, is a creature that loves to be the center of attention. The sweet, therapeutic aroma it releases from its paws can mesmerize and captivate those around it. It is known to be a bit capricious and will sulk if its Trainer pays attention to another Pokémon.

# 907. Floragato
## Look
The evolved form of Sprigatito, Floragato is a bipedal feline Pokémon with a more confident and mischievous look. It wields a long vine that is tipped with a hard, flower bud, which it uses like a yo-yo or a flail.
## Lore
Floragato, the Grass Cat Pokémon, is a very dexterous and clever fighter. It can control its vine with incredible precision, using the hard bud at the end to strike its opponents from a distance. It is a very proud Pokémon and loves to show off its skills.

# 908. Meowscarada
## Look
The final evolution, Meowscarada is a tall, elegant, bipedal feline Pokémon with a magician or masquerade-like appearance. It has a dark green, cape-like cloak and a mask-like pattern on its face.
## Lore
Meowscarada, the Magician Pokémon, is a master of misdirection and surprise attacks. It can attach pollen-filled "flower bombs" to surfaces, which it can then detonate at will. It is a very proud and dramatic Pokémon, though it can be very sensitive and attached to its Trainer.

# 909. Fuecoco
## Look
A small, bipedal, reptilian Pokémon that resembles a chubby crocodile. It has a white body with red accents and a small tuft of fire-like hair on its head. It has a perpetually cheerful and dopey expression.
## Lore
Fuecoco, the Fire Croc Pokémon, is a very laid-back creature that loves to eat. The fire sac on its head is warmed by the heat it absorbs through its scales. When it gets excited, this sac releases a burst of flames. It is a very gluttonous Pokémon and will rush towards any food it sees.

# 910. Crocalor
## Look
The evolved form of Fuecoco, Crocalor is a larger, more robust crocodilian Pokémon. It has a sombrero-like nest of fire on its head, which is actually a flaming egg.
## Lore
Crocalor, the Fire Croc Pokémon, has a flame that is fueled by its life energy. The egg-like fireball on its head is very fragile, and Crocalor is very protective of it. It is said that its voice becomes more powerful and melodious as it prepares to evolve.

# 911. Skeledirge
## Look
The final evolution, Skeledirge is a massive, quadrupedal, cro
